tags: [tech-coding]

<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Coding</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

table {
	border-collapse: collapse;
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.collection-title {
	display: inline-block;
	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {
	margin-top: 1em;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	empty-cells: show;
}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table th {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {
	background: rgb(247, 246, 243);
	color: black;
}
.simple-table-header {
	font-weight: 500;
}

time {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

.icon {
	display: inline-flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: center;
	max-width: 1.2em;
	max-height: 1.2em;
	text-decoration: none;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
}

img.icon {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.callout img.notion-static-icon {
	width: 1em;
	height: 1em;
}

.callout p {
	margin: 0;
}

.callout h1,
.callout h2,
.callout h3 {
	margin: 0 0 0.6rem;
}

.user-icon {
	width: 1.5em;
	height: 1.5em;
	border-radius: 100%;
	margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;
	margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;
	margin-left: 0.07em;
}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

.sans { font-family: ui-sans-serif, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI Variable Display", "Segoe UI", Helvetica, "Apple Color Emoji", "Noto Sans Arabic", "Noto Sans Hebrew", Arial, sans-serif, "Segoe UI Emoji", "Segoe UI Symbol"; }

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;
	height: 16;
	background-size: 16px;
	margin-left: 2px;
	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

.checkbox-off {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20x%3D%220.75%22%20y%3D%220.75%22%20width%3D%2214.5%22%20height%3D%2214.5%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20stroke%3D%22%2336352F%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}
	
</style></head><body><article id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-8fc7-eaba55d32ff4" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Coding</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a87c-ccb930b11a10" class="">1) Formal dominance theorem from the axioms</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-8894-f9d486d3b20b" class="">Primitives</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-a1af-ec5e5227500b" class="">Let  = risk density,  = coordination cost,  = drift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9085-eee7ca5fcf78" class="">Define <strong>stress</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a1d2-fb9c9404e0ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t)=R(t)\,C(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8c2b-de13f5019529" class="">Define <strong>trust mass</strong> (minimal model) as inverse-stress:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-94f3-e9c32c5e59dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(t)=\frac{1}{S(t)}=\frac{1}{R(t)C(t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a902-eb494bf073df" class="">Define <strong>dominance region</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-91cc-fd56fae178db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D}=\{(R,C): R&gt;0,\ C&gt;0,\ S(t)&lt;S_c\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-af28-c69c038c66b8" class="">Theorem A (Sustained dominance as bounded-stress invariance)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-9281-f9fe4fb1f134" class="">Assume the system evolves in time and that there exists a critical threshold . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-9884-e5ff55eb3986" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t\in[0,\tau],\quad S(t)\le S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-a720-d998f7c25cc4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D(\tau)=\int_0^\tau \|\Delta\theta(t)\|\,dt \le D_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-84d7-c8013dc4bf43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(t)\ge \frac{1}{S_c}\quad \forall t\in[0,\tau]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-ab0f-d927a6a060eb" class=""><strong>Interpretation:</strong> bounded  and bounded drift imply dominance persistence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-9039-e74a3ec81035" class="">Theorem B (Dominance break via threshold crossing)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9c69-f19f89271ab5" class="">If there exists  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8093-e78703629a92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t^\*)&gt;S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9570-eecb193fbb5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(t^\*)&lt;\frac{1}{S_c}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-bb01-f8646409dee7" class="">Theorem C (Stability of the dominance basin under shocks)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a634-cc4559a37351" class="">Let shocks add bounded perturbations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bccf-e96a56fa56cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R&#x27;(t)=R(t)+\delta_R(t),\quad C&#x27;(t)=C(t)+\delta_C(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-9dbe-e5fc1203698e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sup_t\left|\delta_R(t)C(t)+\delta_C(t)R(t)+\delta_R(t)\delta_C(t)\right|\le \Delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-82e9-ff80a993d2cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t)+\Delta \le S_c\quad \forall t\in[0,\tau]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-88aa-db00ef1b60e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-94c8-ca57c38cd0ca" class="">2) Rigorous empirical model with testability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b02d-c010e62a1b5e" class="">Observable decomposition (turn primitives into measurable components)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-9eaf-d515d879da72" class="">Risk density:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-8769-e3433037d2c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R(t)=a_1 EL(t)+a_2 MPL(t)+a_3 G(t)+a_4 \text{CryptoDebt}(t)+a_5 \text{Contagion}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-809d-f3d08e0254f5" class="">Coordination cost:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b93e-d510e8df75d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C(t)=b_1 \mathcal{E}(t)+b_2 S_f(t)+b_3 R_{comp}(t)+b_4 \text{Integration}(t)+b_5 \text{Ambiguity}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-95ab-d99301b9c2f9" class="">Trust mass (measured proxy):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-b222-cb012deeb3a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat T(t)=c_1 \ln(1+L(t))+c_2 \ln(1+D(t))+c_3 \ln(1+C_{coll}(t))+c_4 \ln(1+I(t)) - c_5 P(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9cd8-ea7ed92ac44f" class="">Then test the structural claim:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-ad73-d7a93e375617" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat T(t)\approx k_0 - k_1\ln(R(t)C(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-b9f5-ff1978c4a265" class="">Measurable KPIs (examples)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-99d1-de2cd6ce04e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: observed loss rate, fraud loss, exploit loss (per period)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-86c8-d3dc93f08d8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stress VaR / CVaR of losses, worst-case incident bound under scenarios</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-a344-d4d0c2b90da8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: concentration indices, governance-change frequency, emergency-action rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-8588-d96651751ab8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy description length, number of moving parts, change frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-b674-d706ac3c25f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: time+cost to switch (integration hours, legal steps, retraining)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-bcf6-cb4a81821586" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: depth/volume/spread resilience under stress windows</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-aa9c-d754dcbee2c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: insurance capacity and pricing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-813f-e8e954b00d75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: enforcement intensity proxies, regulatory actions, deplatforming risk indices</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8950-df4522d19cfa" class="">Falsifiable predictions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-9a7e-e9a1339a446d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance growth condition</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-a55d-c7407197059a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt}&gt;0\ \Rightarrow\ \frac{d}{dt}(R\cdot C)&lt;0 \ \text{and/or}\ \frac{d\hat T}{dt}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-940e-e53792c93dea" class="">If stress regime , dominance persists iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8fa9-d83e3df5b965" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{Z=1} R(t)C(t) &lt; S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-884e-ce9ce4784c06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{inc}(t)C_{inc}(t) - R_{new}(t)C_{new}(t) &gt; \Theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-b1a1-f1b601543603" class="">Estimation plan</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b9c6-d23058cbbdf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fit by regression or Bayesian inference on historical periods.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-95de-c260c5d737fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Identify as the smallest stress level at which major adoption reversals or liquidity collapses occurred.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-bc2c-f6c02a2f22e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Out-of-sample test on later windows.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-b901-c91638b107e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8295-d07e06a47760" class="">3) Collapse known monetary systems into special cases</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-8b63-c090f960897a" class="">Define the general dominance stress:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8868-e0f3d34b831d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S=R\cdot C</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-b075-d033e7007b80" class="">Case 1: “Hard decentralization” (Bitcoin-like idealization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-aae9-f312985cba81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low governance drift: small (very rigid)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-ada7-edfe4e0066ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coordination cost higher for institutions: elevated (integration/compliance)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b08a-da7714c5e79a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk density depends on custody, UX, and ecosystem incidents: not minimal for many institutional workflows</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-a839-d9cba1d4dec6" class="">Specialization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-a022-ea1c94344460" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{BTC}=R_{custody/ops}\cdot C_{inst-friction}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8333-e8437d025ffe" class="">Case 2: “Centralized rails” (traditional payments)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b697-f8ad0acaa66c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coordination cost low for users (ubiquity): low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-bb47-e2bbc3b0ca6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Political risk and capture risk higher: increases via censorship/controls, plus tail risk from governance capture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-9c1c-d1314d017a68" class="">Specialization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-9851-ca5f9c1aa8ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{central}=R_{political/capture}\cdot C_{low}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-abf8-e0a571f69e77" class="">Case 3: “Hybrid constitutional” (constrained adaptability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-ac6e-f2617d35dc84" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coordination cost targeted low for institutional adoption via standards:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-b930-f289db4fc45d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk density minimized via auditability, insurance, bounded drift:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8ae0-fe7787729491" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Drift bounded by constitution:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-a5d2-c6ac980a3aff" class="">Specialization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-8b4b-eb000303f294" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{hybrid}=R_{bounded}\cdot C_{bounded},\quad \text{with } S_{hybrid}&lt;\min(S_{BTC},S_{central}) \text{ in target segments}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-abe7-d876fb2f230b" class="">Case 4: “Permissioned consortium”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-ab1b-f966e1339676" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">may be low inside the consortium, high outside</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-8e98-c80cb473ff2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">depends on concentration/capture; can be low if legal enforceability is strong, or high if cartel risk dominates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-87d5-dffde223ab19" class="">Specialization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-9b14-fa2fed0e6429" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{consortium}=R_{capture/legal}\cdot C_{boundary}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-aeff-dfbf54167abd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-96ff-d1d057373bad" class="">Unified closure</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-a74f-ecd3be6cc34e" class="">All systems become comparisons of <strong>stress trajectories</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-ad81-eae6cfd07ef7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_i(t)=R_i(t)\,C_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9c2a-d1f8e0ecdd61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_i(t)&lt;S_c\quad \forall t\in[0,\tau]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b508-e32752755fff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_i(\tau)\le D_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-9f71-c048f6460930" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_i(t)=\frac{1}{S_i(t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-9ac0-ed6b4f2c05c0" class="">If you want the next step, specify which target segment (retail, treasury, interbank, settlement layer), and the model can be instantiated with segment-specific  and  terms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-9193-fcf820b3fe3c" class="">XLVI. More: fully define <code>SafeBase(Σ0)</code> (export-eligible validated claims) + gating rules + exact decision procedure + theorems that it is finite, decidable, and invariant under rewrites</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8102-e1c059e9d704" class="">This finalizes the semantics bound theorem by removing the last remaining design freedom: what counts as <strong>validated</strong> and <strong>export-eligible</strong> at time 0.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9fd4-f2718db7b6fb" class="">Everything below is concrete, finite, and decidable in the same finite world/AST model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-ab58-e9721cf6d6d0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-96e4-c27cd03ce769" class="">1) Definitions: evidence, metrics, ownership, support types</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-91dc-eb5f3087e014" class="">Each claim  has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b1c6-ff37e084ddda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stype(c) ∈ {Emp, Inf, Def, MB, Prim, Lim}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-bf36-d97754764cbd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metrics(c) : List MetricId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-b5ed-d970ef703239" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence(c) : List EvidenceId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-9017-e3b76c45046e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>own(c) ∈ {Self, Public, Licensed(id), Unknown, Restricted(id)}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-a752-f38c284ff414" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>status(c) ∈ {Active, Blocked}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-9ccf-d9d7a262ee87" class="">Metric registry  maps <code>MetricId → MetricRecord</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-ac1e-d2853a3fb2b1" class="">A MetricRecord includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-8588-d1ad6791e676" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>unit : String</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-832e-d49f6daefa9d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>samplingHz : Nat</code> (must be &gt; 0)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-91eb-e1ac901b4e7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>validRange : (Int×Int)</code> (must satisfy lo ≤ hi)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-9642-fad01f0a2f1f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>transformsAllowed : Finset TransformId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-a2d1-e8c6aa73112d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>missingPolicy : {block, ignore}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-ad0b-f931e2f8f7c4" class="">Evidence store  maps <code>EvidenceId → EvidenceRecord</code> with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-b7a6-d7d99278f1cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>kind : {Measurement, Citation, Test, Log}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-8db4-e6d7b5cbd3c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>source : {Internal, Public}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-9d1d-d54c40491fa5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>license : Ownership</code> (Public/Licensed/Restricted)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8b0c-de4217503c9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp : Int</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-b614-d5653d55cedc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>hash : Hash</code> (treated as string; hashing correctness can be axiomized if needed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-9ca1-e4174ed40c57"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-b2e8-e6b8874424b9" class="">2) Export eligibility (ownership gate)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-8972-d64d84054cd8" class="">A claim is export-eligible iff ownership is not Unknown/Restricted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-b577-e177fb1e81d9" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-84c8-eb48ca939267" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ExportOK(c) \iff own(c)\in\{\mathrm{Public},\mathrm{Self},\mathrm{Licensed}(\cdot)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-8a70-d9aefbf19af7" class="">If <code>Self</code> is considered exportable only inside your organization, you can refine it, but as a formal base predicate it remains decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8d6f-faaa46fe37fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-93d6-e0d921e7440c" class="">3) Validation gates (strict, conservative)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9e7d-ca01c4146b88" class="">SafeBase must not include claims that require modeling leaps or unverified transforms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8c61-e7f9eaf21e53" class="">We define validation in two layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8c81-c9d70c466a84" class="">3.1 Metric adequacy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-8158-e28d30b8ca6c" class="">For metric :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ab78-c3f1b58b7e5d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MetricOK(m) \iff samplingHz(m)&gt;0 \wedge lo(m)\le hi(m) \wedge unit(m)\neq &quot;&quot;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9402-c8d3ab9e7508" class="">(you can add more constraints; keep it finite)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-8729-cbe7550d0468" class="">3.2 Claim metric gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-809d-c7060d8caf2b" class="">A claim that requires metrics is valid only if <strong>all referenced metrics</strong> exist and are OK:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-8fd5-c24732d0f109" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MetricsOK(c) \iff \forall id\in metrics(c),\ id\in Dom(M)\ \wedge MetricOK(M[id])</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-81eb-ead3006632d1" class="">If <code>metrics(c)</code> is empty, MetricsOK is true.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-af3f-d9523285d665" class="">3.3 Evidence gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-aeec-e1521d828d3b" class="">Define evidence admissibility:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-879c-f8d2bb352118" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EvidenceOK(e) \iff
kind(e)\in\{\mathrm{Measurement},\mathrm{Test},\mathrm{Log},\mathrm{Citation}\}
\wedge source(e)\in\{\mathrm{Internal},\mathrm{Public}\}
\wedge license(e)\neq Restricted(\cdot)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-ae15-de4debce5b1c" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9261-c95bd18b3745" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EvidenceOK(c) \iff \forall e\in evidence(c),\ e\in Dom(E)\wedge EvidenceOK(E[e])</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9858-f3105db74830" class="">If <code>evidence(c)</code> is empty, EvidenceOK is false for Emp claims and true for Def/Prim/Lim (see below).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-bf1f-e5d597727afd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-aa20-d9f47f10ed2e" class="">4) Support-type-specific SafeBase rules (MECE, deterministic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-88a7-e368c5f0be55" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-b048-e53a5facf736" class="">4.1 Empirical (Emp)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-b51f-e8c0f6234487" class="">Emp claims must have both:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-af77-f2a8e646b921" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MetricsOK</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8f91-c1e08476282e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">at least one admissible evidence record (non-empty evidence list)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-95a0-fabb10fa1902" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeEmp(c)\iff stype(c)=Emp \wedge MetricsOK(c)\wedge EvidenceOK(c)\wedge evidence(c)\neq []</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-825a-d9008ee19a63" class="">4.2 Inferential (Inf)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-afc0-c0f5d6a66f41" class="">Inferential claims are not included in SafeBase unless explicitly licensed as exportable inference (rare).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b796-c82affaa2049" class="">Conservative default: exclude all Inf.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-8ae5-e9d1cb66792b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeInf(c)\iff False</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-848f-e1a39e7c1427" class="">(If you later want a gated version: require that all dependencies are in SafeBase and the inference rule is whitelisted; that’s a separate formal layer.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-a06c-e507c0bd7895" class="">4.3 Definitional (Def)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b3a7-e047ee799642" class="">Definitions are exportable if ownership is exportable and the claim is not blocked.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-b197-dbcf13cd0184" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeDef(c)\iff stype(c)=Def</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-9b09-e94e0d3727e9" class="">(Optionally require explicit “definition source” evidence; not required for structural safety.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-958c-f518bf66a424" class="">4.4 Model-bounded (MB)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-b53b-f0ad4a5ae51c" class="">MB claims are excluded from SafeBase by design:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-8d66-e9fede90c4dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeMB(c)\iff False</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-9921-d03be883794d" class="">4.5 Primitive (Prim) and Limit (Lim)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-bde2-f783f3efefc8" class="">These are allowed only if explicitly marked as such, because they do not claim empirical truth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-af64-f30bef571a3e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafePrim(c)\iff stype(c)=Prim</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-a92e-f7be957b29c6" class="">SafeLim(c)\iff stype(c)=Lim<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-a601-de7ce9b4c102"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b3f2-debd60847037" class="">5) SafeBase definition (final, complete)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8c41-d26ad38969aa" class="">SafeBase is computed <strong>only from the initial state</strong> :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-90c1-c81ca36ebd67" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeBase(\Sigma_0)=\{c.id : c\in V_0,\ status(c)=Active,\ ExportOK(c),\ SafeTypeOK(c)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-b4ca-d6b61723e753" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9b13-faf01b4fe2af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeTypeOK(c)\iff SafeEmp(c)\ \lor\ SafeDef(c)\ \lor\ SafePrim(c)\ \lor\ SafeLim(c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-aef0-eeec452d837c" class="">This is finite and decidable because , ,  are finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-aeaf-fe180693230e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a207-f1328af8d650" class="">6) SafeActive at time t (invariant base, intersection with current Active)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-b2d1-f715eebbf0ac" class="">For any later state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-a706-e43fe9a4bca9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeActive(\Sigma_0,\Sigma_t)=\{c\in Active(\Sigma_t) : c.id \in SafeBase(\Sigma_0)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-9602-c7168ae35400" class="">This is the “fixed base” rule needed for the semantic bound theorem.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-b77f-d9b8b5f2359b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-918e-de3effa25b94" class="">7) Decision procedure (fully explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-98eb-cf144bf507c4" class="">Compute <code>SafeBase(Σ0)</code> in three passes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-99ec-d4fc26038dfc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Precompute:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-8e82-dd02d93790a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>goodMetric : MetricId → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-aa3b-c0c5a43cb983" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>goodEvidence : EvidenceId → Bool</code></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a9a7-ed0b658ef11d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>For each claim :<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b6ef-c99e840ebc8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if not Active → skip</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-b6ae-e1aac16aa3c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if not ExportOK → skip</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-b989-e10fc8a4c0d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">check support-type:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-b075-c57142cc26a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Emp: require <code>metrics</code> all present+good, and <code>evidence</code> non-empty and all present+good</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-87ed-e6bf972e054e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Def/Prim/Lim: accept</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a929-e3c0b7ee7d25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Inf/MB: reject</li></ul></div></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-a7e4-fcdc61d35974" class="">Return the set of accepted claim ids.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-875f-d50ea61e25c7" class="">Complexity: linear in total referenced metrics/evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-986d-ea89e8471d9e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8d80-d5a9840195ab" class="">8) Theorems needed for the end-to-end bound (now provable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-846c-daeec3bcdaf8" class="">8.1 SafeBase is decidable and finite</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-9437-e5bceff87ec3" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem SafeBase_finite : ∀ s0, Finite (SafeBase s0) := by sorry
theorem SafeBase_decidable : ∀ s0 cId, Decidable (cId ∈ SafeBase s0) := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-b8fe-c4e16b5e7f7b" class="">8.2 SafeBase is invariant under rewrites (by definition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-82d8-f1d97d4a29f8" class="">Since SafeBase depends only on :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b5e6-c366aeee8c5c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem SafeBase_invariant :
  ∀ s0 s, SafeBase s0 = SafeBase s0 := by intro s0 s; rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8818-d4ed2e68d560" class="">8.3 SafeActive shrinks or stays under step (fixed base)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-a9a2-df73fd7c430f" class="">Because step can block claims or add new claims, but SafeActive selects only ids from SafeBase(s0):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9578-d9a08c4421a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">New claims are not in SafeBase(s0).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-afc7-ce4399c74bf8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Blocking can remove claims from Active, thus removing them from SafeActive.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-a90b-d8e167969622" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b19c-e2e85550c17d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeActive(s0, step(s)) \subseteq SafeActive(s0, s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-8af6-cd5187f8e4e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket step(s)\rrbracket_{safe} \supseteq \llbracket s\rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-bb0a-c9c8d12a5f53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket s\rrbracket_{safe} \subseteq \llbracket s0\rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-b95a-c6f161494c87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-89b5-dd52a0032ef0" class="">9) End-to-end bound theorem (now fully pinned)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-bfe4-e4885bedeabf" class="">Let  be compiled, and  sealed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-a251-d56652780ec1" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-919d-e635247eb75a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_0 = \llbracket \Sigma_0 \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-b087-efc2004cad2a" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8c77-c8ad98d3f3da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma_N \rrbracket_{cons} \subseteq \mathcal{E}_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-aebb-d4903f57ab78" class="">Because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-aee2-e79887ce2410" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">any commitment in that is safe-export-eligible must be derived from claims in SafeBase()</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-978e-ffa6f5fbb755" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and Safe semantics evaluates only that fixed base subset</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-8694-e96e5ef1662e" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-be26-d3743815fe7f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma_N \rrbracket_{cons}\subseteq \llbracket \Sigma_0 \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a7ed-ddd43e64f371" class="">with SafeBase now explicitly defined.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-a67c-cf70af0ab5ef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-91e7-f3c50eb4ce11" class="">10) Only remaining “more” after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-86a8-d7aaa131bc48" class="">At this point, the only meaningful “more” is to expand SafeBase beyond the conservative default:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-9cd7-c55d928bbac6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Gated inferential export</strong>: allow certain Inf claims into SafeBase if their dependency closure is in SafeBase and their inference rule is whitelisted and budget-compliant.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-a655-c3bc78897388" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Quantitative metric semantics</strong>: interpret metrics as actual numeric traces in the world model (not just meta-data), enabling proofs that Emp claims are grounded in measurements.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-8c95-e16673a769ec" class="">Anything else would be repetition of structure already closed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-b218-d6ba2c417f40" class="">XLVI. More: fully define <code>SafeBase(Σ0)</code> (export-eligible validated claims) + gating rules + exact decision procedure + theorems that it is finite, decidable, and invariant under rewrites</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-af6d-d88af97036f8" class="">This finalizes the semantics bound theorem by removing the last remaining design freedom: what counts as <strong>validated</strong> and <strong>export-eligible</strong> at time 0.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-af5d-dc424f3cccd1" class="">Everything below is concrete, finite, and decidable in the same finite world/AST model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-a7e8-e480ed72811e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-bc7b-c385cc7b5784" class="">1) Definitions: evidence, metrics, ownership, support types</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-8cff-e1d91d682998" class="">Each claim  has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-a83f-f2da40ed891e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stype(c) ∈ {Emp, Inf, Def, MB, Prim, Lim}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9c40-d37c897804db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metrics(c) : List MetricId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-8921-faaf7f1c6de9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence(c) : List EvidenceId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-aa63-fdf07ad19f5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>own(c) ∈ {Self, Public, Licensed(id), Unknown, Restricted(id)}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-ba81-c64fa452686f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>status(c) ∈ {Active, Blocked}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a6a4-cb89ebb3e2d9" class="">Metric registry  maps <code>MetricId → MetricRecord</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-9ad1-d0a1148cbd77" class="">A MetricRecord includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-84df-f3a1a1a32b08" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>unit : String</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-926d-e3c35ed8fc0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>samplingHz : Nat</code> (must be &gt; 0)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-b19b-dc519b3fd358" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>validRange : (Int×Int)</code> (must satisfy lo ≤ hi)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a961-d8a252e00117" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>transformsAllowed : Finset TransformId</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a65e-c86da7d24447" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>missingPolicy : {block, ignore}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b078-d0d91f3194d2" class="">Evidence store  maps <code>EvidenceId → EvidenceRecord</code> with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-ab58-cf7ae982b0ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>kind : {Measurement, Citation, Test, Log}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-a57c-d8b67fe99816" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>source : {Internal, Public}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-9514-fc6d4a514968" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>license : Ownership</code> (Public/Licensed/Restricted)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-a0c3-ee53a043f672" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>timestamp : Int</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9684-e691b06cd285" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>hash : Hash</code> (treated as string; hashing correctness can be axiomized if needed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9f9a-d0e537fd3734"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9aed-ec66f6819cc5" class="">2) Export eligibility (ownership gate)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-ad1a-c549dd9880e7" class="">A claim is export-eligible iff ownership is not Unknown/Restricted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-9943-f27f7b5413c6" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-a65f-e081dfeabdcf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ExportOK(c) \iff own(c)\in\{\mathrm{Public},\mathrm{Self},\mathrm{Licensed}(\cdot)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-be62-ee56755d5904" class="">If <code>Self</code> is considered exportable only inside your organization, you can refine it, but as a formal base predicate it remains decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b9ed-df22e5063eaa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-99f8-dd1fd2763971" class="">3) Validation gates (strict, conservative)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9c2a-ddc02b58371c" class="">SafeBase must not include claims that require modeling leaps or unverified transforms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-b0b2-db927a85eaf2" class="">We define validation in two layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b7b4-fb8a1d4a6b5a" class="">3.1 Metric adequacy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-838b-dc8cd7e80aa6" class="">For metric :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-8fe0-e0864f06c15c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MetricOK(m) \iff samplingHz(m)&gt;0 \wedge lo(m)\le hi(m) \wedge unit(m)\neq &quot;&quot;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-bfe0-c183eb595cd9" class="">(you can add more constraints; keep it finite)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-b706-f0e067fc5df2" class="">3.2 Claim metric gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-b345-efa16f745e8f" class="">A claim that requires metrics is valid only if <strong>all referenced metrics</strong> exist and are OK:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-8022-d36827c7c53b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MetricsOK(c) \iff \forall id\in metrics(c),\ id\in Dom(M)\ \wedge MetricOK(M[id])</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-a2bc-ec492b02680e" class="">If <code>metrics(c)</code> is empty, MetricsOK is true.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-85a9-e0cd18ee2c5d" class="">3.3 Evidence gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-83a8-fe93501fd2ae" class="">Define evidence admissibility:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-8f66-c5ca28080cca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EvidenceOK(e) \iff
kind(e)\in\{\mathrm{Measurement},\mathrm{Test},\mathrm{Log},\mathrm{Citation}\}
\wedge source(e)\in\{\mathrm{Internal},\mathrm{Public}\}
\wedge license(e)\neq Restricted(\cdot)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8702-daf193ef379a" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-8c06-d86ae693eef4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EvidenceOK(c) \iff \forall e\in evidence(c),\ e\in Dom(E)\wedge EvidenceOK(E[e])</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-a273-df0e69cc798e" class="">If <code>evidence(c)</code> is empty, EvidenceOK is false for Emp claims and true for Def/Prim/Lim (see below).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-8c51-c5665e137c28"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-95e2-e47acbf334a0" class="">4) Support-type-specific SafeBase rules (MECE, deterministic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-90b7-ce06f312484c" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-ab76-dbf4fd2d1c23" class="">4.1 Empirical (Emp)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b863-d5f2280d6f38" class="">Emp claims must have both:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-aadb-e61194eb41e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MetricsOK</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-b60e-f1a0b2312dd3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">at least one admissible evidence record (non-empty evidence list)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-bc6f-d3c74cffcf24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeEmp(c)\iff stype(c)=Emp \wedge MetricsOK(c)\wedge EvidenceOK(c)\wedge evidence(c)\neq []</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9a5b-cbf7df0e0788" class="">4.2 Inferential (Inf)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-bce9-c0b9c75da88f" class="">Inferential claims are not included in SafeBase unless explicitly licensed as exportable inference (rare).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-9bab-e4389d0c86a7" class="">Conservative default: exclude all Inf.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-bbc2-c55e35a24206" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeInf(c)\iff False</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a353-f1a0b57cf2aa" class="">(If you later want a gated version: require that all dependencies are in SafeBase and the inference rule is whitelisted; that’s a separate formal layer.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-803a-d4e69cc33134" class="">4.3 Definitional (Def)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-91dd-d19367a81a09" class="">Definitions are exportable if ownership is exportable and the claim is not blocked.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-bd4f-c8dd6bf95ee7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeDef(c)\iff stype(c)=Def</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a2ce-fe73251d800c" class="">(Optionally require explicit “definition source” evidence; not required for structural safety.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a042-ed3d9fcba86d" class="">4.4 Model-bounded (MB)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a46a-e8da8cf1002f" class="">MB claims are excluded from SafeBase by design:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-975d-f578108b9d51" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeMB(c)\iff False</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-9f9a-d270f77ad029" class="">4.5 Primitive (Prim) and Limit (Lim)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-a252-dd27444e7281" class="">These are allowed only if explicitly marked as such, because they do not claim empirical truth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-9a7e-ecdfd0ee710f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafePrim(c)\iff stype(c)=Prim</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b1a8-ce97d1302161" class="">SafeLim(c)\iff stype(c)=Lim<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-a954-eb7550b00dc1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8610-e35de74be6b5" class="">5) SafeBase definition (final, complete)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b723-c20493bb7bab" class="">SafeBase is computed <strong>only from the initial state</strong> :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-885a-c54be96b8519" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeBase(\Sigma_0)=\{c.id : c\in V_0,\ status(c)=Active,\ ExportOK(c),\ SafeTypeOK(c)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-ae7f-da9e9ba7d329" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-8bbd-fb4d5f568bc0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeTypeOK(c)\iff SafeEmp(c)\ \lor\ SafeDef(c)\ \lor\ SafePrim(c)\ \lor\ SafeLim(c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-aead-d1d7fc1d049f" class="">This is finite and decidable because , ,  are finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-b05c-f5794180a6bd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-aabf-ce3abce6932c" class="">6) SafeActive at time t (invariant base, intersection with current Active)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-84ae-c58c6ad1602f" class="">For any later state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-92a0-fe8d16eab1a7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeActive(\Sigma_0,\Sigma_t)=\{c\in Active(\Sigma_t) : c.id \in SafeBase(\Sigma_0)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-a3ad-ce4224d216d9" class="">This is the “fixed base” rule needed for the semantic bound theorem.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-bdc7-c66a660da711"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-bdc2-c252e2157d50" class="">7) Decision procedure (fully explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-84eb-e19f42cb60f6" class="">Compute <code>SafeBase(Σ0)</code> in three passes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-b861-db29c7b5f60b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Precompute:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8938-c3db27d7f256" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>goodMetric : MetricId → Bool</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-bf36-f7a0cf3cde6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>goodEvidence : EvidenceId → Bool</code></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-806a-dc5f07cd9a18" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>For each claim :<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-907f-c9913b2f29f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if not Active → skip</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-ab67-de7346ff4844" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if not ExportOK → skip</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-a013-f609ece30153" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">check support-type:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-857b-d84fe4abf39f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Emp: require <code>metrics</code> all present+good, and <code>evidence</code> non-empty and all present+good</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-8739-c76051a2e4c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Def/Prim/Lim: accept</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-a1fe-eb97c27895a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Inf/MB: reject</li></ul></div></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-9bbd-cfb7dd721652" class="">Return the set of accepted claim ids.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-974b-d1263cc126bd" class="">Complexity: linear in total referenced metrics/evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a47c-d22f1f554f21"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-abbd-c4773fa6a298" class="">8) Theorems needed for the end-to-end bound (now provable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-b007-d1e178634631" class="">8.1 SafeBase is decidable and finite</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a938-caccb2c35737" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem SafeBase_finite : ∀ s0, Finite (SafeBase s0) := by sorry
theorem SafeBase_decidable : ∀ s0 cId, Decidable (cId ∈ SafeBase s0) := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-bd9a-f9a7f695e390" class="">8.2 SafeBase is invariant under rewrites (by definition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8c73-c98d3d4c914b" class="">Since SafeBase depends only on :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-8fc6-f3c4764c7690" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem SafeBase_invariant :
  ∀ s0 s, SafeBase s0 = SafeBase s0 := by intro s0 s; rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-b9ad-d97b5099d965" class="">8.3 SafeActive shrinks or stays under step (fixed base)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-80e2-cb5d1ae52b1d" class="">Because step can block claims or add new claims, but SafeActive selects only ids from SafeBase(s0):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-b248-fa78b08a2c54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">New claims are not in SafeBase(s0).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b3e2-e401d9723ab6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Blocking can remove claims from Active, thus removing them from SafeActive.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-ad79-fd70b84c544a" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-be35-e85604dc1fdc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SafeActive(s0, step(s)) \subseteq SafeActive(s0, s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a036-e5d0ccfd3126" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket step(s)\rrbracket_{safe} \supseteq \llbracket s\rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8333-ec389fc78fd2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket s\rrbracket_{safe} \subseteq \llbracket s0\rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8cca-fc875f5353b2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-b34f-e58eff8c0b4c" class="">9) End-to-end bound theorem (now fully pinned)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-a3d3-c8aacb099d9f" class="">Let  be compiled, and  sealed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-80b3-d6f11d8784e3" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a533-c130a18821f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_0 = \llbracket \Sigma_0 \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-876e-f7ed4451972c" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-948a-dd68a77038da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma_N \rrbracket_{cons} \subseteq \mathcal{E}_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a3ef-f511f91a72e1" class="">Because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-939a-e5850e390693" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">any commitment in that is safe-export-eligible must be derived from claims in SafeBase()</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9b69-ef4a1cf40768" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and Safe semantics evaluates only that fixed base subset</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b067-ececc8a548dc" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9204-ed343ede18ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma_N \rrbracket_{cons}\subseteq \llbracket \Sigma_0 \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-8256-e2f8ba3babab" class="">with SafeBase now explicitly defined.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-87c8-c19e314b4562"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-9fb9-db4bb43594f6" class="">10) Only remaining “more” after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9d40-e2c83944b9d1" class="">At this point, the only meaningful “more” is to expand SafeBase beyond the conservative default:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b65c-ebecbcb82b06" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Gated inferential export</strong>: allow certain Inf claims into SafeBase if their dependency closure is in SafeBase and their inference rule is whitelisted and budget-compliant.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-8dd8-f5b1cf01d9b0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Quantitative metric semantics</strong>: interpret metrics as actual numeric traces in the world model (not just meta-data), enabling proofs that Emp claims are grounded in measurements.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b283-cea716d8f96d" class="">Anything else would be repetition of structure already closed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9d63-f751edc43b6c" class="">XLV. More: end-to-end semantic bound theorem in the concrete world model (rewrite-by-rewrite monotonicity + sealed safety envelope)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-a77d-f1e52eaca39b" class="">This completes the semantics track: from a compiled initial state  through rewrites to a sealed , we prove the final commitments are <strong>bounded</strong> by an initial <strong>safe envelope</strong>, using the concrete finite relational+temporal world model from XLIV.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-82ba-d6dae5bbfdbb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-9716-c63833592389" class="">1) Two concrete semantics sets (now fully defined)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9cca-d1f63973f1c2" class="">Let  be the set of all worlds satisfying the world axioms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-b604-e74801523ffd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">literal complement axioms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ac64-eb48dfa25e33" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predicate complement axioms for neg-pairs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9dca-ff8fe9b179e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">mutex axioms for mutex object pairs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-baa0-d68b6cbc38a9" class="">1.1 Conservative semantics (commitments)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b96d-ebe9db9b74d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma \rrbracket_{cons}=\{w\in\mathcal{W}:\forall c\in Active(\Sigma),\ w\models c\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-b6a4-f53031bce326" class="">1.2 Safe semantics (envelope)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e9-af41-ce5b76146fef" class="">Define <code>SafeActive(Σ)</code> as the subset of claims whose commitments are allowed to constrain the safe envelope:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-a26f-e49943a74c8e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exclude blocked claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9d30-f3bb6ecc99de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exclude MB claims unless they have explicit allowance <strong>and</strong> empirical gating passes (if used)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-90ff-fa89cb6abf27" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">optionally exclude “conflict-separation ctx literals” (the ones introduced only to break contradictions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-9134-e7a4158a9023" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-acf3-f5f31b8b5a57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
