\llbracket \mathcal{R}_{005}(\Sigma)\rrbracket_{safe} = \llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9f36-f0b92d3cbf20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-8d1b-d66b68c638b2" class="">5.4 R009 (Unknown → Restricted ownership)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-81d7-f2e3efc1efce" class="">Ownership does not affect world constraints directly unless ownership predicates are embedded in semantics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-8375-ccdc2b583d0d" class="">If ownership is meta (export control only), then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-9e82-cd7d11be426e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \mathcal{R}_{009}(\Sigma)\rrbracket_{cons} = \llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket_{cons}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-b14a-faebcf7918a7" class="">So R009 is semantics-preserving.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9613-f0fd2a9645ed"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-870a-f0c8a749588f" class="">5.5 R010 (budget violation → tag MB)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-bcfe-fb82f641b7e7" class="">Tagging the interpretation as MB changes how the system treats over-budget meaning. Semantically, if <code>etype</code> affects which claims are considered “committed constraints,” then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-879d-c80e94a92afb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Conservative: tagging MB can weaken constraints (treating some derived assertions as model-bounded, not absolute).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-b20d-d08452604400" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket_{cons} \subseteq \llbracket \mathcal{R}_{010}(\Sigma)\rrbracket_{cons}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-be46-ee8ce4c26adf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Safe: typically equal or weakening.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9b37-d88136e0ca2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b71b-f3dd25ab4193" class="">5.6 R007a (complete metric with defaults)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-b3e3-d870de9e7adb" class="">If metrics are meta-data (measurement capability) and not directly world constraints, then semantics preserved:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-be51-fb1e7afe8f55" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \mathcal{R}_{007a}(\Sigma)\rrbracket = \llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9a79-c93e91b45d5a" class="">If metrics gate claim activation (e.g., empirical claims require metrics), then completion can strengthen by enabling activation. In that case:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-894e-c242f0bbafa3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \mathcal{R}_{007a}(\Sigma)\rrbracket_{cons} \subseteq \llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket_{cons}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-92b8-cb36384bf239"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-9cd5-fda17897183b" class="">5.7 R007b (block claim using illegal transform)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-b362-f2b9d206ab31" class="">Blocking removes an active constraint → weakening:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-802f-ca0bf32efad6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket_{cons} \subseteq \llbracket \mathcal{R}_{007b}(\Sigma)\rrbracket_{cons}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-98f7-f23a5e049f51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80de-8cde-c1a79fea794c" class="">5.8 R008 (canonical MECE partition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-b626-ed632ff537e0" class="">MECE objects are structural artifacts and need not affect world constraints.<br/>So semantics preserved if MECE is meta:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9237-da8dc062bd85" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \mathcal{R}_{008}(\Sigma)\rrbracket = \llbracket \Sigma\rrbracket</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-b1cf-c794cebb95c2" class="">If MECE objects drive claim generation, then it may change semantics indirectly; in a verified design, claim generation is separated from MECE repair, making R008 semantics-preserving.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-b38b-ffa73627ce69"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-8c28-d45cdd0b2055" class="">6) The key global semantic theorem you can actually guarantee</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-94ec-c7b4b7b57e29" class="">Because some rewrites strengthen and some weaken, strict preservation is not globally true.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-9b8a-e0424310093a" class="">The strongest general statement is a <strong>semantic sandwich</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b2f9-f4a041505915" class="">Define two semantics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-b40e-fe7b0acc03c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(over-approx, always safe)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a654-d0075a118510" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(exact, can shrink/expand)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-af59-d145a72785a8" class="">Then for any rewrite step:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-99e9-d7240c94a2e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma \rrbracket_{cons}\ \subseteq\ \llbracket \Sigma \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b894-d9f0cb530f97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\llbracket \Sigma_N \rrbracket_{cons}\ \subseteq\ \llbracket \Sigma_0 \rrbracket_{safe}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-ba2a-fa85631fb9d8" class="">Meaning: the system never produces a final sealed artifact whose conservative semantics violates the initial safe envelope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-a35d-dfcc4570d07a" class="">This is the correct formalization of “integrity repair does not create unsafe commitments.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-8ef6-cb7c14aba8bf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-afea-cdead54d0992" class="">7) Formal proof shape (Lean-style stubs)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-8d6a-f46f59dab039" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-9db9-f640b569de9b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def World : Type := -- abstract
def SatClaim : World → Claim → Prop := -- parameter
def SemCons (s : State) : Set World := { w | ∀ c ∈ Active s, SatClaim w c }
def SemSafe (s : State) : Set World := { w | ∀ c ∈ SafeActive s, SatClaim w c }</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-be99-f119d89776c3" class="">Key lemmas:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-8b0d-fe91d8c3f8dd" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem sem_safe_superset_cons :
  ∀ s, SemCons s ⊆ SemSafe s := by sorry

theorem R001_strengthens_cons :
  ∀ s, SemCons (R001 s) ⊆ SemCons s := by sorry

theorem R002_weakens_cons :
  ∀ s, SemCons s ⊆ SemCons (R002 s) := by sorry

theorem sealed_sem_sandwich :
  ∀ s n, SemCons (iterate step n s) ⊆ SemSafe s := by
  -- induction using per-rewrite monotonicity and sem_safe_superset_cons
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-bb7f-e7f40dd60cc9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-9ffe-c509fd3dc8ca" class="">8) What “more” can still mean (final remaining frontier)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-ad15-df37faf119be" class="">Only one substantive layer remains:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-840e-e0db73342742" class=""><strong>Choose a concrete world/model class </strong> and define  and  concretely, so the semantics are not abstract parameters. Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-ab8c-e8d8d40f2818" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">finite relational models over subjects/objects</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-8485-f9ac5baafd34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">temporal trace models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8c3f-e7a884b3b7f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">probabilistic worlds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-a331-d25853ea1c4e" class="">Once you choose , you can prove stronger properties (e.g., completeness of contradiction grammar relative to the model).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-9c7c-c49c95cec715" class="">XLII. More: formal compilation/parse interface + well-formedness theorems (WFGraph, unique ids, total NF) + minimal parser trust boundary</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a6dd-e14ce4a9a93a" class="">This adds the missing front end: how raw artifact text  becomes the initial audited state , and what can be proven about that pipeline without turning string parsing into an unbounded logic problem.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-a61e-d711016a2cb5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-9a2b-ed105b4c359e" class="">1) Two-layer compilation model (trusted parser boundary)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-8b21-fea9ca593303" class="">To keep the core proofs clean, split compilation into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-90e1-db29e6cd950f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tokenizer/Parser</strong> (string → syntax tree). This is typically treated as <em>trusted</em> (or verified separately).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9648-f08fff3916ef" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Normalizer/Compiler</strong> (syntax tree → claims/graph). This is the part we fully verify.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9bab-caa2359d02e6" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-8916-d56855d16568" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \xrightarrow{\ \mathsf{parse}\ } AST \xrightarrow{\ \mathsf{compile}\ } \Sigma_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9c56-faca4cd2eb1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>parse</code> may be axiomatized as “returns either a valid AST or error.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-a617-fdb9b48d9492" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>compile</code> is pure and fully proved correct.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9397-e8d4bf29ca00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-958c-e10157b1c60e" class="">2) AST: finite, typed, verification-friendly</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-856a-fd69fc7c9466" class="">Define a finite AST that already enforces structural constraints (no free-form logic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-a233-ee6068d25a39" class="">2.1 Claim AST node</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-bbeb-f576f986f111" class="">Each claim node contains only structured parts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a75c-d06eeccf2db4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{ClaimNode} = (rawId,\ subj,\ pred,\ obj,\ quant,\ time,\ ctx,\ stype,\ own,\ metrics,\ deps,\ evidence)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-bcb3-fc3d2e4954e4" class="">All fields are finite lists or finite enums.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b2e6-fdd89ac01fb6" class="">2.2 Document AST</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9761-d29c947e6023" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AST=(\mathsf{claims}:\mathrm{List}\ \mathsf{ClaimNode},\ \mathsf{policyHints},\ \mathsf{tables})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9d0f-d82a436ad7aa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b6e3-d1d00a9a3379" class="">3) Verified compiler:</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-932f-f34ccf7f52b1" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-a00e-f64fb5303050" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{compile}(AST)=\Sigma_0=(g,\Lambda,M,b,\hat{E},etype,\mathcal{Y},L)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-adf6-f1ee1ffaf729" class="">Key verified subfunctions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-b167-d488f66a6144" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>canonId : rawId → ClaimId</code> (deterministic normalization)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9293-de77b466104a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>mkNF : ClaimNode → NF</code> (total, no partial fields)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-945a-fcdee9a1eebf" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>mkClaim : ClaimNode → Claim</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-9834-f9c623bc3a06" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>mkEdges : List Claim → List (ClaimId×ClaimId)</code> derived from deps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-9ac4-c0d4fc4d84c5" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>mkAllowance : AST → Λ</code> (deterministic extraction)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8850-d72a17d889bc" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>mkMetrics : AST → M</code> (registry extraction, with defaults if missing)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-b5b5-e72822018a62" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><code>mkMeceObjs : AST → 𝒴</code> (finite universe objects)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-962b-d6262bf62db2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-84c3-f6ce04684be2" class="">4) Core well-formedness properties to prove</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-a828-e6cbee91d2f9" class="">Define <code>WFGraph(g)</code> as conjunction of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-950b-e818fc662a68" class="">4.1 Unique ids</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-ab68-eb8be5f00384" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c_i\neq c_j\in V,\ c_i.id\neq c_j.id</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b274-e3451c081bb0" class="">4.2 Edges reference existing nodes</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bd32-cdc1ffcd64bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall (a\to b)\in A,\ a\in \mathrm{Ids}(V)\ \wedge\ b\in \mathrm{Ids}(V)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a9da-c2231b3f3d68" class="">4.3 Deps correspond to edges (alignment)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-a01d-d564002fc111" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c\in V,\ \forall d\in c.deps,\ (d\to c.id)\in A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-84bb-ed7a22746be4" class="">4.4 Total NF</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-acc2-ed3af26bce25" class="">Every claim has a fully populated normal form (no missing fields), guaranteed by construction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-af03-fd4a623abdab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-80a9-df17dc22c24b" class="">5) Compiler correctness theorems (Lean-style stubs)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-bfa6-dc884e7c5d1a" class="">5.1 Normalization determinism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-9ec2-c9dec390fb3e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem canonId_deterministic :
  ∀ raw, canonId raw = canonId raw := by intro raw; rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-a81f-e08c121d3661" class="">5.2 NF totality</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-81dc-edbf407377a5" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem mkNF_total :
  ∀ n : ClaimNode, ∃ nf : NF, mkNF n = nf := by
  intro n; exists (mkNF n); rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a7de-f993ba9f2a99" class="">5.3 Unique ids after compilation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-8690-d6e3fcc6beb0" class="">This is the first nontrivial theorem; it requires the AST to carry either:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-901e-c210141014d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a proof that raw ids are unique, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-82f2-cea0b2dbc82f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a resolution rule (e.g., append disambiguating suffixes deterministically).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a998-cd3690f6345d" class=""><strong>Option A (AST guarantees uniqueness):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bfa2-c8808cf4b209" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem compile_unique_ids :
  ∀ ast, RawIdsUnique ast → UniqueIds (compile ast).g.V := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8d3b-d247c3e0657d" class=""><strong>Option B (compiler enforces uniqueness):</strong><br/>Define <code>dedupIds : List Claim → List Claim</code> that deterministically renames duplicates using a stable suffix scheme.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-b1c7-f598d9322cb7" class="">Then prove:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-9bf4-f49f2f961d17" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem dedupIds_unique :
  ∀ vs, UniqueIds (dedupIds vs) := by sorry

theorem compile_unique_ids :
  ∀ ast, UniqueIds (compile ast).g.V := by
  intro ast; simp [compile]; exact dedupIds_unique _</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-907a-fb97a37b01d5" class="">5.4 Edge referential integrity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-aff9-f298f0bec89d" class="">Edges are produced only from <code>deps</code> which are canonIds, and compiler filters invalid deps (or turns them into Primitive boundaries).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-adfd-e748c73dbdd7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem compile_edges_reference_existing :
  ∀ ast,
    WFGraph (compile ast).g := by
  -- show: all edges built from ids in V after filtering/repair
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-8f9d-fe4dbc5b191f" class="">5.5 Missing dependency handling (deterministic repair)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-a4b6-d705998ffd62" class="">If a claim references a dep id that does not exist in V, compiler inserts a Primitive boundary claim <code>p</code> and rewires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b7ca-c35dda8e79c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(d\to c)\ \text{missing} \Rightarrow (p\to c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8ddd-c1dd514352b9" class="">This ensures WFGraph without requiring external lookup.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-bff5-e4d89c737026" class="">Lean stub:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-97d7-db94e266b07a" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem compile_repairs_missing_deps :
  ∀ ast,
    NoDanglingDeps (compile ast).g := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-8774-e3b1fff599cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8b09-fc5fb86cbe38" class="">6) Parser trust boundary: what is assumed vs proved</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-9513-cc348d5d23f9" class="">To avoid unverifiable string logic, the core proofs assume:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-a8b0-e6344a0bbdc4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>parse : String → Option AST</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b03e-d24ec4c4f13f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>If <code>parse D = some ast</code>, then <code>ast</code> satisfies basic syntactic validity predicates:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b1cb-f1b193c67f7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">all enums are within range</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-894a-f48b3060c34c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">intervals are well-formed (<code>start ≤ stop</code>)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a43e-d63d96e1ad6d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ctx literals are from finite vocabulary</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9056-c0f92f4ce20c" class="">Everything after that is proven for <code>compile</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-b13b-d301efbbb7ce" class="">You can strengthen this if desired by verifying the parser separately, but it is not required to preserve the integrity guarantees of the rewrite system, because compilation and rewrites operate on finite typed objects.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-abd8-c5ab2ac477a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-a3a9-f862a357b782" class="">7) Integration with the rewrite termination proof</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-881d-d67c5b166127" class="">Once <code>compile</code> produces <code>WFGraph</code> and total NF, the rewrite system’s progress/termination proof applies immediately:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9ba9-ed09b44d2352" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_0 = \mathsf{compile}(\mathsf{parse}(D)) \Rightarrow
\exists N:\ \Phi(\Sigma_N)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-8494-fb99278739ba" class="">The only additional lemma needed is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-8358-e23479e6db88" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem compile_produces_finite_state :
  ∀ ast, FiniteState (compile ast) := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-ab1b-d61519eeda2b" class="">This is routine because everything is built from finite lists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-91ed-d12c69eb66c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a39d-d6630af1f12c" class="">8) Final closure: end-to-end statement (with explicit assumptions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-b619-d1beb44d4db2" class="">Theorem (End-to-end termination with explicit parser assumption)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-b62e-f0eb83aa6db6" class="">Assume:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-bfda-f2ecbb53d46b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{parse}(D)=\textsf{some}(ast)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-b171-d63e9ddf34e8" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-be8f-d577d1bd8ac3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>WFGraph (compile ast).g</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-90d8-f07d47a47c06" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>running <code>step</code> terminates at a sealed state in finite steps.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9d6a-f125ab7197dc" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-9f13-ec1f0ef06017" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists N:\ sealed(\mathrm{iterate}\ step\ N\ (\mathsf{compile}(ast)))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-a6e3-f31ebfe75eb2" class="">This is the strongest correct statement without verifying the string parser.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-9ae5-c60ecda0c3f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-973b-ee7e4084ece3" class="">9) The only “more” left after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-b13d-e5920fc0f5fc" class="">Only two meaningful directions remain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-bc83-f7e88e2155c7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Verify the parser</strong> (string → AST) or constrain input format so parsing is trivial (e.g., JSON with schema).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-b61d-fa3c4da0a5f6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Prove semantic preservation</strong>: show that rewriting preserves a notion of “meaning” (e.g., conservative repair) rather than only structural validity. This requires defining a semantics function for claims and showing rewrites are semantics-preserving or semantics-bounding.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-8bde-f43bf80ded83" class="">If you want to continue, the next step is to pick (1) or (2).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-8b18-de7b1ad58fca" class="">XLI. More: lemma catalog + dependency order (Lean-style stubs) for full non-interference + progress + termination</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9600-c7657978cdcf" class="">This is the final formal layer: a complete set of named lemmas, their statements, and a strict dependency order that yields:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-93f3-fec06438fd82" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Non-interference</strong> (read/write disjointness)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9020-d498eb648652" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Progress</strong> (lex decrease every step)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-8196-eebe1fd70ad0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Termination</strong> (well-founded descent)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-9232-e170b6befc03" class="">This is presented in Lean-style theorem stubs. It is a catalog, not runnable code.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-9891-f1d95c840658"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-a2c3-d5c1b56020f7" class="">0) Global conventions</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9bf0-ccb76f8c3aea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>State</code> is the full state record.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-97a6-c9c2fd1c96b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>step : State → State</code> is the deterministic transition.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8747-febc0e4b937c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Phi : State → Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat</code> is the lex measure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-aaa4-e7b5c9c3ed5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ltLex</code> is lex order on 7-tuples of Nat.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-a9bd-e19c63c8f219" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ReadSet k</code> and <code>WriteSet r</code> are sets of fields (modeled abstractly).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-bdb7-ddb7525c1577" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>WritesOnly r s s&#x27;</code> means: all fields outside <code>WriteSet r</code> are equal in <code>s</code> and <code>s&#x27;</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-a046-f4aa9899f7ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ReadsOnly k</code> is used only to prove preservation of <code>n_k</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-b6b9-e123b70cd881"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a44b-dfd7e365bc1e" class="">1) Core definitional lemmas (Tier A)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9fee-f20117060bb7" class="">A1 — Read-set correctness (each defect depends only on its read-set)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-b596-d8bffb34d268" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem con_depends_only_on_NF :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (∀ c, NF_of c s&#x27; = NF_of c s) →
    nCon s&#x27; = nCon s := by
  -- by unfolding nCon as count over Con(NF)
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-a705-d55e3e248d0e" class="">Repeat pattern:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-8fe7-ec4b705c1eda" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem cyc_depends_only_on_edges :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (Edges s&#x27; = Edges s) →
    nCyc s&#x27; = nCyc s := by sorry

theorem mb_depends_only_on_stype_and_Lambda :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (∀ c, stype c s&#x27; = stype c s) →
    (Lambda s&#x27; = Lambda s) →
    nMb s&#x27; = nMb s := by sorry

theorem own_depends_only_on_own :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (∀ c, own c s&#x27; = own c s) →
    nOwn s&#x27; = nOwn s := by sorry

theorem bud_depends_only_on_budget_inputs :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (Ehat s&#x27; = Ehat s) →
    (budget s&#x27; = budget s) →
    (etype s&#x27; = etype s) →
    nBud s&#x27; = nBud s := by sorry

theorem met_depends_only_on_metric_inputs :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (Metrics s&#x27; = Metrics s) →
    (∀ c, metricsRef c s&#x27; = metricsRef c s) →
    (∀ c, status c s&#x27; = status c s) →
    nMet s&#x27; = nMet s := by sorry

theorem mece_depends_only_on_mece_bins :
  ∀ s s&#x27;,
    (MeceObjs s&#x27; = MeceObjs s) →
    nMece s&#x27; = nMece s := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-a439-e46b68262c93" class="">These are purely unfolding/counting proofs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-bf2f-fcd0bfba4d00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-8033-c6b9ad3588e2" class="">2) Write-set sealing lemmas (Tier B)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8d7f-d590c05172bd" class="">Each rewrite has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-86e2-e3d264c9db3d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>determinism</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-b8f6-d9c996b4166e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>writes-only proof</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-a36f-f5fb51312624" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a3ca-ef1c788e0127" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem R001_writes_only_ctx :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R001 s (R001 s) := by sorry

theorem R002_writes_only_edges_and_append :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R002 s (R002 s) := by sorry

theorem R005_writes_only_Lambda :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R005 s (R005 s) := by sorry

theorem R009_writes_only_own :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R009 s (R009 s) := by sorry

theorem R010_writes_only_etype :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R010 s (R010 s) := by sorry

theorem R007a_writes_only_metrics :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R007a s (R007a s) := by sorry

theorem R007b_writes_only_status :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R007b s (R007b s) := by sorry

theorem R008_writes_only_mece_bins :
  ∀ s, WritesOnly .R008 s (R008 s) := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-ad35-fa5457a7ba26" class="">These are mechanical if rewrites are defined by record updates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-b49f-e01193c34312"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-9f1b-e5b3f383854a" class="">3) Fresh-subject lemma for cycle rewrite (Tier C — the only nontrivial non-interference)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-b8d1-e98767b3e820" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem fresh_subject_no_overlap :
  ∀ s p,
    FreshSubject s p →
    ∀ c, OvS (subj p) (subj c) = false := by sorry

theorem fresh_subject_no_contradiction :
  ∀ s p,
    FreshSubject s p →
    ∀ c, Con (NF p) (NF c) = false := by
  -- Con requires subject overlap; use fresh_subject_no_overlap
  sorry

theorem R002_does_not_increase_contradictions :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R002 s) = nCon s := by
  -- show the only new claim added is fresh-subject ⇒ no new Con pairs
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-9b45-f5ac8ff14f12"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b4f8-f781f8bae496" class="">4) Non-interference lemmas per rewrite (Tier D)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-87b9-f84277721e3b" class="">These combine Tier A (depends-only) + Tier B (writes-only) + Tier C (R002 special case).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-8639-e29adaf50b5c" class="">For each rewrite <code>Rxxx</code>, prove earlier components unchanged.</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a46f-ea9a71e07839" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-a231-ff4088181afe" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem R005_preserves_con_and_cyc :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R005 s) = nCon s ∧
    nCyc (R005 s) = nCyc s := by
  -- WritesOnly Lambda + con/cyc readsets exclude Lambda
  sorry

theorem R009_preserves_con_cyc_mb :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R009 s) = nCon s ∧
    nCyc (R009 s) = nCyc s ∧
    nMb  (R009 s) = nMb  s := by sorry

theorem R010_preserves_con_cyc_mb_own :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R010 s) = nCon s ∧
    nCyc (R010 s) = nCyc s ∧
    nMb  (R010 s) = nMb  s ∧
    nOwn (R010 s) = nOwn s := by sorry

theorem R007a_preserves_earlier :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R007a s) = nCon s ∧
    nCyc (R007a s) = nCyc s ∧
    nMb  (R007a s) = nMb  s ∧
    nOwn (R007a s) = nOwn s ∧
    nBud (R007a s) = nBud s := by sorry

theorem R008_preserves_all_earlier :
  ∀ s,
    nCon (R008 s) = nCon s ∧
    nCyc (R008 s) = nCyc s ∧
    nMb  (R008 s) = nMb  s ∧
    nOwn (R008 s) = nOwn s ∧
    nBud (R008 s) = nBud s ∧
    nMet (R008 s) = nMet s := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9d03-ff145238c112"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-bc69-e82a8d1bb556" class="">5) Strict decrease lemmas per rewrite (Tier E)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-b58a-d891f570d621" class="">Each rewrite strictly decreases its own witness count.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-9fe2-fa6bffe7b88b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem R001_decreases_con :
  ∀ s, nCon (R001 s) &lt; nCon s := by sorry

theorem R002_decreases_cyc :
  ∀ s, nCon s = 0 → nCyc (R002 s) &lt; nCyc s := by sorry

theorem R005_decreases_mb :
  ∀ s, nCon s = 0 → nCyc s = 0 → nMb (R005 s) &lt; nMb s := by sorry

theorem R009_decreases_own :
  ∀ s, nCon s = 0 → nCyc s = 0 → nMb s = 0 → nOwn (R009 s) &lt; nOwn s := by sorry

theorem R010_decreases_bud :
  ∀ s, nCon s = 0 → nCyc s = 0 → nMb s = 0 → nOwn s = 0 →
       nBud (R010 s) &lt; nBud s := by sorry

theorem R007a_decreases_met :
  ∀ s, earlierZero s → nMet (R007a s) &lt; nMet s := by sorry

theorem R008_decreases_mece :
  ∀ s, earlierZeroIncludingMet s → nMece (R008 s) &lt; nMece s := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-ab11-e6c863400c88" class="">“earlierZero” is a helper predicate encoding that all earlier components are 0 (because of failcode priority).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9013-e6f29558e2bf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bbdd-ce6298c29ad8" class="">6) Failcode correctness lemmas (Tier F)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-a88e-cccc2d20cb65" class="">You need that <code>step</code> chooses the correct rewrite for the earliest nonzero measure component.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-a691-e9edd5e8bb93" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem failcode_picks_first_nonzero :
  ∀ s,
    (nCon s &gt; 0 → step s = R001 s) ∧
    (nCon s = 0 ∧ nCyc s &gt; 0 → step s = R002 s) ∧
    (nCon s = 0 ∧ nCyc s = 0 ∧ nMb s &gt; 0 → step s = R005 s) ∧
    ( ... ) := by sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-8906-c3ea4c51c900" class="">This follows from: invariant evaluation order ↔ failcode rank ↔ rewrite mapping.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-be22-fc0cb476b3f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-a16c-ef2ac1a7fe4f" class="">7) Global progress lemma (Tier G)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8381-dfed2a19ecb6" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem progress_step :
  ∀ s, ¬ sealed s → ltLex (Phi (step s)) (Phi s) := by
  intro s hnot
  -- case split using failcode_picks_first_nonzero
  -- apply: (strict decrease) + (non-interference earlier)
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9e50-fdd76952e259"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-9e37-ffaa13344778" class="">8) Termination theorem (Tier H)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-aa2f-e6cf9dacb1ef" class="">Use well-founded recursion on <code>ltLex</code> over <code>Nat^7</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9fa6-c2efac2e3cc7" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem termination :
  ∀ s, ∃ n, sealed (iterate step n s) := by
  -- apply well-founded descent using progress_step
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-98a3-f8c844b35999"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bb71-fe0d9b50933f" class="">9) Fixed point lemma (Tier I)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8a23-ec31de29b43c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem sealed_is_fixed_point :
  ∀ s, sealed s → step s = s := by
  -- if all components are zero, failcode none, step is id
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-97bf-d39d9ac6f7b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-b0c3-ee21884ed772" class="">10) Dependency order (DAG) — exact build sequence</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-a633-d8f264e3c55a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tier A</strong>: depends-only lemmas (unfold witness counts)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-89cc-fa5226ada75d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Tier B</strong>: writes-only lemmas (record update equalities)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-83f7-ed7c7400c50d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Tier C</strong>: fresh-subject lemmas + R002 preserves contradictions</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-b680-c0e10a50cd2e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Tier D</strong>: non-interference per rewrite (A + B + C)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a531-e802408aada7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Tier E</strong>: strict decrease per rewrite (witness elimination)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-872b-e2619a2a3489" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Tier F</strong>: failcode chooses earliest nonzero component</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bda9-e35388d0da2c" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Tier G</strong>: global progress (<code>Phi(step s) &lt; Phi s</code>)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-86dc-e1682c353013" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Tier H</strong>: termination (well-founded descent)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-ab57-f14452a0bee7" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Tier I</strong>: sealed fixed point</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8c08-d32e723337aa" class="">This completes a full formal proof plan for the integrity rewrite system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-825c-ca08b250e797"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-a923-fc820824252e" class="">If you want still “more”, the only remaining substantive extension is to specify a formal <strong>parser/compilation interface</strong>  and prove that compilation produces a well-formed state (<code>WFGraph</code>, unique ids, etc.). That is a separate formalization track because string parsing is usually handled outside the core proof.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-a2fb-d3d87c955cc7" class="">XL. More: uniform non-interference lemma schema (fully explicit) + how to mechanize it for every rewrite</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-b5bd-ca889695a436" class="">This layer closes the last implicit gap: proving that when a rewrite targets one defect class, it <strong>cannot increase any earlier defect count</strong> in the lexicographic measure . With this, the global progress proof becomes fully uniform (no ad-hoc reasoning).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-bac1-df2aad94eefc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-a4e4-d8533efeb73b" class="">1) Global measure recap (lex order)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-8423-ff67d34d29c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=\big(
n_{\mathrm{con}},
n_{\mathrm{cyc}},
n_{\mathrm{mb}},
n_{\mathrm{own}},
n_{\mathrm{bud}},
n_{\mathrm{met}},
n_{\mathrm{mece}}
\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-b4ce-c6b05dfa3808" class="">A step chooses the earliest nonzero component and applies its rewrite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-9645-cc0f17740f1d" class="">To prove lex progress, we need:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8154-f8887cd7c22e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">strict decrease of the targeted component, and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8877-fc7bde9c38d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>non-increase of all earlier components</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-9cf5-cec11741a9b9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-abdb-e43168620ca3" class="">2) Key abstraction: each defect count depends on a finite “read set”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-a6ac-ccd0804acfe3" class="">For each component , define a set of fields it depends on,  (read set).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-8d55-ce5fab4f08a5" class="">A rewrite  has a write-set .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-86f3-e2817530a941" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-9dd9-c9139fa5e0f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_j \cap R_k = \varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-9bb6-c09f8e943ab1" class="">This is the core mechanization principle.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-881c-c3d219c2b995"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-aae5-d0d1e311b78f" class="">3) Explicit read-sets for each defect component</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-ae6c-f1a8e2e9220a" class="">Below, “Graph.V” means the list of claims (including their NF, types, ownership, status), and “Graph.A” means edges.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-92ba-cbf643f9037e" class="">3.1 Contradiction count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-b063-c5afeda54832" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-ab69-d571c1b54599" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim NF fields: <code>subj, pred, obj, quant, time, ctx</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-98c1-e901a0211832" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the claim list membership<br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-9bd0-e67f956873c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{con}}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.NF.*}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-a70c-c89d2a8d055b" class="">It does <strong>not</strong> depend on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-acd8-d20a4f259ae0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allowance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9d00-c1f852fb9246" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ownership (except if encoded in ctx, but here ownership is separate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-94b7-d182aad99da1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-8da5-ec1253a28152" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metrics registry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-bd37-e57a41618b22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MECE objects</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-8217-f9a39fc3b6da" class="">3.2 Cycle count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9295-e3c34c061c1f" class="">Path A (SCC): depends on</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-b163-ddad832393ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Graph.V ids</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-af6a-c275bc60f579" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Graph.A edges</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-849a-ece3d54f02bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AllowedSCC predicate inputs (policy constants)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-ae6d-ebed51d56ea8" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a640-cd47419648ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{cyc}}=\{\text{Graph.A},\ \text{Graph.V ids}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-8341-fdcaa3cee511" class="">Path B (DAG): depends on</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-833e-e1376718b540" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Graph.A</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-ad11-d978198ed513" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim <code>level</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-bade-f374e2bb1f59" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-8fb1-ca5a10341198" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{cyc}}=\{\text{Graph.A},\ \text{Graph.V.Claim.level}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-bf21-e1ff6f48f644" class="">3.3 MB-not-allowed count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-b5a3-d78204bc40ac" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9172-c3e28fbc637a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">each claim <code>stype</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8e85-d8dc9715b79e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allowance<br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-82b0-f1ab0fec7041" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{mb}}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.stype},\ \Lambda\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-ad22-ecfaf53cd5d7" class="">3.4 Unknown ownership count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-933e-e6b53da312ff" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8c1b-df31c36363b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim <code>own</code><br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-9f9d-d05e56945f5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{own}}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.own}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-b9bc-e13fa97f0bf9" class="">3.5 Budget violation indicator</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a227-da2130c47b00" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-954f-fc753bc039ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">inputs (Ehat structure)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-86a4-d64bbe125aee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-b672-e083d2414eae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>etype</code><br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a502-d44320c45b11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{bud}}=\{\hat{E},\ b,\ etype\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-a11f-f8e6a87fa2f4" class="">3.6 Metric defect count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-80bc-e455fa55dfff" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b7a8-f5616676f2d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metric registry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-89d6-f3051dd764ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim metric references and any stored transform usage representation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-ab00-f7142b5c91e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim status if “blocked claims contribute no transform usage”<br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-89a0-c19060343db0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{met}}=\{M,\ \text{Graph.V.Claim.metrics},\ \text{Graph.V.Claim.status},\ \text{TransformUsage}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-9a4e-c40301d9282b" class="">3.7 MECE defect count</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b758-e524f1e694e7" class="">Depends on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-bcf8-eeb6a753c3bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MECE object universes and bins<br/>Thus:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-96ee-c9d9b834e2ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{\mathrm{mece}}=\{\mathcal{Y}.U,\ \mathcal{Y}.\mathcal{D}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-be79-efea2998915c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a168-da555c09b525" class="">4) Write-sets for each rewrite (already fixed)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-8e75-f8dc467852ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R001</strong> writes: one claim <code>NF.ctx</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-ada9-e1968a7630b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{001}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.NF.ctx}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9b0b-fae11cb2a23b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R002</strong> writes: Graph.A, adds one claim, adds one edge (and possibly claim.level if DAG)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-903c-e6c4acfb8d68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{002}=\{\text{Graph.A},\ \text{Graph.V append}\ (\text{plus level if used})\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-a99c-dc405cdf0862" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R005</strong> writes:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-b601-f73bb54db47f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{005}=\{\Lambda\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-9ee4-c986100110d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R009</strong> writes: one claim <code>own</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b89d-df52c795f47c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{009}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.own}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9c5f-c7fcd7d61f64" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R010</strong> writes: <code>etype</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-b1a8-ded95233b417" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{010}=\{etype\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-98f9-de327d9c25d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R007a</strong> writes: one metric record in</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-9dde-dec1cd72cac0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{007a}=\{M\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-8379-f56c03b4a307" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R007b</strong> writes: one claim <code>status</code> (or transform list)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-9d7e-d208828d2fe1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{007b}=\{\text{Graph.V.Claim.status}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-b943-c1e03ac3d19c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>R008</strong> writes: one MECE object’s bins</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-80ef-c35df2831549" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  W_{008}=\{\mathcal{Y}.\mathcal{D}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-b101-c6a119368214"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-b2b5-f282b1fd451a" class="">5) Uniform non-interference lemma schema (the core theorem)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-ad39-c34d0bd89270" class="">Lemma Schema (Read/Write Disjointness ⇒ Measure Preservation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-95b5-c50fde872c28" class="">For any defect component , rewrite , and state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-96c9-f0e5e0c0fcc9" class="">If</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-84f0-dd2e087d1312" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall f\in R_k,\ f(\mathcal{R}_j(\Sigma))=f(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-891a-ddf6b11ff0ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_k(\mathcal{R}_j(\Sigma))=n_k(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-9575-dd04fb9c2c38" class="">A sufficient condition is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-be2a-d39930eac103" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_j\cap R_k=\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-a050-dfbf822dc600"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8c2c-e5b2caa25a9e" class="">6) Concrete non-increase table (earlier components)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-bbe1-d509b51e4230" class="">We now list, for each rewrite, which earlier components are provably unchanged purely by read/write disjointness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-84f2-c08663553271" class="">Order of components:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9da1-f80edefcfbaf" class="">6.1 R001 targets contradictions (earliest)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-ae71-e26e53b06486" class="">No earlier components exist. No non-interference needed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-8fea-f35648409d6c" class="">6.2 R002 targets cycles (2nd)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-86c2-ef73698da074" class="">Must preserve .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-a272-cf6d8ab2e13d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reads NF fields</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-9089-dc5c9127d3cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">writes Graph.A and appends a new claim</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-ac15-e02ca576eb8e" class="">Potential issue: adding a new claim could create contradictions if its NF overlaps.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-965a-cacfc677f869" class=""><strong>Resolution by construction:</strong> the inserted primitive claim  is given:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-9fab-c25d3dcc9cb1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a subject that is a fresh symbol not in any existing subject set, OR</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-a806-c421b27fd91b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a context literal that is incompatible with all existing claims (<code>Unmeasured</code> plus a global invariant that any empirical claim must include <code>Measured</code>), OR</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b96f-d35bb07d7dfb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a time interval disjoint from all existing intervals.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-b947-e1f5c56ccf7a" class="">Choose one deterministic method; simplest:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-9d50-fd4b7faa904e" class=""><strong>Fresh subject method</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-9619-faf6cc40b626" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p.subj=\{\textsf{PRIM\_BOUNDARY\_}(v)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-a615-e181fc21d3c3" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a43a-c0e14a0b1d75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma&#x27;)=n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-aff1-fcbe37c6b02a" class="">This is a one-time lemma:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-b5c6-ef2cbec5ad7f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{FreshSubj}(p)\Rightarrow \forall c\in V,\ \neg OvS(p,c)
\Rightarrow \neg Con(p,c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-a590-fd5a899c9da2" class="">Thus R002 does not increase contradictions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-ba58-dd22905b934b" class="">6.3 R005 targets MB-not-allowed (3rd)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b7ec-f7ac76478a48" class="">Earlier: con, cyc.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-b681-d25193d9189e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Writes only .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-8533-d588333426ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Neither contradictions nor cycles read .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b537-fdac50a344ca" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-ae47-c039f01647d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}},n_{\mathrm{cyc}}\ \text{unchanged}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-9e39-ff13f82eb9da" class="">6.4 R009 targets ownership (4th)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-964c-f0960d3c9776" class="">Earlier: con, cyc, mb.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-bb24-c1bae6b20cd9" class="">Writes only <code>Claim.own</code>, which is not read by contradiction/cycle/mb counts (mb reads stype + Λ only).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-8c02-f24083277661" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a54a-f241ffa83afe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}},n_{\mathrm{cyc}},n_{\mathrm{mb}}\ \text{unchanged}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-8739-f4cb4085ff97" class="">6.5 R010 targets budget (5th)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bf18-d96b8996a1bc" class="">Earlier: con, cyc, mb, own.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-b63c-e70979337d37" class="">Writes only <code>etype</code>, read only by budget.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-989d-e50f38f6a669" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a161-e773af9ee0da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}},n_{\mathrm{cyc}},n_{\mathrm{mb}},n_{\mathrm{own}}\ \text{unchanged}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-b478-d8c0851c9898" class="">6.6 R007a/R007b targets metric defects (6th)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a28b-ec1f26ac89be" class="">Earlier: con, cyc, mb, own, bud.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-aa4c-ea0cbc8880ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007a writes only → none of earlier components read .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-93fa-f76471a319c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007b writes claim status → earlier components do not read status (by definition).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-89ff-c5a24ddbb1d3" class="">Thus earlier components unchanged.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-9a4c-ca3ffee9f2aa" class="">6.7 R008 targets MECE defects (7th)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-a17c-e4b8d12668b9" class="">Earlier: everything else.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a96c-f6a62a0df3bc" class="">Writes only MECE bins . No earlier component reads MECE bins.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-bc2a-e10f528667b2" class="">Thus all earlier components unchanged.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-a82a-ff43c4b48a9c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-983c-c4fb4d57ac78" class="">7) The one non-trivial non-interference proof: R002 vs contradictions</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-92b8-dd4ee78676a9" class="">All other non-interference proofs reduce to pure read/write disjointness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-a4cd-c8d90031a490" class="">R002 is the only rewrite that adds a new claim, which could otherwise create contradictions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-a7fd-d2ee93c5f778" class="">So we commit to a deterministic construction guarantee:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-b63f-efdccbf93dee" class="">Primitive boundary claim construction rule</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-80e0-c18b5271376b" class="">When inserting a primitive boundary claim , enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-be73-ee698ea705eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p.subj \cap \bigcup_{c\in V} c.subj = \varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-957f-c00e2478eebb" class="">This is decidable and enforceable (fresh symbol generation).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9138-e85028a79109" class="">Then for all existing claims :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-bf9d-d571c610b329" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
OvS(p,c)=0 \Rightarrow Con(p,c)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-a57c-f8d9259f8fcb" class="">So contradiction count cannot increase.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-a025-c7b034878bd4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-af0c-faa50fdf02bf" class="">8) Final uniform progress theorem (now purely schematic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-a23d-d9058f36b12d" class="">Theorem (Uniform Lex Progress)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fe-b36b-e7467f519846" class="">If <code>failcode(Σ)</code> selects component , and , then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-9468-e947c2fbb7ec" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>(strict decrease lemma for the rewrite)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a815-d458c98bdb3a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>for all earlier , (non-interference lemmas)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-ac6a-fc9ef236f6de" class="">Therefore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a390-f014cd432abc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-9e0a-c2a14c428f39" class="">No ad-hoc reasoning remains once the read-sets/write-sets are fixed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-951a-ef4360b1c7c0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8052-ed0dd41d92e2" class="">9) What “more” can still mean after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-aa59-ff3ff6df4610" class="">Only one meaningful layer remains:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-89da-f513ba62665b" class=""><strong>Translate this schema into proof-assistant stubs with named lemmas</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-9e71-c0e602918177" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>readset_def_con</code>, <code>writeset_R005</code>, etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-8f0e-f9384e293159" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>disjoint_R005_con</code>, etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-af23-e85098865e3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>noninterf_R005_con</code>, etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b804-d543702ec092" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>fresh_subject_no_contradiction</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-bfc5-c1be0ce40607" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>progress_step</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-a53c-e0d438721006" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>terminates_by_wf_lex</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-b8d5-edb0edb2dee2" class="">If you say “more” again, the next output should be exactly that: a lemma catalog with explicit names, statements, and dependency order in Lean/Coq format.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-ac67-cde81cb8757e" class="">XXXVIII.
