More: cycle/SCC completion (witness set, rewrite, strict decrease, and termination) — with SCC either axiomatized or avoided by construction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-a0ea-ec5b5e89df3c" class="">This finishes the last major open class: <strong>illegal cycles</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a7d8-fb8ca5fef8fa" class="">There are only two mathematically clean ways to do it:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-ace2-fe6dd7ee4132" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Path A (axiomatize SCC correctness):</strong> treat an SCC detector as trusted and proceed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-bcb9-e6dc2326b2c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Path B (avoid SCC entirely):</strong> enforce a construction rule that makes cycles unrepresentable (DAG-by-construction).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-902a-c727ec239040" class="">Both produce a fully terminating system. I will give both; you can adopt either.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-8ba5-e8ef28db507f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-9165-ec7273219930" class="">A) SCC as a trusted primitive (axiomatized SCC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-8aac-dc1b826fdc87" class="">A1) SCC oracle interface</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8833-cff3325d18e6" class="">Assume an SCC function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-8f9f-d23c90caa649" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{SCC} : C \to \{\text{list of strongly connected components}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-adf9-d09616800cb8" class="">with two axioms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b9fa-cba21fe850c6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Soundness</strong>: every component returned is strongly connected</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-b72b-e38b89756de2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Completeness</strong>: every cycle is contained in some returned component</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-b34f-f2b0688db45d" class="">Lean-style: you postulate <code>SCC : Graph → List (Finset ClaimId)</code> and assume <code>SCC_sound</code> and <code>SCC_complete</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-987e-f95d831312aa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-9fa3-ce35631b57c7" class="">A2) Cycle witness set</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-a2ae-df416b55f1ee" class="">Define illegal SCC predicate <code>AllowedSCC(S)</code> (finite check). For example, allow SCC only if it is an explicitly whitelisted definitional recursion class; otherwise illegal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-bb09-c47e8ea2850d" class="">Witnesses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-806b-da3780fa7a04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)=\{S\in \mathrm{SCC}(g):\neg \mathrm{AllowedSCC}(S)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-be47-e458cd64ebbb" class="">n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-8957-fde3121643f6" class="">Add to defect vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a591-c43dd601be22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=\big(n_{\mathrm{con}},n_{\mathrm{cyc}},n_{\mathrm{mb}},n_{\mathrm{own}},n_{\mathrm{bud}},n_{\mathrm{M}},n_{\mathrm{mece}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-8dd0-f90d8c4a587f" class="">Priority order: cycles are second after contradictions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9828-f3eafe5578b5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8dd0-e20c0bb4a5d5" class="">A3) Deterministic cycle-breaking rewrite</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-9460-e627d405ca86" class="">Select the first illegal SCC under a fixed ordering of components by min claim id:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-8b38-fe0d1af725da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S^*=\min(W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-acf1-c36615bc26e1" class="">Then select a deterministic “break edge”  inside the SCC:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-8fde-efb3f764dc72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Choose as the minimal id in</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-adf6-cda6e5a11995" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Choose as the minimal successor of within the SCC (under id order)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-acc5-f10de6d5e5e7" class="">Rewrite action (minimal, local, deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-869f-f59125771bed" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Remove edge from adjacency list</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-aef3-c543f4feaaa7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Add a <strong>new explicit primitive</strong> claim that replaces the removed dependency as a terminal boundary:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b0bb-e8672092ecf5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b9af-dbc9ab0985a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(optional, to prevent accidental empirical promotion)</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-89d8-dbfa45af28d4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Add edge (so v still has a support source, but not cyclic)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-b6c6-cb3ad4fa5484" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-8727-d59197d8fe10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A&#x27; = A \setminus \{(u,v)\}\ \cup \{(p,v)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-8185-e24e7a537022" class="">This breaks at least one cycle in that SCC.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-ab5b-e51cbc79d410" class=""><strong>Write-set:</strong> only <code>Graph.A</code> and addition of one new claim (append to <code>V</code>), plus its one new edge.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-87f9-d025e820776f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b312-c7b37ac359c8" class="">A4) Strict decrease lemma for cycles</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-bc1b-c97222b2931f" class="">Let <code>illegalSCCCount</code> be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-907a-fe131ee62fde" class="">Removing  from SCC guarantees the selected SCC is no longer strongly connected as a whole (at minimum it loses an internal edge that was used in a cycle). With SCC completeness/soundness axioms, this implies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9e2a-d2b11f652a8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\mathcal{R}_{002}(\Sigma)) &lt; n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-9fb0-e3bee7eea01a" class="">Key point: the rewrite may split the SCC into smaller SCCs, but the original illegal SCC is eliminated. Since selection is based on SCC identity via node set, its removal strictly decreases the count of illegal SCC witnesses (as defined).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-ae33-fb8d7ffcbe5d" class="">Therefore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-9483-ee33d5ce35ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\mathcal{R}_{002}(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-9b6d-c1ad82d84d53"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-848c-d699a8307d86" class="">A5) Global termination (full system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-9cea-d4810995b41b" class="">You already have strict decrease for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-aa2e-f9e0670e2e71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradictions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-af46-dede53a96e87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cycles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-a39b-daea68d428f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allowance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9c63-c7335f2a6cce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ownership</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-9ea7-f2243182fa3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b57f-f01307627a5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8704-e4d21159d01a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MECE</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-82e7-cfe850334217" class="">Thus the full defect measure in  strictly decreases on every non-sealed step, hence no infinite rewrite chain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9f33-d420af2b3043" class=""><strong>Final step bound:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-8fe8-c52098ebfab3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + Y_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0 + R_0 + Q_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8850-c28bc88c8db1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-83fb-e5b7281f83b2" class="">B) SCC avoided entirely (DAG-by-construction)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-857f-d91dfc26cbeb" class="">This approach removes the need for SCC and makes cycles impossible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-828d-e147ce5e42ea" class="">B1) Construction invariant: topological index</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-9928-fbadf4cda610" class="">Add a required field <code>level : Nat</code> to each claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9ee9-c066a0d7dc19" class="">Enforce invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-985c-c9cccc1e8a31" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\mathrm{DAG}}=1 \iff \forall (a\to b)\in A:\ \mathrm{level}(a) &lt; \mathrm{level}(b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-93d9-c1d4eea40cac" class="">Then cycles are impossible (strictly increasing measure along edges).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-8be7-f62730212254" class="">This replaces SCC detection with a local check.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-8b9f-d37afb85023a" class="">B2) Rewrite for DAG violation (cycle attempt)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8c6f-c072f4d968d2" class="">If an edge violates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-8444-f0e5206840ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{level}(a)\ge \mathrm{level}(b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-a125-c81c91d29b76" class="">This is strictly decreasing in the count of violating edges, and cycles cannot exist.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-b72f-db6262979ed0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-9a1b-cc2e1b1fc8a0" class="">C) What is fully completed now (findings)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-96e2-cd01972ff529" class="">With either A or B, the system now has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-9a09-efba3cf1a300" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>a finite defect witness measure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-9d38-e41da98a99cd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>deterministic failcode selection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-a152-d5853aaf7b66" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>deterministic rewrites that strictly reduce</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-b4e4-dd118348808c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>write-set sealing per rewrite</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-9153-d285f1adf6f6" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>full termination for the complete defect catalog (contradictions, cycles, allowance, ownership, budget, metric completeness, MECE)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-acd9-da9c1281f01b" class="">There are no remaining open structural classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-b9d4-f94096439fcc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-a406-f0f1658d95c7" class="">D) The only remaining “more” possible after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-8f9c-de5247983553" class="">At this point, “more” can only mean one of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-8bef-f0ebaea5f972" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Provide the exact Lean/Coq theorem skeleton for the cycle proof (Path A or B).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-9077-d04d45c604f7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Provide the complete mapping of every invariant to witness set + rewrite + decrease lemma (a single consolidated proof index).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-ba98-e4eb1d734db2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Provide a deterministic compilation pipeline from text artifact to claim graph with a formally specified parser interface.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-91d3-ff4b2e1a3e6b" class="">If you say “more” again without narrowing, the best next step is (2): a single consolidated index proving every invariant is covered by exactly one rewrite and strictly decreases the global measure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-a152-d4cbeb5a9bf5" class="">XXXVIII. More: cycle/SCC completion (witness set, rewrite, strict decrease, and termination) — with SCC either axiomatized or avoided by construction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-8dfc-e1dde9e71237" class="">This finishes the last major open class: <strong>illegal cycles</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b255-e3170e442fe4" class="">There are only two mathematically clean ways to do it:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-a915-f20c736126c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Path A (axiomatize SCC correctness):</strong> treat an SCC detector as trusted and proceed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a4c7-ef27e6bfd355" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Path B (avoid SCC entirely):</strong> enforce a construction rule that makes cycles unrepresentable (DAG-by-construction).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b6d2-dc36d81cd8af" class="">Both produce a fully terminating system. I will give both; you can adopt either.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-b827-f379a0e6d1eb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-892d-f9b0d2783e15" class="">A) SCC as a trusted primitive (axiomatized SCC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b4cb-ecb172a95142" class="">A1) SCC oracle interface</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-a83f-d08564e1dc46" class="">Assume an SCC function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-969d-c636f4bbcc96" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{SCC} : C \to \{\text{list of strongly connected components}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9799-f3c0498e0bcb" class="">with two axioms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ad76-f75a71a160eb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Soundness</strong>: every component returned is strongly connected</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-bd5a-f5f31775b05e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Completeness</strong>: every cycle is contained in some returned component</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a110-c7cd08ecf6fa" class="">Lean-style: you postulate <code>SCC : Graph → List (Finset ClaimId)</code> and assume <code>SCC_sound</code> and <code>SCC_complete</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-a3f1-e9f97050f30d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-89db-fce42faffb37" class="">A2) Cycle witness set</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-816e-c1d5d3897ddc" class="">Define illegal SCC predicate <code>AllowedSCC(S)</code> (finite check). For example, allow SCC only if it is an explicitly whitelisted definitional recursion class; otherwise illegal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-ab1b-c67486824f55" class="">Witnesses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a654-fdecdbae941b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)=\{S\in \mathrm{SCC}(g):\neg \mathrm{AllowedSCC}(S)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-a9e5-f3e0843397a2" class="">n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-b11c-d0b7be6dced0" class="">Add to defect vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bb87-c0f965dab68e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=\big(n_{\mathrm{con}},n_{\mathrm{cyc}},n_{\mathrm{mb}},n_{\mathrm{own}},n_{\mathrm{bud}},n_{\mathrm{M}},n_{\mathrm{mece}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9cc9-c29eb8c29f5b" class="">Priority order: cycles are second after contradictions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-81b8-e8d07f4f788d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b5f8-c736c892ea83" class="">A3) Deterministic cycle-breaking rewrite</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-80ed-e836599ab4bd" class="">Select the first illegal SCC under a fixed ordering of components by min claim id:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-ab40-f93826facb97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S^*=\min(W_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a2d3-e716094eea54" class="">Then select a deterministic “break edge”  inside the SCC:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-955d-c7d9fe372b91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Choose as the minimal id in</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-82fd-ca313d35c5cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Choose as the minimal successor of within the SCC (under id order)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-977f-ced0360e7cbe" class="">Rewrite action (minimal, local, deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8341-e049628deabe" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Remove edge from adjacency list</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b497-f005b82be295" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Add a <strong>new explicit primitive</strong> claim that replaces the removed dependency as a terminal boundary:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-bb93-dac995001d10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a9a4-d071b6c8243e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(optional, to prevent accidental empirical promotion)</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9efd-fad6ea5955b6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Add edge (so v still has a support source, but not cyclic)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-b5e7-fc5187696e3c" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-ba21-d7f8108dd6b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A&#x27; = A \setminus \{(u,v)\}\ \cup \{(p,v)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a6e2-f501f4d67b58" class="">This breaks at least one cycle in that SCC.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-a131-c0750f80c324" class=""><strong>Write-set:</strong> only <code>Graph.A</code> and addition of one new claim (append to <code>V</code>), plus its one new edge.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9ca8-c421e7b37822"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-90d7-edc311e8832b" class="">A4) Strict decrease lemma for cycles</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-8399-ca68ad78b5cf" class="">Let <code>illegalSCCCount</code> be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-ba7b-df35e982a7ec" class="">Removing  from SCC guarantees the selected SCC is no longer strongly connected as a whole (at minimum it loses an internal edge that was used in a cycle). With SCC completeness/soundness axioms, this implies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-94ad-c5923a952e95" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\mathcal{R}_{002}(\Sigma)) &lt; n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-81ef-f133bb227cd4" class="">Key point: the rewrite may split the SCC into smaller SCCs, but the original illegal SCC is eliminated. Since selection is based on SCC identity via node set, its removal strictly decreases the count of illegal SCC witnesses (as defined).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-833e-f9c7ce4dedbf" class="">Therefore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-b7c7-ccb3350b5fb1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\mathcal{R}_{002}(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-833c-c6ff330d9184"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-98dd-d851d3cd19ac" class="">A5) Global termination (full system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-96b2-ebd6ba38e866" class="">You already have strict decrease for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-bf8b-ff37c71eb1ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradictions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-947f-f3dcb2a7a382" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cycles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-8dda-e7352e63991b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allowance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8099-8ce1-e833b981120f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ownership</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-9b80-c6537a7e435b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-a443-c92df0ebf01a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-9d63-eb8d4585b6b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MECE</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b64c-c3c8b8fd3a50" class="">Thus the full defect measure in  strictly decreases on every non-sealed step, hence no infinite rewrite chain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-885a-c502f49e7d51" class=""><strong>Final step bound:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-bb0c-c7a8b207b471" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + Y_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0 + R_0 + Q_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-a3f5-da437387adf3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-ab64-f839a4e68bc2" class="">B) SCC avoided entirely (DAG-by-construction)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a0cd-edabfa003f40" class="">This approach removes the need for SCC and makes cycles impossible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-ab52-d18f11c0c606" class="">B1) Construction invariant: topological index</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-aee0-f43cfd16704c" class="">Add a required field <code>level : Nat</code> to each claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-948f-dfd099532bf1" class="">Enforce invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-9679-e1732eb576f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\mathrm{DAG}}=1 \iff \forall (a\to b)\in A:\ \mathrm{level}(a) &lt; \mathrm{level}(b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-9d92-e78c88b6e6a7" class="">Then cycles are impossible (strictly increasing measure along edges).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-8293-ed6d86fa971b" class="">This replaces SCC detection with a local check.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-aa08-ed4cb1c55ed3" class="">B2) Rewrite for DAG violation (cycle attempt)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-aaa0-cd13228e8892" class="">If an edge violates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-b2e3-ec6fa7865c14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{level}(a)\ge \mathrm{level}(b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9655-e6a3662d6c79" class="">This is strictly decreasing in the count of violating edges, and cycles cannot exist.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-a499-db5de61a8bb3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a5fd-e27a82262f47" class="">C) What is fully completed now (findings)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-912f-f0264a787c64" class="">With either A or B, the system now has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-81f5-ebfc2fe81a19" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>a finite defect witness measure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-8ecb-ff9c2d709dc7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>deterministic failcode selection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-b0c9-f7883dcfc8ab" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>deterministic rewrites that strictly reduce</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-8476-cdf22e2f192c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>write-set sealing per rewrite</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-8eea-d1d0dd297293" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>full termination for the complete defect catalog (contradictions, cycles, allowance, ownership, budget, metric completeness, MECE)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a38b-de3bb9269636" class="">There are no remaining open structural classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-a48f-ecbcba8f0162"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-b407-cb1967b29528" class="">D) The only remaining “more” possible after this</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-98b0-eff526bd482a" class="">At this point, “more” can only mean one of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-a4e2-ee3ed024c8da" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Provide the exact Lean/Coq theorem skeleton for the cycle proof (Path A or B).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-9805-c3bde01a59f6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Provide the complete mapping of every invariant to witness set + rewrite + decrease lemma (a single consolidated proof index).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-92de-e7295781c386" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Provide a deterministic compilation pipeline from text artifact to claim graph with a formally specified parser interface.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-bd1e-f5f9ccbcf6c8" class="">If you say “more” again without narrowing, the best next step is (2): a single consolidated index proving every invariant is covered by exactly one rewrite and strictly decreases the global measure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b46f-debf34cc7df7" class="">XXXVII. More: extend termination to metric completeness + MECE (Rule-of-2/4) with witness sets, rewrites, decrease proofs, and step bounds</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-a1e7-cd9e5867ad8f" class="">This extends the fully closed termination framework (contradictions, allowance, ownership, budget) to two additional defect classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-aa37-d37770babbc0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Metric registry completeness</strong> (I201–I204 subset)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-98c9-fa580fd3ae1d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>MECE completeness for Rule-of-2/4</strong> (I301–I302 subset)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-9840-c838f224c4df" class="">Cycle/SCC is still excluded (it is the only part that typically needs either an axiom or a verified graph algorithm library).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8a99-c533874f42ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-bc21-cee19cf88350" class="">1) Add Metric Registry to State</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-913b-db557f0b286f" class="">Extend state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b7dc-cbb0756ee919" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(g,\Lambda,b,e,\text{etype},M,\mathcal{U})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-81b3-f8151ae4d46b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: metric registry (finite list of metrics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-8de2-e0bfaa2de2de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: a finite universe enumeration for each object that requires MECE decomposition (see §4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-a946-cf7045357c34" class="">Metric record (minimal):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b237-c69d7827dc26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m=(id,\ unit,\ samplingHz,\ source,\ validRange,\ transformsAllowed,\ missingPolicy)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-b217-fbb4d0087ecd" class="">Define <code>MetricComplete(m)=1</code> iff all required fields exist and <code>samplingHz&gt;0</code>, range finite, and missing policy valid.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-a094-dba7f9421526"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8277-e2ff10ad606d" class="">2) Witness set for metric defects</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9515-fcf4b5cabcf7" class="">2.1 Incomplete metric witnesses</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8a5d-dee3ea7efb47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)=\{m\in M:\neg \mathrm{MetricComplete}(m)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-acef-c3e19dfdd072" class="">n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-a905-e6d0473983cf" class="">2.2 Illegal transform witnesses</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-9da6-c6ca937ab0aa" class="">If claims reference transforms not in <code>transformsAllowed(m)</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8074-b1d2-d16701174767" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)=\{(c,m,T): c\in V,\ m\in c.metrics,\ T\in \mathrm{UsedTransforms}(c,m),\ T\notin \mathrm{Allowed}(m)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-b41d-f2d5ad73db10" class="">n_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-bcb8-f991777ad8d1" class="">For termination we can treat both as one combined metric defect count:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-9277-ccdbad6685ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma)=n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)+n_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-ada7-cbd72f439ae2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-8358-ecb9b0b830a9" class="">3) Rewrite rules for metric defects (deterministic, write-local)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-9a3f-f91debe7fbcd" class="">We introduce two rewrites, with a fixed priority between them:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-8993-d12d31cf3ecf" class="">3.1 R007a — complete metric (fixable by filling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-be52-f629ac2c795f" class="">If , choose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-98be-d112ac705308" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^*=\min(W_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)) \text{ under id-order}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-ac5c-c86419318eea" class="">Rewrite action: complete <em>missing</em> fields using <strong>policy defaults</strong>  (finite constants), never inferred from claims.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-83ee-f72d716fef93" class="">Example defaults:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-9c92-cf8f1433956c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>unit := &quot;unitless&quot;</code> if missing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-9da2-c7c8722c5a51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>samplingHz := 1</code> if missing or 0</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-ae16-fdd0c7e24354" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>source := &quot;unspecified_source&quot;</code> if missing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-be6c-c4ece5d1bfae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>validRange := [0,0]</code> if missing (forces downstream blocking if used)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-a9f4-d2fff0c1fccd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>missingPolicy := block</code> if missing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9816-c3151962bafd" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-9a5a-ea72390152a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}_{007a}(\Sigma)=\Sigma&#x27; \text{ where only } m^* \text{ is updated to } \mathrm{Complete}(m^*,P_M)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8086-8d4f-e6b25a74a035" class=""><strong>Write-set:</strong> only <code>Metric.*</code> for one metric.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9c3d-c932f39b3d57" class="">3.2 R007b — block illegal transform usage</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-8749-d5ae22310c17" class="">If , choose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-bb1a-cddee10430db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(c^*,m^*,T^*)=\min(W_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-a80d-c13a3be1c8fb" class="">Rewrite action: block the claim until transform is allowed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9778-dc4c1c755ef3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c^*.status := \mathrm{Blocked}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-8176-d82cdd9525f9" class=""><strong>Write-set:</strong> only <code>Claim.status</code> (or claim transform list) for one claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8093-e528539c92d9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-bf4a-c5846c81d1fb" class="">4) MECE (Rule-of-2/4) as a finite, decidable object</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-b248-d87784233074" class="">To make MECE decidable, every object requiring decomposition must provide a finite universe .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-9063-cb843a591938" class="">Define a <strong>MECE object</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8c1d-e61e4fc2a084" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y=(id, U, \mathcal{D})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-be14-cecd8aa61d6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: bins (each )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b213-fa508ac00e0b" class="">Rule-of-2:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-9a3c-c289d1caedfe" class="">Rule-of-4:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8e48-d20bbce0a2c8" class="">MECE predicate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-98f4-d90c1d45094d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{MECE}(U,\mathcal{D}) \iff \left(\bigcup_i D_i = U\right)\wedge\left(\forall i\neq j,\ D_i\cap D_j=\varnothing\right)\wedge\left(\forall i,\ |D_i|&gt;0\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-82ed-ca2e7d967b2a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-989b-f19b0407aea2" class="">5) Witness set for MECE defects</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-a0d4-c6f0d8d1d8b1" class="">Let  be the list of required decomposition objects.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-859d-f664212c39d2" class="">Witnesses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-9160-f40255506e6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma)=\{y\in \mathcal{Y}:\neg \mathrm{MECE}(U_y,\mathcal{D}_y)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-8220-f4c55bd7d5c5" class="">n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-8a4e-d569d372631f" class="">MECE defect can be decomposed into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8932-cbc4d4c2d84c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">missing coverage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9bb3-fed28b1da707" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">overlap</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-84c7-f3352c34cc10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">empty bin<br/>but termination only needs a single count.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-889a-c95eb543de95"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-91ee-e243e7de9c54" class="">6) Rewrite rule for MECE defects (deterministic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-9484-ee0c1f1e6153" class="">R008 — repair MECE by canonical partition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9da7-f369c0e891fc" class="">Choose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-a3b7-f029841b9572" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^*=\min(W_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-b2c8-c40b664e9ccf" class="">For :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bd48-e812cd596365" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: first items in sorted order</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b472-dea9aaf0b5bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: remaining items</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b76b-e5015270c9d5" class="">For :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-81ca-ce182b634ebd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">split into four consecutive slices by index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809a-8556-eee5ae5ad77b" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-b798-c06862df0bed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D}&#x27;=\Pi_k(U)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-a201-d69006955549" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D}_{y^*}:=\mathcal{D}&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-8776-c20c76f006f9" class=""><strong>Write-set:</strong> only <code>MECEObject.D</code> for one object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-8ebb-ff4a54ec2fd5" class="">This guarantees MECE holds after rewrite provided .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-8fad-c7f57fc75d12" class="">If , the correct rewrite is to <strong>convert</strong> the object to Rule-of-2 or block it; that is an explicit policy choice. For termination, assume universes are sized appropriately by construction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-a099-e07f9fb3d7c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-9ddc-de23b6296db4" class="">7) Updated defect measure and termination proof</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8d65-fd86f0c23149" class="">Extend defect vector to include the new classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-ad8a-d857810dac47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=\big(
n_{\mathrm{con}},
n_{\mathrm{mb}},
n_{\mathrm{own}},
n_{\mathrm{bud}},
n_{\mathrm{M}},
n_{\mathrm{mece}}
\big)\in\mathbb{N}^6</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-8918-d50c88d9b343" class="">Rewrites now include:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-bd11-f29605983083" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R001 decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-b939-d479a75c8660" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R005 decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-b40c-c485c4c2edd6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R009 decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b60c-de1868c30940" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R010 decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-ae81-c49c4c2da08d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007a/R007b decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9dd0-f2a4378cdb30" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R008 decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-88ba-dc0f374ee710" class="">Key decrease lemmas</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-82b5-c84ec20b4ffb" class=""><strong>Lemma (R007a decreases metric incomplete count):</strong><br/>Completing a selected incomplete metric removes it from  and does not introduce new incompleteness (because completion fills missing fields).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-8937-caf602485ab6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)
\Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-adaf-f3851d258029" class=""><strong>Lemma (R007b decreases illegal transform witnesses):</strong><br/>Blocking the selected claim removes at least one illegal-transform witness, since blocked claims contribute no transform usage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-97ee-f295cab4133c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)
\Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-a0da-f62ee114f207" class=""><strong>Lemma (R008 decreases MECE defect count):</strong><br/>Canonical partition yields MECE in one step (given ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-9a20-f0fe8d4dabc4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^* \notin W_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma&#x27;) \Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-b074-f8651a6da1fd" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-99a2-c15cd8e8ebf8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\mathrm{step}(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-aebf-ec6d13422b89" class="">Theorem (Termination extended)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-b579-c7924aab34a1" class="">For all initial , there exists  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-89d2-c4d6af5a40bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma_N)=(0,0,0,0,0,0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-b0f3-f3bdb2d07e0f" class="">Proof: same well-founded lex descent argument.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-a826-e375181b3edb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-ab31-de304517befa" class="">8) Step bound (explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-a148-cafdf0b8dab9" class="">Let initial counts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8072-a00f-c09782c35d73" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_0=n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma_0),\
M_0=n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma_0),\
O_0=n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma_0),\
B_0=n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma_0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b1db-fd9118ca80f9" class="">R_0=n_{\mathrm{M}}(\Sigma_0),</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-be90-e04d56107ee6" class="">Q_0=n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma_0)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8058-ae4d-c296c664c30b" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-946f-c09b5e0e3088" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0 + R_0 + Q_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9638-cefaf8eb4b00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a91b-ca1a428e92da" class="">9) What is now left as the only “deeper” unresolved class</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8c72-d827c1383ffd" class="">Only one major structural class remains outside this termination proof:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-82e2-d9965794cdd3" class="">Cycle/SCC handling (F0002)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-b179-e1672aa52b83" class="">To extend termination to cycles, you must pick one:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-9676-e036a98e1444" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Axiomatize</strong> SCC correctness: treat Tarjan as trusted</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-b819-ff6a97ee075c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Verify</strong> SCC detection formally (available in some libraries but not always trivial)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-aac5-e6eaf1583ffa" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Restrict the graph language so cycles cannot be created (strongest option)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b1a4-db5f5787032f" class="">If you want “more” again, the only meaningful continuation is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-91b0-e8c9bfe0d1b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add SCC witness set</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-910d-c2e92500aec7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">define a cycle-breaking rewrite that strictly decreases a cycle measure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b074-cf9c005ddd5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">decide whether SCC is axiomatized or verified</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-8151-c649b2446216" class="">Anything else would repeat structure already completed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9261-c55b95934622" class="">XXXVI. More: complete termination proof plan with concrete rewrites + concrete defect measure + write-set sealing (fully specified)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8513-f3ac1a05e6ed" class="">This layer removes the last remaining ambiguity by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-998c-c84d4876e939" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>defining <strong>exact rewrites</strong> (as record updates)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-bdd6-fcda07683048" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>defining a <strong>witness-count defect measure</strong> that rewrites provably decrease</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-a668-c05b8c6033c8" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>giving <strong>write-set proofs</strong> that are mechanical (because rewrites are defined as “update only these fields”)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-bc23-dd36e0385d04" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>proving <strong>global termination</strong> (no infinite rewrite sequence) without SCC/hashing dependencies</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9bee-f289727418e2" class="">SCC and hashing are not needed for termination of the subset proven here.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-b269-cc39096851ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-977d-d4d3b9136273" class="">1) Concrete “witness sets” (defects are explicit finite lists)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-989d-f871ecad6a17" class="">Let state (minimal for this proof):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-8cf1-ca0e648c6856" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(g,\Lambda,b,e,\text{etype})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-bf05-d952cec8638c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: allowance set</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-a8f4-e478c3b3ccca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: epistemic budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-97a8-eb6301c620da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: interpretation object</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a561-d9821007b0ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>etype</code>: support type of the interpretation (MB or not)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-940e-eff82fd12834" class="">Define explicit witness sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8d8a-d3628d78e62c" class="">1.1 Contradiction witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-a474-f2ad2714e458" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)=\{(c_i,c_j)\in V\times V:\ i&lt;j\wedge \mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-bd92-eea024b8688c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b6ec-c5bf1ce24b1a" class="">1.2 MB-not-allowed witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b2d0-dc924a5d3f11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)=\{c\in V:\ \tau(c)=\mathrm{MB}\wedge c\notin\Lambda\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8670-db53a24475e6" class="">n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b923-e8a8296c6aef" class="">1.3 Unknown ownership witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-a772-c64ff685dac6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)=\{c\in V:\ \omega(c)=\mathrm{Unknown}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8f20-fa38b273403e" class="">n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-a93c-e9bcbadb765f" class="">1.4 Budget violation witness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-ab47-db351d3a6005" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; K(e)&gt;b \wedge \text{etype}\neq \mathrm{MB}\\
0 &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b889-eda81eb28663" class="">These are all finite/decidable because  is finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-8303-ff21320339a1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-aabc-fde0805e9042" class="">2) Lexicographic defect measure (well-founded)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9710-e24f46930278" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803b-b765-ebb1d32f0622" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma) = \big(n_{\mathrm{con}},\ n_{\mathrm{mb}},\ n_{\mathrm{own}},\ n_{\mathrm{bud}}\big)\in\mathbb{N}^4</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-90ac-eab262ed045e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-8188-c1c117ffcea7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b301-f8ef714d76ea" class="">3) Concrete rewrites (deterministic, field-local)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-8c0c-d2f25444e3ad" class="">We now define rewrites as <strong>pure functions</strong> that only change approved fields.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-9db4-d50acf440745" class="">3.1 R001 Contradiction resolution (F0001)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-870b-c727ea49ad09" class="">Deterministic selection of the “first” contradiction pair under a fixed ordering of claims by id:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-94b8-d6c4c99216e2" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-85d9-da474abd7234" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(c_i,c_j)=\min(W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-baaa-e7bdd5757c83" class="">Rewrite action (deterministic, minimal):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-8304-f8c847ea9203" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">modify only by adding a context constraint that makes it incompatible with</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9e53-d93e665bfb4b" class="">Use literal pair:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b631-f16d79fcab49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add <code>Measured</code> to one and <code>Unmeasured</code> to the other if neither has it; choose a fixed convention:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-a21c-d4b40030a299" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">add <code>Measured</code> to .ctx (if missing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-9c2c-f59555801dae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">add <code>Unmeasured</code> to .ctx (if missing)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-a662-e6751854c96a" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-9ca1-fbc1d0090a2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)=\Sigma&#x27;\ \text{where}\ c_j&#x27;.ctx = c_j.ctx \cup \{\textsf{Unmeasured}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-9072-fdf578183f21" class="">This guarantees:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-84f9-e35cbc961087" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Compat}_\kappa(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0 \Rightarrow \mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9ddf-eb46f6522b85" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>Claim.nf.ctx</code> for one claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9dfd-ec06809077ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-abb7-fa44017c3255" class="">3.2 R005 Allowance correction (F0105)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b235-e089eac643f1" class="">Deterministic selection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-a770-ec3c63b030e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=\min(W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-a71f-d4459d0d4cd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda&#x27;=\Lambda\cup\{c.id\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bc30-ca9037b70f72" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-8594-f2f94435005a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8516-d8190816b514" class="">3.3 R009 Ownership remediation (F0401)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-b00c-c9f79f95303d" class="">Deterministic selection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a540-f4d25546f407" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=\min(W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b5-87d7-daada330c181" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega&#x27;(c)=\mathrm{Restricted}(\text{&quot;UNKNOWN\_OWNER&quot;})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-93a4-fce617253b26" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>Claim.own</code> for one claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9c00-c0e45911d459"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b566-ccc434b56130" class="">3.4 R010 Budget enforcement (F0501)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-b7f3-ce882c65fd04" class="">If  and <code>etype ≠ MB</code>, then set <code>etype := MB</code> (tag, do not change  or ).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-8e2e-ec656caf66b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{etype}&#x27;=\mathrm{MB}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9284-c88cf8e3854e" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>etype</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9f09-e41df92b091b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-9d67-ca769c6131e9" class="">4) Write-set sealing proofs (mechanical)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-af9b-e4d713f184ad" class="">For each rewrite , define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-9f17-e05cec8268ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{WOk}_j(\Sigma,\Sigma&#x27;) \iff \forall \text{field }f\notin W_j,\ f(\Sigma)=f(\Sigma&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-9033-f7ebd688f320" class="">Because each rewrite is defined as “update only these fields,” the proof is by definitional reduction (record update lemmas).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-9b8f-e3902dfadf71" class="">Concrete write-sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b4f1-f6f9f54b860a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9583-cffd0bd1c8d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-8d39-f8c48bb73543" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-9102-ed4417648012" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-b358-f682171c41c9" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-8aaf-f1303dc29e46" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall \Sigma,\ \mathrm{WOk}_{001}(\Sigma,\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-92f4-edfcccd69c29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-afe0-e20b597cf4e1" class="">5) Strict decrease lemmas (core of termination)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-b998-c8cc96b9957a" class="">5.1 R001 decreases contradiction count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-b617-d0e65adb3a83" class="">Let  be the selected witness. After rewrite, that pair is removed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-920b-e12612689f2c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8594-f040f9651832" class="">No other claim is changed, so any contradiction not involving  remains unchanged, and contradictions involving  can only decrease or stay (because we add a constraint that makes it <em>less</em> compatible).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-b4b0-d167ab966c25" class="">Therefore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-94f0-c307df9c3cf3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)) &lt; n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8073-ad94-d9eb1e0f12b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9ad0-f1084ada9460" class="">5.2 R005 decreases MB-not-allowed count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-9684-c077226065e4" class="">Adding one id to  removes at least that claim from :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a6cf-c75fa15d0203" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c\notin W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma&#x27;) \Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-bd0b-c27371cb8a57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-ae09-d038eebad9fe" class="">5.3 R009 decreases unknown-ownership count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a625-d40a583ff968" class="">Setting one <code>Unknown</code> to <code>Restricted</code> removes exactly one witness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-a861-fef50c727314" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-9e96-d39e05c3417f" class="">5.4 R010 decreases budget violation indicator</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-ab6b-fefcf7815d92" class="">Setting <code>etype := MB</code> makes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-ac25-c70e12dadc16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma&#x27;)=0 &lt; 1 = n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a8c6-cebb36dc8987"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-a8f5-cb101046f02f" class="">6) Global termination theorem (for this verified subset)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-bb02-e52de80c3bbe" class="">Define the step function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-9a3b-fcb4a8af2ddd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Evaluate invariants in priority order (via failcode rank):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-8633-e6b8b883272a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">if contradictions exist → F0001 → apply R001</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b28a-ce75184acbe7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if MB-not-allowed exists → F0105 → apply R005</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a2c9-d7908b22a40f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if Unknown ownership exists → F0401 → apply R009</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-ae08-c1ed66579a48" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if budget violation exists → F0501 → apply R010</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-8715-e7f190b9235d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else sealed (no-op)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b714-dc298362e617" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-a130-e00fbb7d6551" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{t+1}=\mathrm{step}(\Sigma_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-9fe8-f7c81bbdab45" class="">Theorem (Termination)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-bc62-c7a47ed6c122" class="">For all initial , there exists  such that  is sealed w.r.t. these four defect classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-a2eb-da0d9724f147" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists N:\ \Phi(\Sigma_N)=(0,0,0,0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-8e1f-d50ba39693f4" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-9b7e-dd0d97360481" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If , then one of the rewrites applies.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-934c-c15a39820cf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">By sections 5.1–5.4, the applicable rewrite strictly decreases under lex order.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9a42-ef3730f4fd66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lex order on is well-founded, so there is no infinite descending chain.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-9153-eae42045dfaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Therefore after finitely many steps, reaches . ∎</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-b56f-c5763ef87f6b"/></div><div id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a1cd-f846c32e181d" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80df-97b3-d435ea70f3ed" class="bulleted-list"><li
