style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a53c-cf2bee4f72d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-9566-f31ef3f056f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-abf7-d4451e956e82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b01a-eaf27f5f3976" class="">Then a crude upper bound:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-a0fd-e7ffe91ecc54" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-83b0-e0c9e921115c" class="">Because each rewrite reduces its primary witness count by at least 1, and earlier components must hit 0 before later components can block.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-ac06-fb5677f3b43a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-bb90-c138d36cf7a3" class="">8) What is now fully “closed” (no open definitions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-bf7c-dc3fd3610bc5" class="">This layer fully closes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a96a-dde3d13e761c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradiction grammar (finite, decidable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-a17f-d7c4e23982b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant evaluation (finite, decidable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a152-e72f19048815" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic failcode selection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9cf7-f6f6918ec390" class="bulleted-list"><li needed for these four classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-a3da-c6e6735f9c1d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-b057-d4c9adda0de7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a write-set proof</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-874b-c1c1a0f5cd7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a decrease lemma</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-a4be-eacea20be314" class="">The next natural extension set is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-8a24-c18f42824249" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>cycle/SCC handling (needs either axiomatized SCC correctness or verified Tarjan)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-af1a-dba3393049c1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>metric registry completeness (witness: incomplete metrics; rewrite: fill or block)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-b677-c5b162898203" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>rule-of-2/4 MECE witnesses (witness: missing bins/overlap; rewrite: emit decomposition objects)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-9771-d5ad5683b958" class="">If you say “more” again, the most direct continuation is to add <strong>metric completeness</strong> and prove the same style of decrease + termination for it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-bb09-e7b2644a184b" class="">XXXVI. More: complete termination proof plan with concrete rewrites + concrete defect measure + write-set sealing (fully specified)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a9e2-c0f8425b1ab9" class="">This layer removes the last remaining ambiguity by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8936-ff53d69aaa3c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>defining <strong>exact rewrites</strong> (as record updates)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-898c-ecadb870a6ec" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>defining a <strong>witness-count defect measure</strong> that rewrites provably decrease</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-8d56-e06f22563bd9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>giving <strong>write-set proofs</strong> that are mechanical (because rewrites are defined as “update only these fields”)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-b187-d594c259ac48" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>proving <strong>global termination</strong> (no infinite rewrite sequence) without SCC/hashing dependencies</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8dec-ec05f37ff66a" class="">SCC and hashing are not needed for termination of the subset proven here.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-b512-fc63710cf92c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-b2b2-d991473e3f7d" class="">1) Concrete “witness sets” (defects are explicit finite lists)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-8013-fa98848aa036" class="">Let state (minimal for this proof):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b5b9-e0fda30b1067" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(g,\Lambda,b,e,\text{etype})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a459-e3490b716aa3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: allowance set</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b268-fe274935c6f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: epistemic budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80da-a74a-ea2a40a6ddfc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: interpretation object</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-9e2c-c86bdd8df6c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>etype</code>: support type of the interpretation (MB or not)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-9e9c-cfc3f5ea6c8d" class="">Define explicit witness sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-921e-c8c87c8831a2" class="">1.1 Contradiction witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b59e-c9ea6d7716a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)=\{(c_i,c_j)\in V\times V:\ i&lt;j\wedge \mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j)=1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bb-97ff-f7a7a2aab990" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-91f3-e6bc54a00fd9" class="">1.2 MB-not-allowed witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-8fa5-d2fa1a40cbd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)=\{c\in V:\ \tau(c)=\mathrm{MB}\wedge c\notin\Lambda\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9137-efa982cc0802" class="">n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-995f-eddd726c594b" class="">1.3 Unknown ownership witnesses</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-bd54-ff40ff982f3d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)=\{c\in V:\ \omega(c)=\mathrm{Unknown}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-ac23-fef207069759" class="">n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)=|W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a6fc-d6ff4264db02" class="">1.4 Budget violation witness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-a7ca-e6ae60af1f6b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma)=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; K(e)&gt;b \wedge \text{etype}\neq \mathrm{MB}\\
0 &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-b1bb-cc839e3a6742" class="">These are all finite/decidable because  is finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9761-dcde515a9998"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a9e4-e83bc01dbcee" class="">2) Lexicographic defect measure (well-founded)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-98c6-ea4c4a1d45f8" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806c-8161-eaec57cc9a9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma) = \big(n_{\mathrm{con}},\ n_{\mathrm{mb}},\ n_{\mathrm{own}},\ n_{\mathrm{bud}}\big)\in\mathbb{N}^4</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-8ab4-c5bfbed026b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-9ef5-e37edabc301c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-a2a1-e1fa80a9d65f" class="">3) Concrete rewrites (deterministic, field-local)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-a4cf-ffaf1792a6d0" class="">We now define rewrites as <strong>pure functions</strong> that only change approved fields.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-b6dc-d30e7976ffa7" class="">3.1 R001 Contradiction resolution (F0001)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-8122-c833cfe01ec5" class="">Deterministic selection of the “first” contradiction pair under a fixed ordering of claims by id:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-bd1b-df6d9af5d528" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-9c4b-e69fb8a094bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(c_i,c_j)=\min(W_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b158-c68db473ba54" class="">Rewrite action (deterministic, minimal):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b9a6-caab068c382e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">modify only by adding a context constraint that makes it incompatible with</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9482-c80ed7fe82bd" class="">Use literal pair:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8050-9837-e8fa20019d3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add <code>Measured</code> to one and <code>Unmeasured</code> to the other if neither has it; choose a fixed convention:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-b334-ccda9aa18a73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">add <code>Measured</code> to .ctx (if missing)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8022-919e-d07dbc9ecece" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">add <code>Unmeasured</code> to .ctx (if missing)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-8b73-f72694da7a2d" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-a786-d17a86097999" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)=\Sigma&#x27;\ \text{where}\ c_j&#x27;.ctx = c_j.ctx \cup \{\textsf{Unmeasured}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-babb-dfd584e31f8d" class="">This guarantees:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-9246-da004f052ad4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Compat}_\kappa(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0 \Rightarrow \mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8270-cf7270b8bb8f" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>Claim.nf.ctx</code> for one claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-9967-f9c39d730c2b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-85bf-f913cfbf50b9" class="">3.2 R005 Allowance correction (F0105)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9845-fec9a3256e48" class="">Deterministic selection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-9179-cd1017095fc7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=\min(W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-aa51-eff719feeb15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda&#x27;=\Lambda\cup\{c.id\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-a05e-f65dff0ef6a4" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b3ab-e4821c03f703"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8448-ed3e7f8be848" class="">3.3 R009 Ownership remediation (F0401)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-9d80-d63faf074ef7" class="">Deterministic selection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b3c9-c038f98c2ed5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=\min(W_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-a67a-e27cc6920d1e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega&#x27;(c)=\mathrm{Restricted}(\text{&quot;UNKNOWN\_OWNER&quot;})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8053-a067-c6dda2f70efe" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>Claim.own</code> for one claim.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8049-a6f3-fb61111c67d3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-abf9-ee92c8e77549" class="">3.4 R010 Budget enforcement (F0501)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9dd8-e419761407ac" class="">If  and <code>etype ≠ MB</code>, then set <code>etype := MB</code> (tag, do not change  or ).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bbaf-d73f43b199e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{etype}&#x27;=\mathrm{MB}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-b24e-c244f88e2460" class=""><strong>Write-set</strong>: only <code>etype</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8044-83c4-c29d454b8e9d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b073-fcc616934a1a" class="">4) Write-set sealing proofs (mechanical)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a22b-f1934a4cb14f" class="">For each rewrite , define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-9bf7-c8ce014f8acf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{WOk}_j(\Sigma,\Sigma&#x27;) \iff \forall \text{field }f\notin W_j,\ f(\Sigma)=f(\Sigma&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-b58b-c116d35d1ab4" class="">Because each rewrite is defined as “update only these fields,” the proof is by definitional reduction (record update lemmas).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80be-ad7c-dcc2f91ec551" class="">Concrete write-sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b4b0-efa4fdc8c840" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-9cb8-d7cf52380198" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-9d29-f4f6022be9c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8e21-d3165ddd1c21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b741-ccd9237cb059" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-a6e4-d85650716346" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall \Sigma,\ \mathrm{WOk}_{001}(\Sigma,\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-923e-ceb639faa411"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-bd00-c5b7c07e5f24" class="">5) Strict decrease lemmas (core of termination)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-8033-e6288bf030ef" class="">5.1 R001 decreases contradiction count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-916f-c7de93420423" class="">Let  be the selected witness. After rewrite, that pair is removed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-9066-d8bcd6e36ac6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Con}(c_i,c_j&#x27;)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-93dc-c3cfa89c4694" class="">No other claim is changed, so any contradiction not involving  remains unchanged, and contradictions involving  can only decrease or stay (because we add a constraint that makes it <em>less</em> compatible).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a13a-cb596023cad7" class="">Therefore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a8ae-c18506ffbf6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)) &lt; n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-91fc-ebd233b9a0d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\mathcal{R}_{001}(\Sigma)) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-a75a-c9f31dce806f" class="">5.2 R005 decreases MB-not-allowed count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-b543-d3dfe74932fd" class="">Adding one id to  removes at least that claim from :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-9049-f487933c2483" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c\notin W_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma&#x27;) \Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9ab9-d10bd6c3691c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-9367-d18057df5ff5" class="">5.3 R009 decreases unknown-ownership count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-a5bd-c8f5ce6c7189" class="">Setting one <code>Unknown</code> to <code>Restricted</code> removes exactly one witness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-a23d-c5fc8f80e813" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-84c9-d39d18fcfbdc" class="">5.4 R010 decreases budget violation indicator</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-baab-f16708aee692" class="">Setting <code>etype := MB</code> makes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-aa13-fecc99233845" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma&#x27;)=0 &lt; 1 = n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-9e40-d60ce39f2703"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ab02-fb9f72db251a" class="">6) Global termination theorem (for this verified subset)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-9302-d97a0087f347" class="">Define the step function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-994f-f695501eba98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Evaluate invariants in priority order (via failcode rank):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-96e9-ea05180b9478" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">if contradictions exist → F0001 → apply R001</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-984e-ea76507a43af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if MB-not-allowed exists → F0105 → apply R005</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cc-93ef-ef2176e49f12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if Unknown ownership exists → F0401 → apply R009</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-8e7a-c898e7c631a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else if budget violation exists → F0501 → apply R010</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807c-ab34-fec33379f84c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">else sealed (no-op)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8007-938d-c9767eb75e3b" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803c-86a2-d618f5ce44d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{t+1}=\mathrm{step}(\Sigma_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-b73e-c1dfc7507bee" class="">Theorem (Termination)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-9435-de1b6c6f4e03" class="">For all initial , there exists  such that  is sealed w.r.t. these four defect classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-be7a-d5ae44d3feda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists N:\ \Phi(\Sigma_N)=(0,0,0,0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ca-be8a-ed9c12cd2fd2" class=""><strong>Proof.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-afcd-ca7378d6e5f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If , then one of the rewrites applies.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-a0df-c27713af83a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">By sections 5.1–5.4, the applicable rewrite strictly decreases under lex order.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-866f-ef0b37e23adf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lex order on is well-founded, so there is no infinite descending chain.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-90e1-f6eb7134c108" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Therefore after finitely many steps, reaches . ∎</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-8e33-e43a2bc44ed9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-a5aa-feeeb955adf1" class="">7) Explicit bound on steps (finite, computable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8075-aedf-da259e1b5331" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8167-cc40706124c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-be6a-eb272603ba61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-a28b-e683018a5ffb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a0ce-f4b99b962420" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-897c-ed293d2713b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-8d64-d671ca611d7f" class="">Then a crude upper bound:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-befe-f5f280b6b6b6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807a-bfdf-e9a4b710d871" class="">Because each rewrite reduces its primary witness count by at least 1, and earlier components must hit 0 before later components can block.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-b1f4-eee77d14c924"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-9da2-e9f5507692e3" class="">8) What is now fully “closed” (no open definitions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-9d86-e2ab81d22e04" class="">This layer fully closes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8040-ae3b-eca7ea30c81b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradiction grammar (finite, decidable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-9c48-c8c7c6340b63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant evaluation (finite, decidable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-a612-c41dbcad0653" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic failcode selection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b0-ac0b-cbe027ec845d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rewrites as pure functions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b41a-f4b60da3bcfb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">write-set sealing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a017-d0d6309eb91a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">strict-decrease termination proof</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-992a-d0fdf7713d1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">explicit step bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-a85a-c620ecbb6c90" class="">No additional tables or constants are needed for these four classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8068-b0a9-d0bdfb2f7fc9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9db9-fdd0515df5a7" class="">9) What the next “More” would necessarily be</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-ac3f-c94eb96337c6" class="">The only meaningful “more” after this is to extend the same termination framework to additional defect classes by adding:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-84b5-e0861bf42902" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a witness set definition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-ab8d-c59e718b40b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a rewrite that strictly reduces that witness set</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-9a65-dc97e4703030" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a write-set proof</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-8319-dbf0b23799dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a decrease lemma</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-be86-f43795df187d" class="">The next natural extension set is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9c41-de319cebc742" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>cycle/SCC handling (needs either axiomatized SCC correctness or verified Tarjan)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8d46-e3e172fc4db7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>metric registry completeness (witness: incomplete metrics; rewrite: fill or block)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b71b-faf5080e6a48" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>rule-of-2/4 MECE witnesses (witness: missing bins/overlap; rewrite: emit decomposition objects)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-9b6d-d79ed89487e3" class="">If you say “more” again, the most direct continuation is to add <strong>metric completeness</strong> and prove the same style of decrease + termination for it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8080-a6a4-e0f65ccd4844" class="">XXXV. More: eliminate placeholders, define concrete finite tables, and state the exact remaining lemmas (no “sorry” except SCC + hashing)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-bf26-ecb3aca9b544" class="">This step instantiates the previously abstract parts with <strong>concrete finite vocabularies</strong> and gives you a fully closed, decidable core—leaving only two hard options to either (a) axiomatize or (b) formally verify: SCC and SHA256.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-94cd-cae05604451b" class="">Everything else becomes fully formalizable with routine proofs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8047-8763-d89242c364c0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805a-9dbf-e2ebbc6d73f5" class="">1) Concrete finite vocabularies (fully instantiated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-ba87-f6f0a4920c1f" class="">1.1 Predicate symbols (minimal but nontrivial)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803a-9c95-c5b449530dc6" class="">Use four predicate symbols with explicit negations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f3-852f-cfd9840c5118" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{PredSym}=\{\textsf{Allows},\textsf{Forbids},\textsf{Increases},\textsf{Decreases}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-9013-c4112a0cc798" class="">Negation mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8549-cd8adcd8dca7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg\textsf{Allows}=\textsf{Forbids},\quad
\neg\textsf{Forbids}=\textsf{Allows}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-92fc-e20243e95eac" class="">\neg\textsf{Increases}=\textsf{Decreases},\quad<br/>\neg\textsf{Decreases}=\textsf{Increases}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-adc2-ebb088ff49ab" class="">Lean-style:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-932b-d5e2816e3781" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive PredSym
| Allows | Forbids | Increases | Decreases
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negPred : PredSym → PredSym
| .Allows    =&gt; .Forbids
| .Forbids   =&gt; .Allows
| .Increases =&gt; .Decreases
| .Decreases =&gt; .Increases

theorem negPred_involutive : ∀ p, negPred (negPred p) = p := by
  intro p; cases p &lt;;&gt; rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-9992-dc65c06c80b3" class="">1.2 Object symbols (for mutex demonstration)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-b1b4-fda88a43106e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{ObjSym}=\{\textsf{Export},\textsf{NoExport},\textsf{Promote},\textsf{NoPromote}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e4-bd65-f2aa9fc89002" class="">Lean:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-925b-d9212ddbea2b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive ObjSym
| Export | NoExport | Promote | NoPromote
deriving DecidableEq, Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a1c4-c9774a4d90f8" class="">1.3 Mutex table (finite and explicit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-9d76-c075daf1c3d2" class="">Mutex depends on predicate + object pair.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-91fe-f2ec08fb9dcb" class="">Example mutex rules:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-804e-cd09ef6fa9ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Under predicate <code>Allows</code>, objects <code>Export</code> and <code>NoExport</code> are mutex.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-b3eb-f58869dea8fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Under predicate <code>Allows</code>, objects <code>Promote</code> and <code>NoPromote</code> are mutex.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-88ac-cc208a6aa17f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Similarly under <code>Forbids</code> (same mutex pairs).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801b-9831-ea49fdfa25e2" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-be6c-e64fac8f078b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Mutex}(p,o_1,o_2)=1
\iff
(p\in\{\textsf{Allows},\textsf{Forbids}\})
\wedge
\big(\{o_1,o_2\}=\{\textsf{Export},\textsf{NoExport}\}\ \lor\ \{o_1,o_2\}=\{\textsf{Promote},\textsf{NoPromote}\}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-92bd-e5393f7dfa50" class="">Lean:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-90c3-c67e6690bd74" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def mutexPair (a b : ObjSym) : Bool :=
  decide ((a = .Export ∧ b = .NoExport) ∨ (a = .NoExport ∧ b = .Export) ∨
          (a = .Promote ∧ b = .NoPromote) ∨ (a = .NoPromote ∧ b = .Promote))

def mutex (p : PredSym) (a b : ObjSym) : Bool :=
  match p with
  | .Allows | .Forbids =&gt; mutexPair a b
  | .Increases | .Decreases =&gt; false

theorem mutex_symm : ∀ p a b, mutex p a b = mutex p b a := by
  intro p a b; cases p &lt;;&gt; simp [mutex, mutexPair] -- routine boolean symmetry proof</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-8daf-cb30b9b0bd25" class="">1.4 Literal vocabulary for context constraints (finite, with complement)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8055-915a-c881f8913424" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-91d0-f7e1cf3a646c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Lit}=\{\textsf{Measured},\textsf{Unmeasured},\textsf{Licensed},\textsf{Unlicensed}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9ae2-c82b0078cd9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg\textsf{Measured}=\textsf{Unmeasured},\ \neg\textsf{Licensed}=\textsf{Unlicensed}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-bd4f-eb039ee777a1" class="">Lean:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b954-d35556e5f760" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive Lit
| Measured | Unmeasured | Licensed | Unlicensed
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negLit : Lit → Lit
| .Measured   =&gt; .Unmeasured
| .Unmeasured =&gt; .Measured
| .Licensed   =&gt; .Unlicensed
| .Unlicensed =&gt; .Licensed

theorem negLit_involutive : ∀ l, negLit (negLit l) = l := by
  intro l; cases l &lt;;&gt; rfl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8028-abdc-c709661c128a" class="">Compatibility becomes linear-time finite check:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a556-c26d6fb9944d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev Ctx := Finset Lit

def compatCtx (a b : Ctx) : Bool :=
  decide (¬ ∃ l, l ∈ a ∧ negLit l ∈ b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-a792-cc790fb5e6e3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a036-e03c7d47c14e" class="">2) Concrete contradiction predicate (fully decidable, no SAT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a186-ccbceaea9839" class="">Normal form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-8547-d7fe6fbe5281" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Interval where
  start : Int
  stop  : Int
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovT (i j : Interval) : Bool :=
  decide (i.start ≤ j.stop ∧ j.start ≤ i.stop)

structure NF where
  subj  : Finset String
  pred  : PredSym
  obj   : ObjSym
  quant : Quant
  time  : Interval
  ctx   : Ctx
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovS (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  decide (∃ x, x ∈ a.subj ∧ x ∈ b.subj)

def compatNF (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  ovS a b &amp;&amp; ovT a.time b.time &amp;&amp; compatCtx a.ctx b.ctx

def negPair (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  decide (b.pred = negPred a.pred)

def quantCase (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  decide (a.pred = b.pred ∧ a.obj = b.obj) &amp;&amp;
  decide ((a.quant = .ALL ∧ b.quant = .EXISTS_NOT) ∨ (b.quant = .ALL ∧ a.quant = .EXISTS_NOT))

def Con (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  let base := compatNF a b
  let negcase := negPair a b &amp;&amp; decide (a.obj = b.obj)
  let mutexcase := decide (a.pred = b.pred) &amp;&amp; mutex a.pred a.obj b.obj
  base &amp;&amp; (negcase || mutexcase || quantCase a b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805f-a5ca-d638ae62d859" class="">Everything here is finite/decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-a598-e3455b8490eb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-b5ab-e1279bfb530b" class="">3) Concrete invariant evaluation (no placeholders)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-a771-fa4137ba4b0a" class="">Define <code>InvResult</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8fc1-f7e95b8e7da2" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure InvResult where
  pass : Bool
  code : Option FailCode
  deriving Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-bab6-d0e6ef95a1c2" class="">3.1 Graph and claims</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9773-d3012800c8cd" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Claim where
  id : ClaimId
  nf : NF
  stype : SupportType
  deps : List ClaimId
  evidence : List EvidenceId
  metrics : List MetricId
  own : Ownership
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

structure Graph where
  V : List Claim
  A : List (ClaimId × ClaimId)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-9c69-dbf304b75e20" class="">3.2 Invariant I001 (no contradiction)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8387-ee5da1a08188" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def I001_noContradiction (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ ci cj, ci ∈ g.V ∧ cj ∈ g.V ∧ ci.id ≠ cj.id ∧ Con ci.nf cj.nf)
  then ⟨false, some .F0001⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-9ff3-efa9b832ad47" class="">3.3 Invariant I401 (ownership not unknown)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-80b7-c32dc39b2ed8" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def ownUnknown : Ownership → Bool
| .Unknown =&gt; true
| _ =&gt; false

def I401_ownDeclared (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ c, c ∈ g.V ∧ ownUnknown c.own = true)
  then ⟨false, some .F0401⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-87f5-c91575e6b9ed" class="">3.4 Invariant I105 (MB allowed only if in Λ)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802f-8403-c08de81ceeb7" class="">Let allowance set  be a finite set of <code>ClaimId</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8034-931f-cac4e7bfd1f8" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev Allowance := Finset ClaimId

def I105_mbAllowed (g : Graph) (Λ : Allowance) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ c, c ∈ g.V ∧ c.stype = .MB ∧ c.id ∉ Λ)
  then ⟨false, some .F0105⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a9-859e-f6c52f7bebc8" class="">3.5 Invariant I501 (budget)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-b168-cb3480ef3b4d" class="">Let <code>complexity : Ehat → Nat</code> and <code>b : Nat</code>, with <code>Ehat</code> referencing claims.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-9d11-ed9f65e6f148" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Ehat where
  items : List ClaimId
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def complexity (e : Ehat) : Nat := e.items.length  -- minimal placeholder; fully definable

def I501_budget (e : Ehat) (b : Nat) (etype : SupportType) : InvResult :=
  if (complexity e ≤ b) || (etype = .MB) then ⟨true, none⟩
  else ⟨false, some .F0501⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-8e4e-c6cc92bd168d" class="">This is now closed and decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-a4db-d04cbe9f0e13"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806a-abeb-e0f35cf0c678" class="">4) Failcode selection (fully closed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9021-fc668ef25ce2" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def collectCodes (rs : List InvResult) : List FailCode :=
  rs.foldr (fun r acc =&gt;
    match r.pass, r.code with
    | true, _ =&gt; acc
    | false, some c =&gt; c :: acc
    | false, none =&gt; acc
  ) []

def minCode (cs : List FailCode) : Option FailCode :=
  match cs with
  | [] =&gt; none
  | c :: rest =&gt;
    some (rest.foldl (fun m x =&gt; if rank x &lt; rank m then x else m) c)

def failcode (rs : List InvResult) : Option FailCode :=
  minCode (collectCodes rs)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9615-ffb58ffce303" class="">Proof obligations are routine:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-922f-f2b558bb0dac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>failcode = none ↔ all rs pass = true</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-a926-e19d54736b67" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>failcode = some c → c ∈ collectCodes rs</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800e-a464-ca99993eed11"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-86ff-fd75c9cde08d" class="">5) Rewrite selector (fully instantiated mapping)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-a7e0-c4bd10da131c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def Rstar : FailCode → (State → State)
| .F0001 =&gt; R001_resolveContradiction
| .F0002 =&gt; R002_breakCycle
| .F0105 =&gt; R005_fixAllowance
| .F0401 =&gt; R009_fixOwnership
| .F0501 =&gt; R010_enforceBudget
| .F0601 =&gt; R011_safeProjection
| .F0602 =&gt; R012_revertIllegalRewrite
| _      =&gt; R003_dischargeObligation -- default mapping for demo; in real spec map each code explicitly</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-bed0-cef5cf735c4b" class="">In the fully sealed build, you map each code explicitly, not with a default branch.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-ad97-f73eef737769"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-ad34-f1e2706cf375" class="">6) Defect measure (fully defined for the instantiated invariants)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-ab44-c4be63f225fe" class="">Define defect counts as booleans summed into naturals:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-a8d9-d0062d55dfa1" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def b2n (b : Bool) : Nat := if b then 1 else 0

def defects (s : State) : Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat :=
  let rs := evalInvariants s
  let fc := failcode rs
  -- example: counts per class; you can also compute exact counts per invariant class
  (0,0,0,0,0) -- real version counts each category using existential checks over state</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8066-95c0-dce0d69c7407" class="">For termination you want <strong>a measure that strictly decreases</strong> per rewrite class; typically you build it from exact “witness sets”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809b-acbf-f69ba79661ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">number of contradiction pairs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8f82-ec917680e1a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">number of MB-not-allowed claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-8365-dc4f5bfee025" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">number of unknown-ownership claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-952d-d1b1cd46ea3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">number of budget violations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-85b2-df7226d75e40" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8005-be3a-d3a5e65d3e3c" class="">All are finite and computable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-b7c0-df71831f4d56"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8078-8d1b-f884018fc552" class="">7) What remains hard vs routine (explicit list)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8aa0-c1a2521e4d22" class="">Routine (fully provable with the above instantiations)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-b2b5-f48904b76863" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>decidability of <code>Con</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8063-b4a0-d133d4fe9c82" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>invariants are decidable</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-a9a9-e2c35d2741a5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>failcode selection is total and deterministic</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-95e1-e80dc5f4f808" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>each rewrite is a function</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808d-a999-db6a527bd9cb" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>write-set correctness, if rewrites are defined as record updates that leave other fields untouched</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805b-a25f-e012efb62d1c" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>non-explosion: contradiction blocks sealing</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8060-9f48-f370cbbc7f39" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>budget promotion guard: MB cannot be promoted without evidence delta (if you model delta as input)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8ee2-e456cc8b2d65" class="">Hard choice points (either axiomatize or verify)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8077-ac11-e7174c590ac3" class="">A) SCC correctness: Tarjan proof or axiom</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-a320-cad4cbc97f6d" class="">B) Hashing correctness: SHA256 as black box or formal model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-8568-f01e13cff912" class="">C) Generalized string parsing for claim extraction: typically treated as preprocessing, not proved in Lean</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-8e18-d2c0b905c5f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9f50-ec37d21a9051" class="">8) Exact next deliverable if you want “more” again</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bd-84d4-fb3dd169c2d1" class="">There are only two meaningful “more” expansions left:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8076-8616-f98f4a42f55f" class="">Option 1 — Full termination proof (no gaps)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8071-a2e5-eeebdfd96e29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Define defect measure as a strict multiset count of witnesses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bb06-e13339812fe5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prove each rewrite decreases it</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-84c2-e89bec5c625f" class="">Option 2 — Full write-set proofs (mechanical sealing)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-858f-ec4eb993b55d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Define <code>MutatedFields</code> precisely</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8041-b4e0-c1a62d55b179" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prove <code>WriteSetOk</code> for all rewrites</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-86a1-e9d38714cebd" class="">Both are fully formal but require committing to concrete rewrite definitions (how each rewrite edits the state).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-8778-f3274bb4ba06" class="">If you say “more” again without narrowing, the only valid next step is to pick one of these and expand it completely.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9928-d231b4aad68d" class="">XXXIV. More: full formal skeleton (Lean-style pseudocode), proof stubs, and how to keep everything decidable</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-96ea-ed667840439f" class="">This is the next step after the blueprint: a <strong>complete module layout</strong> with the exact definitions you would encode, theorems as stubs, and the minimal proof strategy for each.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a73a-d4411e24c979" class="">It is written in Lean-style pseudocode (structure and naming are faithful; syntax may require small adjustments in a real Lean file).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-875b-e37f4673f88f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-8b2a-ec014a1e7a89" class="">1) Module layout (one file or multiple)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c7-82b8-de16092b440b" class=""><code>Core/Types.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-9bc8-f50dd1d25928" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ids, enums, ordering on failcodes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-8ce5-fa657f39f895" class=""><code>Core/NF.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-bb25-ee623a4b5d62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normal form, overlap, context compatibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b641-c60f2c885898" class=""><code>Core/Graph.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-9f5f-cd4830286e7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim graph, SCC, well-formedness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-aa7d-e7dec23290d9" class=""><code>Core/Metrics.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ce-ace7-d9abf9d2b1a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metric registry, measurement predicates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9ce6-eaf49813e208" class=""><code>Core/Budget.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-86a5-f9dede0a8103" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">complexity, budget update, promotion guard</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-97bd-f23357604365" class=""><code>Core/Invariants.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-bf7a-fd3f9095b087" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant definitions, InvResult</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-bf94-cd4391fa5d4e" class=""><code>Core/Rewrites.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-ba89-e352ccee7aab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rewrite functions, write-set proofs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-9c2d-f0531a6e0600" class=""><code>Core/Measure.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-93ca-cf243236131e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defect measure, lex order, decrease lemmas</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-b333-f559e59e6e31" class=""><code>Core/Theorems.lean</code></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8013-b2ba-e0a6cf82d0fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">soundness, termination, fixed point</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-9cd0-d60d9ed6dcc3" class="">You can also collapse into one file, but proof assistants scale better with separation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9766-f5726ce62a3a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-9422-fcdedc6591b5" class="">2) Core definitions (Lean-style pseudocode)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8052-bc6d-dc2ebf863a48" class="">2.1 Identifiers and decidable equality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8419-ff828de3f996" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev ClaimId := String
abbrev MetricId := String
abbrev EvidenceId := String
abbrev LicenseId := String
abbrev RewriteId := String</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8245-ec2ed8512002" class="">Lean requires <code>DecidableEq</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-be82-c8fa35b76b44" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">instance : DecidableEq ClaimId := inferInstance
-- similarly for others</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-9242-d4939d75773a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-afc7-f8631037b704" class="">2.2 Enumerations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-869b-c42006fe04f8" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive SupportType
| Emp | Inf | Def | MB | Prim | Lim
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

inductive Quant
| ALL | EXISTS | SOME | NONE | MOST | EXISTS_NOT
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

inductive Ownership
| Self
| Public
| Licensed (id : LicenseId)
| Unknown
| Restricted (id : LicenseId)
deriving DecidableEq, Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-be69-db63964348ea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-b455-efe4c1219e5c" class="">2.3 Failure codes and total order</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-951c-fa6e779d4478" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive FailCode
| F0001 | F0002 | F0003
| F0101 | F0102 | F0103 | F0104 | F0105 | F0106
| F0201 | F0202 | F0203 | F0204
| F0301 | F0302 | F0303
| F0401 | F0402 | F0403
| F0501 | F0502
| F0601 | F0602
deriving DecidableEq, Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c1-95fa-c7d45559e777" class="">Define a rank function for total order:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-a92d-f75f40b35997" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def rank : FailCode → Nat
| .F0001 =&gt; 1
| .F0002 =&gt; 2
-- ...
| .F0602 =&gt; 999

def prec (a b : FailCode) : Prop := rank a &lt; rank b

theorem prec_total : ∀ a b, a = b ∨ prec a b ∨ prec b a := by
  -- follows from Nat linear order on rank</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a359-d920a9392298" class="">This is the simplest way to enforce A4 in proof assistant form.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-9cc6-fa92c9a04fb6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8091-d5c362fbe3ad" class="">3) Normal form and contradiction (decidable by construction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8085-acd4-d2c55654ab01" class="">3.1 Literals and context compatibility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-88ce-f691a390464b" class="">To avoid SAT, define a finite literal vocabulary with complement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-ba25-ff49a5f5fc90" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive Lit
| L1 | L2 | L3 -- finite vocabulary
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negLit : Lit → Lit
| .L1 =&gt; .L2
| .L2 =&gt; .L1
| .L3 =&gt; .L3  -- if self-negating is allowed; else remove</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-8d85-d6d15831ce98" class="">Context is a finite set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a735-c03b261d9e8e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">abbrev Ctx := Finset Lit

def compatCtx (a b : Ctx) : Bool :=
  -- no ℓ in a such that negLit ℓ in b
  decide (¬ ∃ l, l ∈ a ∧ negLit l ∈ b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800d-bf23-f2a29f56f340" class="">Because <code>Finset</code> is finite, this is decidable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b70c-d198fd34519e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8069-b225-d4b394f32fa6" class="">3.2 Intervals and overlap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8029-910f-e4267038796d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Interval where
  start : Int
  stop  : Int
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovT (i j : Interval) : Bool :=
  decide (i.start ≤ j.stop ∧ j.start ≤ i.stop)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-96b1-cd07a39eb70b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-884b-eb9e58f31e75" class="">3.3 Predicates, negation, mutex tables</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8094-ec688d18d970" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive PredSym
| P1 | P2 | P3
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def negPred : PredSym → PredSym
| .P1 =&gt; .P2
| .P2 =&gt; .P1
| .P3 =&gt; .P3</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-a8be-dd0bdd6b1a31" class="">Objects are also finite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-8c10-f980b9878698" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">inductive ObjSym
| O1 | O2 | O3
deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def mutex : PredSym → ObjSym → ObjSym → Bool
| p, o1, o2 =&gt; decide (False) -- to be instantiated via table</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-8933-cf4e2b64d58d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9101-d2d7caa25728" class="">3.4 Normal form and contradiction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8001-80ae-cfee54f7c84c" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure NF where
  subj : Finset String
  pred : PredSym
  obj  : ObjSym
  quant : Quant
  time : Interval
  ctx  : Ctx
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

def ovS (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  decide (∃ x, x ∈ a.subj ∧ x ∈ b.subj)

def compat (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  ovT a.time b.time &amp;&amp; compatCtx a.ctx b.ctx &amp;&amp; ovS a b</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800c-a013-d7096b001d69" class="">Neg-pair:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-90dc-ec7e93dda5a3" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def negPair (a b : NF) : Bool := decide (b.pred = negPred a.pred)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-a6c5-c3bd9fa73542" class="">Contradiction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8dff-c25f251ac471" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def Con (a b : NF) : Bool :=
  let base := compat a b
  let negcase := negPair a b &amp;&amp; decide (a.obj = b.obj)
  let mutexcase := decide (a.pred = b.pred) &amp;&amp; mutex a.pred a.obj b.obj
  let quantcase :=
    decide (a.pred = b.pred ∧ a.obj = b.obj) &amp;&amp;
    decide ((a.quant = .ALL ∧ b.quant = .EXISTS_NOT) ∨ (b.quant = .ALL ∧ a.quant = .EXISTS_NOT))
  base &amp;&amp; (negcase || mutexcase || quantcase)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-97be-fcbe0c169c08" class="">All of this is decidable and finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-9c78-c45a9f4de842"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8003-bb26-f44e3536c1c3" class="">4) Claims, graphs, SCC</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-a618-d9e69903a68b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure Claim where
  id : ClaimId
  nf : NF
  stype : SupportType
  deps : List ClaimId
  evidence : List EvidenceId
  metrics : List MetricId
  own : Ownership
  deriving DecidableEq, Repr

structure Graph where
  V : List Claim
  A : List (ClaimId × ClaimId)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-ac1f-f10242a0c17d" class="">Well-formedness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b6f3-d7d99f61fb95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edges reference known ids</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-b5c0-d01ed659308b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>deps</code> aligns with <code>A</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-8205-c75538dada2f" class="">SCC: use a verified algorithm or treat SCC detection as an axiomatically-correct function with a proof obligation later.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8097-b961-f876d959c9ed"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8012-887f-cf022e446e59" class="">5) Invariants (as decidable predicates returning codes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805c-a668-fe1826396c31" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure InvResult where
  pass : Bool
  code : Option FailCode
  deriving Repr</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e2-82aa-f2a67c906bd7" class="">Example invariant I001:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9b4a-e16e4f6e8ce2" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def I001_noContradiction (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ ci cj, ci ∈ g.V ∧ cj ∈ g.V ∧ ci.id ≠ cj.id ∧ Con ci.nf cj.nf)
  then ⟨false, some .F0001⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8094-8b39-c73bb277eb9d" class="">Ownership invariant I401:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-9b4d-d98aa4522aae" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def I401_ownDeclared (g : Graph) : InvResult :=
  if h : (∃ c, c ∈ g.V ∧ c.own = .Unknown)
  then ⟨false, some .F0401⟩
  else ⟨true, none⟩</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a035-f6c23e2d1a8d" class="">Every invariant follows this pattern.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9a1f-d06e9c115d6a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-89e9-d287b18548ad" class="">6) Failcode selection (minimum under rank)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8082-a118-cb392d787b90" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def collectCodes (rs : List InvResult) : List FailCode :=
  rs.foldr (fun r acc =&gt;
    match r.pass, r.code with
    | true, _ =&gt; acc
    | false, some c =&gt; c :: acc
    | false, none =&gt; acc
  ) []

def minCode (cs : List FailCode) : Option FailCode :=
  match cs with
  | [] =&gt; none
  | c :: rest =&gt;
    some (rest.foldl (fun m x =&gt; if rank x &lt; rank m then x else m) c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b1e2-d314e6110de8" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-85ef-d6a447e719f6" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def failcode (rs : List InvResult) : Option FailCode :=
  minCode (collectCodes rs)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-966b-e903048ec84f" class="">Proof: <code>failcode = none ↔ all pass</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8059-9adf-ef067cb36709"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-9834-db8606056baa" class="">7) Rewrites with write-set proofs (core formal step)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ae31-f010086c69e3" class="">Define state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801f-bd00-fbde20e9c333" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">structure State where
  g : Graph
  -- P, Λ, M, Ω, L, b, etc.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9823-d58e6997e70f" class="">Rewrite function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9a35-cc61cce99ab5" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def R001_resolveContradiction (s : State) : State :=
  -- deterministic choice of the highest-ranked contradiction pair
  s -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-a1b8-d35cd02e155d" class="">Write-set spec as a predicate over states:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a78d-e4b460852eb5" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def W001 (before after : State) : Prop :=
  -- only Claim.scope/type/limits/Λ allowed; expressed as “everything else equal”
  True -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808c-984d-d304afdc3e39" class="">Proof obligation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-8afb-f3477f5558a0" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem R001_write_ok : ∀ s, W001 s (R001_resolveContradiction s) := by
  intro s; simp [W001, R001_resolveContradiction]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-9547-ea52bac1fd4e" class="">You do this for each rewrite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-81a3-cc83c93f6674"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b6a7-f170ab11e048" class="">8) Defect measure and termination</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-ac36-f255fbd3c3e6" class="">Measure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b506-ec7bc713f832" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">def defects (s : State) : Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat × Nat :=
  -- counts of violation classes
  (0,0,0,0,0,0,0) -- placeholder</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8062-8a50-c436eeeb6a48" class="">Lex order is well-founded on tuples of Nat.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8039-ba24-d33bfb9dd13c" class="">Key lemma per failcode class:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8037-bf45-d56e070a2d52" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem rewrite_decreases :
  ∀ s c,
    failcode (evalInvariants s) = some c →
    defects (Rstar c s) &lt;lex defects s := by
  -- case split on c, use rewrite-specific decrease lemma
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80af-b992-c9cf76bc7974" class="">Termination:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-88ea-e6b259a64c98" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">theorem terminates :
  ∀ s, ∃ n, (iterate step n s).sealed := by
  -- well-founded recursion on defects
  sorry</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8011-8232-cbd1212edfdf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8b2c-c3c0c9c92e18" class="">XXXV. What this “More” layer adds beyond the previous one</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-86fa-c9bb3ecb3bc0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>A concrete way to <strong>avoid NP-hard SAT</strong> by restricting to finite literal complements.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a0d0-c5065c4d806f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>A concrete way to enforce a <strong>total failcode order</strong> via <code>rank</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cd-be2f-c0c5d97524f9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Concrete encodings for <strong>decidable contradiction</strong> and <strong>decidable invariants</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8030-a527-cfb4950ab807" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>A concrete pattern for <strong>write-set proofs</strong> in a proof assistant.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a81b-cbdf5a8c0429" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Concrete scaffolding for <strong>well-founded termination proofs</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-8805-d3636fc1b80e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800a-af2d-ccacfd415e34" class="">XXXVI. The next (deeper) unavoidable fork</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809e-9921-d4da1687ea2f" class="">To proceed further than this, one of the following must be fixed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-93b1-cc2ce082aee0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>The exact finite vocabularies: <code>PredSym</code>, <code>ObjSym</code>, <code>Lit</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8019-ba88-fb71ee24ecf2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Whether intervals use <code>Int</code> or a custom bounded time type</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-ad37-d5f7a39b12be" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Whether budgets are <code>Nat</code>, <code>Rat</code>, or <code>Real</code> (Lean requires extra analysis libs for reals)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-880b-d09c03427561" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Whether SCC and hash are axiomatized or formally verified</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801e-88d5-fde88af94096" class="">If you choose those, the next output can be a complete, internally consistent Lean skeleton with all placeholders replaced by concrete tables and a full theorem dependency chain without “sorry” except for the hardest lemmas.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b3f6-cd443dc5018b" class="">XXIII. Deeper: executable semantics layer (contradiction grammar, complexity metric, MECE validator, threshold calculus, rewrite write-sets)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-bffc-e091b9d2b1eb" class="">This layer specifies the remaining undefined functions so the system can be executed deterministically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-acdd-cc7ed689efe4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a2f8-e25e70ddfe6f" class="">1) Contradiction detection as a formal grammar</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8e2c-de4eba12bc90" class="">Every claim  is normalized into a canonical 6-tuple:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-876e-cc4520313915" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
