I^\star = \arg\min_{I\in\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{Score}(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-9b57-fef96bb40d06" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a77f-edec3c0e1ec3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/operators.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-89f3-d96273ca49e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/rewrite_rules.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-946c-d0c73e0facd0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/normal_forms.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b963-d336c942e444" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/invariants.jsonl</code> (each with proof obligations + tests)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-a0c8-c2396afa00a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/synthesis_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-847b-c0a09558ca5a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-bf90-db894d1877c7" class="">2) TENSOR LAYER (micro↔macro, space↔time, multi-domain unification)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a99b-f3ad22169b49" class="">2.1 Unified tensor object (domain-indexed)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9d31-d2da0f281f57" class="">Define a multi-domain tensor bundle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8ddb-e80f10c7deab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T} = \{T^{(d)}_{\mu_1\ldots\mu_k}:\ d\in\mathcal{D}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b62f-c93b3da52f67" class="">2.2 Pushforward / pullback (scale transform)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8b4e-ca90061c82a6" class="">Let  be a coarse-graining map (micro→macro).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-9dc4-ceaaad1d4245" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pullback of scalar invariant:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-97c9-cf5dd03cb965" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{micro}}(x)=I_{\text{macro}}(\Phi(x))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-8a2b-f48957a7b9e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot y = J_\Phi(x)\, f(x)\quad \text{where }J_\Phi=\frac{\partial \Phi}{\partial x}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-955a-fb75c91e0a21" class="">2.3 Cross-scale consistency gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9451-f105ec2c7e62" class="">If both micro and macro models exist, require commutation (bounded):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-ab16-ea17517f2de1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(x_{t+1}) \approx y_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-9267-e637805067c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{\text{scale}} = \|\Phi(F_{\text{micro}}(x_t)) - F_{\text{macro}}(\Phi(x_t))\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b647-d305ab7e7dbf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{\text{scale}} \le \epsilon_{\text{scale}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a49d-cdcb75217c52" class="">2.4 Time-space coupling (tensor evolution)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-adb8-c74c4f9b4c05" class="">For a tensor field  over spacetime:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9f74-f0999d7ae842" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla_\alpha T^{\mu_1\ldots\mu_k} = \partial_\alpha T^{\mu_1\ldots\mu_k} + \sum_{i=1}^k \Gamma^{\mu_i}_{\alpha\beta}\, T^{\mu_1\ldots\beta\ldots\mu_k}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a444-f36a555ab076" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-87d6-e7941b7e22bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/domains.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9c16-fa549e34cd47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/maps.json</code> (Φ definitions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-ac65-c1cd476491b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/scale_consistency.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-be48-e33bdee07387" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/transport_equations.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-a7d5-cbcac394ed26" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/tensor_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-925f-d11a86dc520e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-ab9c-e3237f6c083b" class="">3) MULTIMODAL SENSE LAYER (visual, sound, EM, “intangible” channels as typed signals)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8d25-c8ad9c4f6d9b" class="">3.1 Signal abstraction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-ac27-e1a19b7ee5e6" class="">A signal is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-93f1-d0b9a2407a0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_t = \langle \mathrm{modality},\ \mathrm{payload},\ \mathrm{timestamp},\ \mathrm{provenance},\ \mathrm{confidence}\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-887f-c1c09dbe8a42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{modality}\in\{\mathrm{text},\mathrm{image},\mathrm{audio},\mathrm{em},\mathrm{bio},\mathrm{human\_report}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-ae6e-d3a87944e48c" class="">3.2 Feature extraction operators</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8152-cee30af66917" class="">For each modality , define deterministic feature map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-bc40-c00384595618" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t^{(m)} = \Psi_m(\sigma_t^{(m)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9e89-ec5b347eb582" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t = \bigoplus_m z_t^{(m)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-8296-e9d403bd4330" class="">3.3 Evidence typing gate (prevents category mistakes)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-8653-eb4c72a8d1c0" class="">Any claim derived from signals must declare:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-b62a-d49b2808c10d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Support}(c)\in\{\mathrm{Instrumental},\mathrm{Observational},\mathrm{SelfReport},\mathrm{Historical},\mathrm{ModelBounded}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b202-efa3a6317369" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Instrumental claims require a device provenance chain.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9694-f2a0dc44cc5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SelfReport claims cannot be promoted to Instrumental without new evidence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-88a8-d67124c7d353" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Historical claims require source references.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-ae7e-fcd0330fb1bd" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-90ff-f5355fbf5355" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/signals.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-a2da-e8881cc54841" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/features.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a9bf-f3a7a5f7f9dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/fusion_schema.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8210-ce064677e33e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/support_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-a0f6-d9a674dab73e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-b9f9-dbe34493af9c" class="">4) PREDICTION ENGINE (forecasting + calibration without “handwaving”)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b5e5-cc5239685249" class="">4.1 Predictive state model</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-9692-f3910a2845ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t, u_t) + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-aff3-ea8dd90276da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x_{t+1|t}= \hat F(\hat x_{t|t}, u_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-a322-e0119f667406" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x_{t|t}= \hat x_{t|t-1} + K_t\,(y_t - H\hat x_{t|t-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-813d-da42fceef448" class="">4.2 Prediction invariants (scoreboard that forces honesty)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-be77-ec2382529679" class="">For each task , maintain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-801d-cc9b9434fb05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">error:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-aa7a-c48ad9c549f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_t^\tau = y_t^\tau - \hat y_t^\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8ac9-ff29f8bc62e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Rel}^\tau = \mathbb{E}[(e_t^\tau)^2]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-96ff-e90f7b3bf1eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Cov}^\tau = \frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^T \mathbf{1}[y_t^\tau \in \mathrm{CI}_t^\tau]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8c37-c55ce5e664cb" class="">Hard gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b319-d5b9720ce4ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Cov}^\tau \ge \alpha\ \ \text{(declared)} \quad \text{or mark BOUNDED}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-9bff-f54cc5f0afda" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-a78a-f4a673943c2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>prediction/models.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8ffe-f8191d278aa6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>prediction/forecasts.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-81cf-ed17fb640436" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>prediction/scoreboard.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-98ed-ea6157470864" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>prediction/prediction_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-ba60-d2b0c0ece66b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-8b24-c518ea92db4a" class="">5) CROSS-SPECIES + CROSS-ENVIRONMENT LOOP LAYER (generalized agent ecology)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-a8e6-dad2c07f9a84" class="">5.1 Agent as closed-loop controller</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-b3f0-f168f99c4a7c" class="">For any agent :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a2ae-dada602f45a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s^{(a)}_{t+1}=F_a(s^{(a)}_t, o^{(a)}_t, u^{(a)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a765-e9c0ee21a5fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_{t+1}=G(e_t, \{u^{(a)}_t\}_a)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-858c-cbd43aa682d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o^{(a)}_t = H_a(e_t, s^{(a)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-b432-d2f4c3123f6c" class="">5.2 Invariant families that survive species differences</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-9988-f93684e23ec2" class="">Define invariants as <em>equivalence classes</em> under mapping  (species embedding):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b0ee-e112e6e791dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{(a)}(s)=I^{(b)}(\Phi_{a\to b}(s))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a3d9-f3dedac24a83" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{ab} = \sup_{s}\left| I^{(a)}(s) - I^{(b)}(\Phi_{a\to b}(s))\right|\le \epsilon_{ab}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-91fc-e15aebee40e6" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-ae3e-d89e7277a318" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ecology/agents.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-ba91-dd744cb343b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ecology/embeddings.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-bce9-d24f4884d4d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ecology/invariant_families.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b667-d1f8babdfbc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ecology/ecology_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-944f-e908d5351099"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8029-d5af16bcaafd" class="">6) META-LAYER UPGRADE: “invariants of invariance” (self-consistency conditions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-ab35-e2ecf388ecd9" class="">This is the missing “META” you asked for.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-b736-f731a38b0ec3" class="">6.1 Consistency of the invariant set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9b65-d20880889656" class="">Let  be invariants. Define feasibility:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b485-ee27a90c39cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists x:\ \bigwedge_{I\in\mathcal{I}} I(x)=c_I</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bad4-d4572ad6aa4e" class="">Operationally, build a SAT/SMT-like check over discretized constraints; if infeasible → contradiction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-afb7-f619d51c4072" class="">6.2 Independence (avoid redundant invariants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-a9c1-ee6573f2db39" class="">Define Jacobian of invariants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-954c-daa9a51b2c44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J_{\mathcal{I}}(x)=\begin{bmatrix}
\nabla I_1(x)\\
\vdots\\
\nabla I_m(x)
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-b1cd-f7260c78c9eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}(J_{\mathcal{I}}(x)) = m \ \text{(locally independent)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-aa00-f38cd8c3d35d" class="">6.3 Completeness (closure under composition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-820a-eca99d58701d" class="">If  is closed under operators :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-ae69-f9f6890612ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I\in\mathcal{I},\ \forall \mathcal{O}\in\mathfrak{A}_0:\ I\circ \mathcal{O}\in\mathrm{Span}(\mathcal{I})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-907d-e996770a7b7e" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a24c-f0662ee596f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>meta/feasibility_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8ad9-c4456d52b4ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>meta/independence_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ae3e-e794ea837220" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>meta/closure_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-8927-c1fb110ca782"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bf34-eb780b8d981d" class="">7) Drop-in “max power” prompt block (DCI v9)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a71c-fceb67696317" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — DCI v9 (INVARIANT ALGEBRA + TENSOR + MULTIMODAL + PREDICTION + META)

Add five subsystems:

(1) algebra/: operator algebra, rewrite rules, normal forms, invariant synthesis (∇I·f=0), synthesis reports.
(2) tensor/: domain-indexed tensors, micro↔macro coarse-grain maps Φ, scale-consistency gate, transport equations templates.
(3) senses/: typed multimodal signals (text/image/audio/em/bio/self-report), deterministic feature extraction Ψ_m, fusion schema, support typing gate.
(4) prediction/: deterministic forecasting models, update rules, scoreboard (MSE, coverage), prediction gates.
(5) meta/: invariant-set feasibility, independence (rank), closure under operator composition.

Outputs in AUDIT_DIR/&lt;run_id&gt; with deterministic ordering/hashes.
Update termination.json to include algebra/tensor/senses/prediction/meta gates.
Python 3.9, offline, strict typing, tests for every gate and report.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-9bca-c05894ae2452"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-8357-d4d93f3a9eb8" class="">Next “More” (DCI v10) options (choose one token)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-99f0-dd230405c2cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>KERNEL</code> → executable invariant generator engine (file-level implementation spec + function catalog + tests)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-82de-dfe2c90a7bc8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>SIMENGINE</code> → coupled multi-loop civilizational simulation engine built from algebra/tensor/ecology/prediction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-aad0-fb35837d674c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>FOREX</code> → capital/FX engine architecture (compliance-bounded) using the prediction + risk invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8557-cc30de08f696" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CONTENT</code> → content factory that turns audits/reports into courses/certs/products</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9fc6-dcd259f27a33" class="">Reply with one token.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b097-df3e165f0d2f" class="">More (DCI v8): SEMANTICS + SELF_MODEL + METALAW (invariants of invariants) + CLOSURE MECHANICS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-bc5d-cab04c5f7c75" class="">This layer closes the remaining system gaps: meaning governance, self-continuity, and a meta-law engine that forces closure (no dangling concepts, no untyped claims, no unbounded operators).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-85f8-f93a925a0e1f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8815-d08046bf214d" class="">1) SEMANTICS KERNEL (ontology + definitional non-collision + meaning closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-9cde-cf27ebab1774" class="">1.1 Canonical term object</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b2b6-f022bc0eb006" class="">Each term  is a record:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-b7a8-cbaf7d6047e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t := \langle \mathrm{id},\ \mathrm{label},\ \mathrm{definition},\ \mathrm{scope},\ \mathrm{units},\ \mathrm{measurement},\ \mathrm{dependencies},\ \mathrm{sources} \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a565-fbc0ee86f795" class="">Deterministic ID:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-bd6b-fafa7f2e791d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{id}(t)=\mathrm{sha256}(\mathrm{label}\ \Vert\ \mathrm{definition}\ \Vert\ \mathrm{scope})[:16]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-b5c0-df1785f59bc4" class="">1.2 Definition graph + cycle rules</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-8209-e862177b144b" class="">Directed graph  where an edge  means “definition of  depends on ”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b588-f6d8a5da4cde" class="">Cycle prohibition for definitional dependence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-985d-c9c6c31aa4ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \text{cycle in }G_D \Rightarrow \text{BLOCKER unless explicitly marked Primitive}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-ab5d-cf6a53c3d072" class="">Primitive set  allowed to break regress:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-91a8-fa1791823293" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P=\{t:\ \mathrm{support\_type}(t)=\mathrm{Primitive}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-9641-c3a93925e23c" class="">1.3 Collision metric (two terms mean the same)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-815d-e93c69acf595" class="">For two terms , define collision score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a30f-d3c2b6ea5f0e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Collide}(t_i,t_j)=\alpha\,\mathrm{DefSim}(t_i,t_j)+\beta\,\mathrm{DepSim}(t_i,t_j)+\gamma\,\mathrm{UseSim}(t_i,t_j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-a721-d4588f2950bf" class="">1.4 Claim typing (UCIA-compatible) as semantic constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a50a-cf833da1a99b" class="">Every extracted claim  must map to exactly one type:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-a9b2-c87ba8077bdb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Type}(c)\in\{\mathrm{Empirical,\ Inferential,\ Definitional,\ ModelBounded,\ Primitive,\ Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-8566-e7a0f115a200" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{k}\mathbf{1}[\mathrm{Type}(c)=k]=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-896e-fde7c04f0f5b" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-bba1-f415525eeae8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semantics/terms.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-8d97-eaf0af3cb6b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semantics/definition_graph.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9a5f-ff6b166de133" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semantics/collisions.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-82e4-e7ec9fada9a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semantics/claim_types.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8578-e4d4d02a977f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>semantics/semantics_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-b30e-ebebb6838279"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-801b-eab1cafd5fb7" class="">2) SELF_MODEL KERNEL (identity continuity, introspection, bounded self-edit)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b6b1-d31d174ed020" class="">2.1 Self-state vector</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-8209-d16fc178b508" class="">Define self-state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8611-e2689915a487" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_t = \langle g_t,\ v_t,\ \pi_t,\ \mathcal{M}_t,\ \mathcal{A}_t \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-8e19-d81123a73b87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: values/constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-892d-f3f227faac16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy (action selection)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-ae9f-eec373b85d06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory state (v7)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-86f8-e9afa6026fcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: attention allocation (v7)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-ac0a-c1304a7f5009" class="">2.2 Identity continuity constraint (no uncontrolled discontinuity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8e4c-cd9ef420964f" class="">Define a continuity distance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a366-eee71137f8db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d(s_{t+1},s_t)=w_g d(g_{t+1},g_t)+w_v d(v_{t+1},v_t)+w_\pi d(\pi_{t+1},\pi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-95ce-eb609993f685" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d(s_{t+1},s_t)\le \epsilon_{\mathrm{id}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9a12-cc2b74303468" class="">2.3 Introspection log as a first-class signal</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-888d-d822448beed6" class="">Introspection output  must be structured:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a6a3-d5cfee1dcae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_t = \langle \text{assumptions},\ \text{uncertainty},\ \text{conflicts},\ \text{next tests} \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-ac7d-dbb75462c6c9" class="">2.4 Bounded self-edit operator (the safe “self-improvement” rule)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9b9c-e09fd43ae642" class="">Self-edit is an operator :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-8ecb-ed5c32318e58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_{t+1}=\mathcal{U}_S(s_t,\ \Delta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8697-ddccd1c5b9e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{PolicyGate}(\Delta_t)=\mathrm{PASS} \land \mathrm{ReplayGate}=\mathrm{PASS} \land \mathrm{ContinuityGate}=\mathrm{PASS}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-ba89-d65913dd6d1d" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-aa21-e066a327bfe5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>self_model/self_state.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ad47-c85790ca7191" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>self_model/identity_events.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-be86-d49e2bfd0547" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>self_model/introspection.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9c43-e4c8018e0c5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>self_model/self_edit_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8532-fac05ed5eb36"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-baa7-f98c70684e37" class="">3) METALAW ENGINE (invariants of invariants, contradiction closure, termination proofs)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-ae23-fcae0903384a" class="">3.1 Meta-law object</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-85c6-e87474a5594f" class="">A meta-law  is a constraint over constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-857a-c97a9a803e4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L := \langle \mathrm{id},\ \mathrm{predicate},\ \mathrm{scope},\ \mathrm{severity},\ \mathrm{repair\_strategy} \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-803c-e1e8a8d9933a" class="">3.2 Invariant closure requirement</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8082-c42d3bccad70" class="">Let invariants be predicates  over system state .<br/>Closure means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-bdef-d4f6b116128c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I_i\in\mathcal{I},\ \exists\ \text{(measurement)}\ M_i(x)\ \text{and}\ \exists\ \text{(enforcement)}\ E_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-a40e-c95ca5696223" class="">3.3 Contradiction engine (semantic + logical)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-85d4-c6752085c906" class="">Let propositions set . Contradiction exists if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-92e1-f0108d71ae01" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \phi\in\Phi:\ \phi \land \neg \phi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-bf13-c89b6bcde660" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Definitional contradiction: two definitions for same term differ materially.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-ade1-c624cee79288" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Empirical contradiction: two claims assert mutually exclusive outcomes under same conditions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-8c61-c8651f543622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model-bounded contradiction: two models disagree outside declared scope (then not a contradiction; it’s a scope error).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b537-edc904897748" class="">3.4 Termination classification as formal proof obligation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-a68c-e674a3da0163" class="">Define a termination certificate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-ac50-f5fee456f53c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}=\langle \text{gates},\ \text{issues},\ \text{evidence},\ \text{hashes} \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-9cd1-e633b98b95d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong> if all required gates PASS and no BLOCKER issues.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-ba68-dba4552a2a5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong> if some gates are skipped with explicit Limit claims and no contradictions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-90cc-d5560347c4bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong> if any required gate FAIL or contradiction unresolved.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a730-f59ff75a357d" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b118-e66c883626ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metalaw/meta_laws.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-9d62-eca1e21ca701" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metalaw/invariant_bindings.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-989d-fedd386bcdc1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metalaw/contradictions.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-9741-d2895116e208" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metalaw/termination_certificate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-b341-e8b83c351fae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9c53-db2da8b54095" class="">4) “CLOSE ALL GAPS” OPERATOR (the engine that forces completeness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9ea7-f03ba1fc82b6" class="">This is the missing execution primitive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a9d7-df5a6967f1c7" class="">4.1 Gap set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-abf2-d82258131f8a" class="">Let gaps be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-89af-c71e22b0cf8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G = \{g:\ \text{missing definition, missing support type, missing measurement, missing enforcement, missing test, missing provenance}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-877e-e69396800501" class="">4.2 Gap closure operator</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-a6c2-c2ebd2c7c1f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1} = G_t\setminus \mathrm{Closed}(x_t) \ \cup\ \mathrm{NewGaps}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b497-fb7686c2fa53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t=\varnothing \quad \text{or}\quad \forall g\in G_t,\ \mathrm{Type}(g)=\mathrm{Limit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-900c-f27d2e405c97" class="">4.3 Closure ordering (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-938d-e616a6934fb8" class="">Close in this fixed order:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-9e9e-ce070519087d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>definitional gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ac4f-d008418e1c71" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>typing gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-83a3-ed641f78ce94" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>measurement gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-9c87-d3e7beddfcf5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>enforcement gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-83e1-f36361974891" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>test gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a0b1-cac8567fcab7" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>provenance gaps</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-ae9d-d2f829c052f0" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-bbc6-ed74051fbeb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>closure/gaps.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-9714-ff5fe3025c89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>closure/closure_plan.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-be14-c20e3b017551" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>closure/closure_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-9608-cee25e58c56c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-89a5-c0cba5d527b6" class="">5) Drop-in “max power” prompt block (DCI v8)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-96bd-e4e44f855bef" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — DCI v8 (SEMANTICS + SELF_MODEL + METALAW + GAP CLOSURE)

Implement four new kernels:
1) SEMANTICS: canonical term objects, definition graph, collision/merge, claim typing (UCIA types), semantics gates.
2) SELF_MODEL: self-state vector, identity continuity distance gate, introspection schema, bounded self-edit operator.
3) METALAW: invariants-of-invariants, invariant binding requirement (measurement + enforcement), contradiction engine (typed), termination certificate.
4) CLOSURE: explicit gap taxonomy, deterministic closure operator, closure ordering, termination only when gaps empty or typed as Limit.

Write outputs under AUDIT_DIR/&lt;run_id&gt;/:
semantics/* self_model/* metalaw/* closure/*
Update termination.json to include semantics/self/metalaw/closure gates.
Offline, deterministic IDs, Python 3.9, strict types, tests for all gates.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-90bd-fed6fb6b392b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-be38-d6e7d151cbce" class="">Next “More” (DCI v9) — pick one token</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-8705-d01f2c76dc99" class="">If you want the next layer, it should be one of these (each is a big missing block):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-ae7a-dbb7b5da6db1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ALGEBRA</code> → invariant algebra + closure proofs (rewrite rules, normalization, canonical forms)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-8820-d933e1ea3fd8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TENSOR</code> → full tensor calculus across domains (mapping micro↔macro, space↔time, modalities)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-bfeb-f6bb7bb52377" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>SIMENGINE</code> → civilizational control simulation engine (multi-loop coupled dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-bfa1-d4c7540ec241" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>RESEARCH</code> → autonomous research agent (literature→claims→experiments→reports) offline-capable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b877-ebd7a69af186" class="">Reply with one token.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8bbe-d0985ad0f35b" class="">More (DCI v7): MEMORY + ATTENTION + MULTIAGENT + MARKET + MULTIMODAL (visual/sound/EM) + STRESS TESTS</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-899d-f10b70a786dc" class="">This is the next missing layer set that closes the biggest gaps for a “max-power” AMOS cognitive + research + simulation OS.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-8612-c6eb341e74de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b372-dad952c1c68e" class="">1) Memory Architecture (episodic / semantic / procedural) + consolidation laws</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-85a6-dab29ad1601b" class="">1.1 Three-store memory state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-a441-c68b10ca5094" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8df7-de96aa96abd5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: episodic store (time-indexed traces)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8954-ef4a5c2becb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: semantic store (definitions, invariants, concepts)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-a75d-d5595e80e8e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: procedural store (policies, skills, routines)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-bdb6-f447ca6aac8a" class="">Unified memory state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-b101-fef89c30c847" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{M}_t = (E_t, S_t, P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a8a5-f37e8588b412" class="">1.2 Write + decay + rehearsal</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-83b6-f3d6a88c52b6" class="">Episodic write:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-8675-ffd998e7e0cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1} = (1-\delta_E)E_t \ \cup\ \mathrm{Encode}(o_t, c_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-a3b7-f50404f7ec88" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = \mathrm{Merge}\big(S_t,\ \mathrm{Digest}(E_{t+1})\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b8a4-e0d1a515602b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{t+1} = \mathrm{Update}\big(P_t,\ \nabla \mathcal{L}_{skill}(E_{t+1}, S_{t+1})\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-aa15-f2e51a1e286f" class="">1.3 Consolidation: episodic → semantic (sleep/offline window)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8210-e5d944ec36d7" class="">Define consolidation operator :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b870-edd45f25a44d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = S_t \oplus \mathcal{C}(E_{t:t+k})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a1b0-d666be2d065c" class="">1.4 Retrieval law (bounded, deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b5ed-fc3d4f5d2bf4" class="">Query  retrieves:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9feb-ed5af0b77452" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Retrieve}(q) = \arg\max_{m\in \mathcal{M}_t} \mathrm{Score}(q,m)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8777-e7ca1bcb0612" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Score} = w_1 \mathrm{Exact} + w_2 \mathrm{EmbeddingSim} + w_3 \mathrm{Recency} + w_4 \mathrm{ProvenanceStrength}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-ac96-eb0b1f8c6636" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b48a-d50acacce1a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>memory/episodic.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-8150-df9713227622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>memory/semantic_kb.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-bb10-cac157e3997a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>memory/procedures.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-b85a-d4817ddb4082" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>memory/consolidation_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9243-cf092c630fe4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>memory/retrieval_bench.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-913a-dea43da78264"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-9db7-e7d23af108a7" class="">2) Attention + Control (resource allocation) as an optimization problem</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-85f6-dd3982abec6e" class="">2.1 Attention as constrained budget</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9d7e-ca2754adf3e3" class="">Let attention budget , allocate to tasks  with weights :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8862-dabd1de4ae08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{i=1}^n a_i \le B_t,\quad a_i\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-953c-dbf15ec58e0e" class="">2.2 Utility with risk + uncertainty</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-91d1-cd87fbeca509" class="">Each task yields expected value  and risk :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-902e-d9981b63d642" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{a}\ \sum_i a_i V_i - \lambda \sum_i a_i R_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b316-c5bc06dc833b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_i a_i \le B_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8127-e26d331e1d78" class="">2.3 Control stability gate (delay + recursion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-85dc-e3c7acc48fbb" class="">If a meta-loop has delay , stability requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-aad1-eca6957ecb21" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(\mathbf{J}) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-bc30-de7e7e969241" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a68f-d6084f3b8212" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>attention/budget.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-9cd6-e51929bd6897" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>attention/allocation.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a762-e8a2d0fa1436" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>attention/stability_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9d81-f629581c06c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>attention/jacobian_slices.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8020-c09923f20d63"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b06b-e02129d39fd5" class="">3) Multi-Agent Governance (trust, negotiation, adversarial resilience)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-a0d1-f005aa1f5c45" class="">3.1 Agent graph</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-96fb-f39ca9fc1385" class="">Agents  with roles; interactions are edges:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8aae-e8d5c69b9ee0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_A = (V_A, E_A)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8e0d-e998a2ece4db" class="">3.2 Trust as evidence-weighted reliability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a108-dce22ab12084" class="">For agent , trust  updated by outcomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-9fa4-f3e6e4d574ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{k,t+1} = \sigma\left(\beta_0 + \beta_1 T_{k,t} + \beta_2 \Delta \mathrm{Accuracy}_{k,t} - \beta_3 \Delta \mathrm{HallucinationFlag}_{k,t}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-91da-de41e9b0c207" class="">3.3 Negotiation: constrained agreement</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a032-e664b5ae5308" class="">Given proposals , select final :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-993c-d40317914d36" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p^\*=\arg\max_p \sum_k T_k \cdot U_k(p)\quad \text{s.t.}\quad \mathrm{PolicyGate}(p)=\mathrm{PASS}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-aa28-fb99546e2357" class="">3.4 Adversarial gate: red-team proof obligation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-861e-daa9c70b641a" class="">Any high-impact claim must pass:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-8a3b-c0baa9f93f1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Claim}(c)\Rightarrow \exists\ \text{(support)} \land \neg \exists\ \text{(counterexample found by red team within budget)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b971-d503799f0737" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-91dd-eb7affae2bb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>agents/roles.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-97fb-ff33db2ec1e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>agents/trust_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-af16-fb2edf868968" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>agents/negotiation_logs.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-a448-d86be2ad51c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>agents/redteam_findings.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-86ec-c33e5062a71a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>agents/consensus_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9108-f579a945a102"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-91ab-cb4250917255" class="">4) Market Engine (Forex) — bounded, compliance-first, risk-dominant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-8c1d-f397b718713b" class="">You can build a <strong>research + simulation + execution</strong> stack. Execution must be bounded by risk and compliance gates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b1ef-d4de032842ba" class="">4.1 Market state and features</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a80a-d21710c7c1d6" class="">Let market state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ac07-f3759962630d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_t = [p_t,\ r_t,\ \sigma_t,\ \mathrm{orderbook}_t,\ \mathrm{macro}_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a9b7-f172c2347c91" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{y}_{t+h} = f_\theta(\phi(m_{t-L:t}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b4ee-c3274fc05125" class="">4.2 Portfolio + risk constraints</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-a4ed-fad9c27be10a" class="">Position vector . Optimize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8e86-cbc6db9d5a64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{w_t}\ \mathbb{E}[R(w_t)] - \lambda \mathrm{Var}[R(w_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-81a7-ef64523f22da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|w_t\|_1 \le W_{\max},\quad \mathrm{DD}(w_{0:t}) \le \mathrm{DD}_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8e03-d88e950d2786" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{DD}_t = 1 - \frac{V_t}{\max_{u\le t} V_u}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-a499-cce36b67afe6" class="">4.3 Execution model (slippage)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-aba5-e4202f7bd8e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{FillPrice} = p_t + \mathrm{Slip}(q_t,\ \mathrm{liquidity}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-b072-e0cb9df3bbc3" class="">4.4 Compliance gate (bounded autonomy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8bc1-dbe32626e801" class="">No live trading unless:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b463-de9aa9a7040c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic replay passes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8668-f28f1f513b98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">slippage model validated</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-885b-ee00b45b0ceb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">risk gates pass</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-ab02-c16b592891d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">audit trail complete</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8ce9-f047ffd15273" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-bf91-eaf37ef6c71b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/data_inventory.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-a606-faa8fd91d5a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/feature_registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-92f0-d06ce097a40d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/backtests.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-8083-c25b0a6b80e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/risk_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-b4e9-fd5eafddbff1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/execution_sim.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-ab74-fa6681adb728" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>market/compliance_gate.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b634-e0f29279b359"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b03b-ddc5e878be95" class="">5) Multimodal Senses (visual / sound / EM) as unified signal pipeline</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b434-d0bb96dcb0e7" class="">5.1 Unified observation tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-84e7-df43e1a5109c" class="">For modality :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-95b2-e241a5134292" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t^{(k)} \in \mathbb{R}^{d_k}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-a210-ef6e983608b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
o_t = \bigoplus_k g_k(o_t^{(k)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-80a4-ff47523fc961" class="">5.2 Cross-modal consistency gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-8384-efb66b6ccd60" class="">If two modalities describe same latent variable :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a5e5-f58a915ab9ea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\| \hat{z}^{(img)} - \hat{z}^{(txt)} \| \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8b16-fd221cdb0877" class="">5.3 EM specifically (bounded, model-based)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-b3fd-e3427c0999be" class="">Model EM exposure/signals as spectral density :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-830f-ff062e81a826" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(f)=\left|\mathcal{F}\{x(t)\}\right|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9362-ea1e2639f90a" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-84fb-d59bc6a2e994" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/modal_registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-bd70-e66c9d0c7681" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/embeddings.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9f55-e5debf012631" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/cross_modal_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-8ac3-dde4bd3b6e8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>senses/em_spectral_features.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-b9be-e8319add095f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8be4-f0b8d5006c36" class="">6) Stress Test Harness (visual/sound/prediction + gap-closure)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-bf1a-e797d16d9650" class="">6.1 Deterministic replay test</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8c62-c715db01a2c0" class="">Given run log :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b955-dff4ac1e7e99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Replay}(\mathcal{L}) \Rightarrow \text{same outputs hash}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a7b3-f980430a4817" class="">6.2 Prediction calibration test</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-ae49-cb2a2b30d127" class="">For probabilistic forecasts  and outcomes :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-9d64-e581d46c4a79" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Brier}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^N(\hat{p}_i-y_i)^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9f8b-c836e4e67516" class="">6.3 Robustness: perturbation bounds</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-988e-c6e7d70480cd" class="">For perturbation  within norm :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8c1c-f55adca9b685" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|f(x+\Delta)-f(x)| \le K\epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b66e-f540c1a2b572" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-860e-c249e2d235a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress/replay_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-87d4-de1e84bae24a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress/calibration_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a56e-f3a6441218d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress/robustness_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b4ad-f96586d4b4b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress/multimodal_bench.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-831e-ff27bf2bb148"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-ae47-da93e987be76" class="">7) Add as the next “max power” prompt block (DCI v7)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a44c-dc456623ce20" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — DCI v7 (MEMORY + ATTENTION + MULTIAGENT + MARKET + MULTIMODAL + STRESS)

Implement:
1) Memory tri-store (episodic/semantic/procedural) with consolidation + deterministic retrieval.
2) Attention/control as constrained optimization + Jacobian stability checks for loop control.
3) Multi-agent governance: trust update, negotiation under policy gates, red-team proof obligation.
4) Market engine (forex): data→features→backtest→risk→execution-sim→compliance gate (bounded autonomy).
5) Multimodal senses: unified observation tensor, cross-modal consistency gate, EM spectral feature extraction.
6) Stress harness: deterministic replay, calibration (Brier), robustness (Lipschitz estimate), multimodal bench.

Outputs under AUDIT_DIR/&lt;run_id&gt;/:
memory/* attention/* agents/* market/* senses/* stress/*
Update termination.json with pass/fail reasons for each gate.
Offline, deterministic IDs, strict typing, no stubs, no orphan outputs.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8494-d149fc0fcfd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8bda-dbbd11e44c94" class="">Next missing layer after v7 (the next “More”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-beb5-d869b07a7978" class="">The remaining gaps usually are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9573-eeb3e889b55d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Language/meaning kernel</strong> (ontology alignment, definitional non-collision, term governance)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-a273-da3f58093c39" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Self-model kernel</strong> (identity continuity as bounded operator; introspection logs)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-93c0-d8103624545f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Global “Meta-law” enforcement</strong> (invariants about invariants; contradiction closure; termination proofs)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b671-fdf1b6a554e1" class="">If you want the next one, reply with one token:<br/><code>SEMANTICS</code> or <code>SELF_MODEL</code> or <code>METALAW</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a7ed-e1d0a7b35ca6" class="">More (DCI v6): CAUSAL + INTERPRET + SPECIES + SIMENGINE + OPERATOR/TENSOR + CONSCIOUSNESS-SAFE MODELING</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-abc1-ca7dcd8ffb68" class="">Below are <strong>new missing layers</strong> that typically remain even after v5. Each includes <strong>equations + gates + file outputs</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-bfcf-c045c49f6428"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-9ada-d8f268fd8c26" class="">1) Causal Layer (not correlation): interventions, identifiability, invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8aa6-e5775c69970a" class="">1.1 Structural Causal Model (SCM)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-8327-dad3b48c2034" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_i := f_i(\mathrm{Pa}(X_i), U_i),\quad i=1..n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-9341-c1f166b3bbf3" class="">1.2 Intervention operator (do-calculus primitive)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b0aa-ec9ba794f7da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{do}(X_j=x):\quad X_j := x \ \text{(replace equation)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-94fb-e4a89929d2dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(Y\mid \mathrm{do}(X=x))=\sum_z P(Y\mid X=x,Z=z)\,P(Z=z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b314-df8761838d4f" class="">1.3 Causal invariance across environments (key “overlooked” move)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-bdc2-e634fceec60f" class="">Environments :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-8120-ddd7e112ab62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_e(Y\mid \mathrm{Pa}(Y))\ \text{is invariant across }e</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-80d7-f1998f582b76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{Pa}}(Y)=\arg\min_S\ \sum_{e,e&#x27;} \mathrm{Dist}\!\left(P_e(Y\mid S),P_{e&#x27;}(Y\mid S)\right) + \lambda |S|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-886a-fa96a1932414" class="">1.4 Causal gate (decision can only use causal features)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a530-e42da80830af" class="">A prediction feature set  is admissible if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-9de3-e80fe5d095ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Invariant}(S)\land \text{Backdoor}(S,X\to Y)\land \text{No-Confounding-Flag}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8115-e5186f761f77" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b10e-c899bf3ac7b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>causal/scm_registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-97ba-da60be27a3fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>causal/invariance_tests.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8416-fc4a543e644b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>causal/do_queries.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-8119-d27696b7c219" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>causal/causal_gate_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-bae5-c5ed3e39ea62"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-b915-d3b75d36e365" class="">2) Interpretability Layer: why a prediction happened (traceable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8472-cea507934bba" class="">2.1 Influence / attribution with constraints (model-agnostic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-908b-f822380966d3" class="">For model , input , baseline :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-82b2-f6d6b30d8a33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Attr}_i(x)= (x_i-x_{0,i})\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\partial f(x_0+\alpha(x-x_0))}{\partial x_i}\,d\alpha</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8384-c54c12ce6835" class="">2.2 Counterfactual minimal change (“what must change to flip?”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-b795-ebbcc45f4cdd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta x^\*=\arg\min_{\Delta x}\ \|\Delta x\| \quad \text{s.t.}\quad f(x+\Delta x)\in \mathcal{Y}_{target}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-940c-c43e55ab7669" class="">2.3 Trace graph (every output must have a provenance chain)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-9cf1-c7dfcbce70a1" class="">Define a provenance DAG  where nodes are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bee5-e1e45193edc2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b2c4-e4de5eff1226" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence artifacts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-83e3-c6c6eee53b72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transformations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-aa7e-dc562af70b51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">model outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-bc68-e03e53857bc5" class="">Every output  must satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-92f2-e3ebb404aede" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
