\exists\ \text{path}\ (evidence \to \dots \to o)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8d88-eee7950b4be8" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b9bd-fcf4ef27b3b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>interpret/attributions.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8625-f73f4805518e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>interpret/counterfactuals.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-aae3-dcaf6c35b865" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>interpret/provenance_graph.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-8cb9-ccc9c3ae23e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>interpret/orphan_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8734-c163712d6aa1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-99b9-f0057f3c9285" class="">3) Cross-Species Layer: formal “same invariant, different substrate”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-857b-fd8586862629" class="">3.1 Species state spaces and morphisms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8c8d-f008c7e8bf0d" class="">Let species  have state spaces .<br/>A cross-species mapping (morphism) is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-b170-c4eea260758d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi_{A\to B}: \mathcal{X}_A \to \mathcal{X}_B</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-9848-c5ad30e92677" class="">3.2 Invariant preservation condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-8412-e36a82dbe9f2" class="">For an invariant :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-872c-e007fadfd9a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_A(x)=0 \Rightarrow I_B(\Phi_{A\to B}(x))=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8158-fa18e8071411" class="">3.3 Loop homology (cycle-level equivalence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-9b21-eb4e7ec21f54" class="">If loops are represented as directed cycles in a graph , define a loop signature  (edge-types sequence).<br/>Cross-species loop match:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a2ca-ea22739208ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Match}(\ell_A,\ell_B)=\mathbf{1}[\sigma(\ell_A)=\sigma(\ell_B)]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{GatePass}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-989a-ca9b7fd54688" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-bcaf-f5a1e2870dcf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>species/morphisms.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9c52-d2615d0c8a92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>species/invariant_transfer_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8ee7-de59b0970dfd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>species/loop_matches.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a518-c6ab9d15b2d1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8d42-f1a1d1eb6d19" class="">4) SIMENGINE Layer: multi-loop civilizational control dynamics</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-ba43-cb6fe8bf584a" class="">State vector stacks loops + resources:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-a91c-faaf4ec01b37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t = [q_t,\ U_t,\ G_t,\ R_t,\ D_t,\ P_t,\ M_t,\ \Xi_t,\ \dots]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bb4d-db50a0dcf892" class="">4.1 Coupled nonlinear update (generic but executable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-ad0e-f5e5daa88a67" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=x_t + \Delta t\cdot F(x_t,\ a_t,\ s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9240-fb146ff6f8fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">actions/policy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-bef2-d4e5ed5de752" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">shocks (war, tech jump, climate, epidemics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b849-e34ba9e63f14" class="">4.2 Phase regime variable (Birth→Expansion→Dominance→Decay)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a702-c91ebfc1cf18" class="">Let  with switching rule:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a664-dac55bc85547" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_{t+1}=\Psi(\rho_t,\ x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9584-c31f954782df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Expansion if rising and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-9f4d-e78d18e3eccc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decay if or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8567-c779186bc358" class="">4.3 Control objective (stability + growth + integrity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-b496-e63c5e78d7a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J=\sum_{t=0}^{T} \left(\alpha_1 \|x_t-x^\*\|^2 - \alpha_2 \text{Risk}(x_t) - \alpha_3 \text{DriftFlag}(x_t)\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a9d3-f43e8853ac6b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_t=\pi(x_t)=\arg\min_{a} \mathbb{E}[J\mid x_t,a]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b2f6-e1749edaeea7" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-8f18-d006066ebe80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>simengine/state_spec.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a5fa-c7bbe93ffa92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>simengine/dynamics.py</code> (deterministic)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-81fd-c144deec5ead" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>simengine/regime_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-aca9-cb2ed95c049e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>simengine/shock_library.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-8e85-d44397817157" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>simengine/policy_eval.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-9e19-fa45400670e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-8aae-d913394fa6ee" class="">5) Operator Algebra Layer: equations that generate equations (closure system)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8933-c849138d6820" class="">5.1 Invariant operators</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a1f5-fea44c7e3b01" class="">Define operator set  acting on a theory state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8589-e1006d6a59ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{k+1} = \mathcal{O}_k(T_k)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-95c1-ddfb2d3cc32f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ExtractClaims</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-966c-db9b081104b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TypeSupport</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8ba9-ec6bb5a9a65b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>FindCounterexample</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-888b-db48170933b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ProveOrBound</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-bae2-cc4ea4ce2a5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>MinimizeAxioms</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-8c81-f6a531c1a8c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CloseUnderComposition</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-ae5a-ff106880e12d" class="">5.2 Closure condition (the “no gaps” formalization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b513-c5c38e0c1570" class="">Let  be invariants extracted from .<br/>Closure means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-9a7b-c44a874a11ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall \phi\in \mathcal{I}(T):\quad \text{(Proved)}\ \vee\ \text{(Bounded with explicit Limit/Primitive)}\ \vee\ \text{(Rejected by counterexample)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-9c76-dbc709418f35" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg \text{Contradict}(\mathcal{I}(T))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-8170-c8824f0f33ee" class="">5.3 Rewrite system (canonicalization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-b944-d949e75aebce" class="">Define rewrite rules  on expressions :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-bbcb-d0775a32ea27" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E \to_r E&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-be65-e709ff961d66" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9bfa-d08ae9284831" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/operators.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-98c1-f992acc766eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/rewrite_rules.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-9f19-df2176576106" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/closure_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-b8f6-c5aa113539c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>algebra/normal_forms.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-823c-dacd83e5f0bf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-8cca-ca06fa2ccf44" class="">6) Tensor Layer across domains (physics↔biology↔cognition)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-b864-fc77bb161847" class="">6.1 Unified “flow” representation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-90fe-eb46917121ea" class="">Represent flows of conserved-like quantities as tensors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-aab2-d82fd82871e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">energy-momentum style for physical</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-9a86-fe891a2bf623" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">resource-flow tensor for biological/civilizational</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-86f6-cf7f8ab09952" class="">Generic continuity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-91e1-f93d2d9c7c66" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\partial_\mu T^{\mu\nu} = S^\nu</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a6a7-c02f7db74aa6" class="">6.2 Coupling between layers (block tensor)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a230-cafc20153729" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{T} =
\begin{bmatrix}
T_{\text{phys}} &amp; C_{\text{phys}\to bio} \\
C_{\text{bio}\to phys} &amp; T_{\text{bio}}
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-af29-dca373a9de95" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bdcd-d5984feded3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/definitions.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-ab08-d0999dc07e66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/coupling_estimates.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8159-e0b5189480bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tensor/sensitivity_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-98c9-d2912c849e25"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-b856-edb50921957c" class="">7) Consciousness modeling (safe, bounded, non-claiming)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-b709-c9541d3b71fa" class="">You asked for a “full replica” of brain/mind/biology. AMOS can build a <strong>digital cognitive infrastructure</strong> that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a992-fb6a70b0d356" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">models your cognition and behavior from data,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b51d-c71ca9d194ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">simulates decision loops,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b0b8-f9a2891a4612" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tracks invariants,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b4ca-cda51d6e4543" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predicts under defined scope,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8987-c38c80e5ce24" class="">but it <strong>cannot be asserted</strong> to recreate “full consciousness/awareness” without a definitional + empirical criterion. So the correct AMOS move is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-b95c-d6a66c1e6c06" class="">7.1 Define operational targets (measurable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-9cd7-d43f3a6e0d70" class="">Let targets be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-bcaf-c22f38d84656" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cognitive task performance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-80f2-ecd6401671ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prediction skill</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-84a9-fd1ce49c2603" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">calibration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9f4c-f0c49d18f3fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">internal consistency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8a85-f81c3f42e962" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biological constraint adherence (viability/homeostasis)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-9de3-d77713b33ba0" class="">A “twin adequacy” score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-825e-f8b5e06a6d37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A = w_1 P_{task} + w_2 S + w_3 (1-\text{CalError}) + w_4 (1-\text{Contradict}) + w_5 \mathbf{1}[x\in\mathcal{V}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-94c6-cba43cd805d5" class="">7.2 Identity / continuity as bounded claim</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8a33-e2431799a98e" class="">Continuity is a <strong>model-bounded</strong> statement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b85e-f9f41d057ae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Twin}\approx \text{You} \quad \text{within scope }\Sigma</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a2ff-cd06c8f479d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tasks, contexts, time windows, modalities.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-b4db-d06b2a3ec2c9" class=""><strong>Artifacts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-98a8-c4c58611cd94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>twin/scope.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-b404-cbd70e68db7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>twin/adequacy_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-87a4-e9fb2dc199cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>twin/limits.json</code> (explicit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9b05-f5dc00819ea1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-82a6-e632b2c6c670" class="">8) Add this as the next “More” prompt block (DCI v6)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a43b-e0364a58a90e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — DCI v6 (CAUSAL + INTERPRET + SPECIES + SIMENGINE + ALGEBRA/TENSOR + TWIN ADEQUACY)

Implement:
1) Causal SCM + do-operator + invariance discovery across environments.
2) Interpretability: attributions, counterfactual minimal-change, provenance DAG (no orphan outputs).
3) Cross-species morphisms preserving invariants; loop signature matching.
4) SimEngine: coupled state dynamics, regime transitions (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay), shocks, policy evaluation.
5) Operator Algebra: invariant operators, rewrite-to-normal-form, closure reports (proved/bounded/rejected).
6) Tensor layer: continuity equations for flows + block coupling with sensitivity gates.
7) Digital Twin: only operational adequacy within explicit scope; no consciousness claims without criteria.

Outputs under AUDIT_DIR/&lt;run_id&gt;/:
causal/* interpret/* species/* simengine/* algebra/* tensor/* twin/*
and updated termination.json.
Offline, deterministic IDs, strict typing, proof/counterexample gates, no stubs.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9d13-f8076427d4c2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-aedb-cacabd5a6980" class="">Next missing layer after v6 (if you want “More” again)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a57e-ebfc31417631" class="">The next gaps are usually:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-8149-d5d0362c6663" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Memory architecture</strong> (episodic/semantic/procedural separation + consolidation equations)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-b304-eb7094886f9c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Attention/control</strong> as constrained resource allocation (Lagrangian)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8fc7-c0e94df17e39" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Multi-agent governance</strong> (trust, negotiation, adversarial resilience)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-bcff-e974e926d90a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Market engine</strong> (if you still want forex): data pipeline + risk + execution + compliance gating</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-83e4-f9b9329c1b66" class="">If you want the next expansion, reply with one token:<br/><code>MEMORY</code> or <code>ATTENTION</code> or <code>MULTIAGENT</code> or <code>MARKET</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-baba-ea9e15f46c69" class="">Overlooked invariants (with equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-83a1-c476f54a4c18" class="">1) Referential transparency (where intended)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-981a-f38175b808e1" class="">If a function is declared “pure”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9e40-ce785737de72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x,\ s:\ f(x; s)=f(x; s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9277-cc6bb7baed92" class="">2) Deterministic serialization / canonical form</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-95b2-e1a833efd018" class="">For a canonical serializer :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-9771-c01410aab23c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ \sigma(x)=\sigma(x)\ \text{and}\ \sigma^{-1}(\sigma(x)) \equiv x</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-876b-cf338df03e7c" class="">3) Hash / signature stability (domain-separated)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-aed7-dab5079d36fa" class="">Let  be a hash and  a domain tag:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-97a6-d8a8b5a9f0ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ H(d \,\|\, x)\neq H(d&#x27; \,\|\, x)\ \text{for}\ d\neq d&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8566-ca42b7a3922b" class="">4) Monotonic versioning</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b5e7-c5adc5c7cf4f" class="">For versions :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a5ef-c7648e5b8630" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall i&lt;j:\ v_i &lt; v_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-9160-e6f25608c4e7" class="">5) Idempotency key uniqueness window</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b24e-cc1df7e56013" class="">Let  be idempotency keys used in window :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-963f-ee26857031eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall k\in K_W:\ \text{count}(k)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9a80-e0af54953a53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{apply}(k,x)\ \text{is at-most-once within}\ W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-be68-ffab5a6473f2" class="">6) At-least-once delivery ⇒ idempotent handler</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-b59b-e3d44043a74d" class="">If delivery can repeat, handler  must satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-be89-c77744ec3172" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h(h(s,e),e)=h(s,e)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9ad5-dca71bfc86dc" class="">7) Exactly-once effect via dedupe set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-ad64-f489bbda7bfc" class="">Let  be processed event IDs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-abbb-eea66435a408" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e.id \in D \Rightarrow \text{no-op}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-ba06-d64e75b3c9f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D&#x27; = D \cup \{e.id\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-9636-c9b4208b92a9" class="">8) Invariant-preserving refactors (behavioral equivalence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-8171-c4dd34fb7d96" class="">For observable behavior function :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-b2cc-d8cf3b98d92d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{O}(P_{\text{before}})=\mathcal{O}(P_{\text{after}})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b23e-e98206b618d2" class="">9) Error invariants (stable error taxonomy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b1be-cdf12be3a826" class="">Let  be error codes produced by API :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-bf77-f4426d70a073" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E(A)\subseteq E_{\text{declared}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a01b-fa37e2b9564d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall e:\ \text{schema}(e)\ \text{is stable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8766-fe0618668035" class="">10) Retry invariants (bounded amplification)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-a648-df17d247ff14" class="">Let retry count be  and max :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-abaf-d47e0cd90436" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
0 \le r \le R</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a3d4-e208c79f3e98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta t_{i+1} \ge \Delta t_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8dad-cc78d6db01a4" class="">11) Time invariants: no backward wall-clock dependence</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-b206-fe9bdc59dfe4" class="">Use monotonic clock :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-99d5-f69cb591e8f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t_m(i+1)\ge t_m(i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-aee0-d31946b3453c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t_w\ \text{may decrease} \Rightarrow \text{no correctness dependence on}\ t_w</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9cf2-d4a0fb08d5ee" class="">12) Floating point tolerance invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-a700-ec25c4c8e460" class="">For numeric comparisons, define tolerance :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9de4-c1191390c50c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|a-b|\le \epsilon \Rightarrow a \approx b</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b22b-e80b180efd62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(a+b)+c \ne a+(b+c)\ \text{in floating point}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-afaf-c40836eaa81f" class="">13) Units invariants (dimension correctness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-88a9-f4fa66b08fd9" class="">For quantity  with units :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9306-e46e584da073" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(a)=U(b)\Rightarrow a\pm b\ \text{valid}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b7f5-db56896b494a" class="">U(a)\cdot U(b)=U(a\times b)<br/><br/>Prevents silent unit-mismatch bugs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b0b4-c5dab3fa924e" class="">14) Character encoding invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-b16e-c2626f95cc61" class="">All boundary I/O must satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-b67f-d99af2510bee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{decode}(\text{encode}(s,\text{utf8}),\text{utf8})=s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-9715-c154d19a0393" class="">15) Locale invariants (case-folding, sorting)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8ac0-e2c6f09e00cf" class="">If equality is required, avoid locale-sensitive transforms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-acc2-f97ef5436628" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{casefold}(s)\ \text{is used, not locale lowercasing}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-8cb2-d3a260ff6d34" class="">16) Pagination invariants (no duplicates / no gaps)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-895e-e4536fd1382f" class="">For cursor pagination returning item IDs sequence :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8ae8-dfe8203b88c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{page }p}\cap I_{\text{page }p+1}=\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-81b5-d2d6c629c095" class="">\bigcup_p I_{\text{page }p} = I_{\text{all}} \quad (\text{within snapshot semantics})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8235-f6ec712a6047" class="">17) Snapshot semantics under concurrent writes</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-8839-e97a5de9521e" class="">If you claim snapshot isolation for a query :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-a0aa-d4c7518ffe7c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q(t_0)\ \text{observes state }S(t_0)\ \text{even if commits occur during execution}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8b53-c176d9d8f56c" class="">18) Cache correctness invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-9840-c9a6bc750b2b" class="">For cache  of function :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-844e-f499bb900fd0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C[x]=f(x)\ \text{must imply}\ \text{invalidate when dependencies change}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a7f3-d632337f84ea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta Dep(x)\neq 0 \Rightarrow \text{invalidate}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-aade-d0d19b9f2bf6" class="">19) Cache key invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8eb5-de84d50f8f6d" class="">If key is :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-88a8-e60304e51781" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_1\neq x_2 \Rightarrow k(x_1)\neq k(x_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-825a-e70793b07271" class="">20) Permission invariants across layers (no “auth gaps”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8587-cca5a65cec05" class="">For any privileged action :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-846e-c949f1bf8158" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{UI allows}(op)\Rightarrow \text{API checks}(op)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-9514-c9efc5bfe436" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{API checks}(op)\ \text{must not be weaker than UI gating}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8be7-d10da44d9c7d" class="">21) Logging invariants (no secrets)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-aab8-fba8b74c3a16" class="">Let  be log output and  secrets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-a0f9-ff5804ec5709" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \cap S = \varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-9318-f74064b455f4" class="">22) Observability invariants (correlation IDs)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-94e5-fee852464d5d" class="">For request  with correlation ID :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-84e8-c7dce0744032" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall \text{log/trace/metric emitted by }r:\ cid = cid(r)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-80bd-cb60eb87b35b" class="">23) Migration invariants (reversible or forward-only declared)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-94e1-e9a85d38e69c" class="">If reversible migration  with inverse :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-b2f4-dd2391e2387c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^{-1}(m(x))=x</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-a220-ff1ddbf691d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^{-1}\ \text{undefined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-aab0-ce442270ec54" class="">24) Schema evolution invariants (backward compatibility window)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-9155-e555b9980330" class="">If you promise compatibility for window :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-bc58-d39c36f8049a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t\in W:\ \text{new writer} \rightarrow \text{old reader works}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9334-cbaee48d2b2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{old writer} \rightarrow \text{new reader works}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9b25-dd4c308d5e8b" class="">25) Boundaries invariants (input validation at trust boundary)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a575-d610b059b269" class="">For boundary function :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9783-f66678ee5aed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ \neg \text{valid}(x)\Rightarrow \neg \text{process}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-816c-cc7422a7fc28" class="">26) “No partial commit” invariant (transactionality)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-8096-e44c50920c64" class="">For a transaction  producing writes :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b1ae-c34cf7b28a7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{commit}(T)\Rightarrow \bigwedge_{i=1}^{n} w_i\ \text{visible}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-bb72-caa6bdc84c80" class="">\neg \text{commit}(T)\Rightarrow \bigwedge_{i=1}^{n} w_i\ \text{not visible}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-97b6-f675b387780a" class="">27) “Single source of truth” invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-88dd-dfdb5e86a946" class="">If  is authoritative and  derived:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a563-c2fd5c0a1cd6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B = g(A)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-b827-eeb471e08b2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta A \Rightarrow \Delta B\ \text{via }g,\ \text{not independent writes}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-a225-df26dae7bb54" class="">28) Configuration invariants (no hidden defaults)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b7ef-e852e5fa8023" class="">For config  with required keys :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-adb2-d8ee8eaed74c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R\subseteq K(C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-ad31-e1dd3f13f6d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{\text{effective}}(k)=\max_{\prec}\{C_{\text{cli}},C_{\text{env}},C_{\text{file}},C_{\text{default}}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-bb74-c02fe014f37f" class="">29) Rate-limit invariants (fairness / no starvation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-bc91-ea35bf1d84ec" class="">If users  share capacity :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b0e2-d1ac3c94d0d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_u usage(u,t)\le Cap(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-81ac-cac04fdb3843" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
usage(u,t)\ \text{bounded below by policy if active}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-81af-db69abc0cbee" class="">30) “No silent data loss” invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8cf4-c8657bbf9841" class="">If parse/transform  may drop fields, it must be explicit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-b4d5-f4517a4c2793" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{drop}(f)\Rightarrow \text{emit warning/metric}(f)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9443-e7bfc12651cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{drop}(f)\Rightarrow f\in \text{declared\_discard\_set}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bd80-d3a36cb90f01"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a409-ff5a42ed010a" class="">If you want this operationalized: name the domain (web API, distributed system, ML pipeline, compiler, fintech ledger). I will produce a minimal invariant set for that domain, each with: equation → enforcement mechanism (type/test/DB constraint/runtime guard/CI gate).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-8ea2-c91b2aed4caf" class="">Below are <strong>additional, often-missed invariants</strong>, grouped by domain, expressed formally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-9dc0-db958ac508b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9293-d61665d35c5f" class="">A) Distributed Systems Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-86c0-d395bf4e9593" class="">1) Consensus safety (no two leaders simultaneously)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a05e-f01deccefae4" class="">Let  be leaders at time :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-950c-c488d8e7a519" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t:\ |L(t)| \le 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-842d-cff35f81df2e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8f72-d44cc4214e01" class="">2) Log consistency (Raft-style)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-b21b-fc5b1284b06d" class="">If two logs share an entry at index  with term , then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-915d-de7a585aeb7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall j &lt; i:\ \text{log}_1[j] = \text{log}_2[j]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-b850-d6c34b3c6b2e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a6e4-cb0636b989cb" class="">3) Eventual consistency convergence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-94b8-cfb4804d1ac4" class="">For replicas :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-bd6b-f6d34640c44d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lim_{t \to \infty} state(R_i,t) = state(R_j,t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b044-e8f41c634bf2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9eab-c5e525449fcf" class="">4) No duplicate side effects under retries</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-ac81-f5f2d745d251" class="">For operation :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-98f3-d5e53e59e129" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
effect(op, s) = effect(op, effect(op, s))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-88db-e99d39b410c3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-a318-f99f33060dd5" class="">5) Monotonic reads (session guarantee)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-85e6-d326e1908af3" class="">If client observes version , then later:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8909-ccc67a3c9c50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
v_2 \ge v_1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-abef-da398c77c3c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-b657-d0319dcc2ae6" class="">B) Numerical / Algorithmic Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-baaf-e65259a0b4b4" class="">6) Loop invariant (classic but underused)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-bb18-c3e92859f17f" class="">For loop index :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-ab25-e75d68f23d9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(i) \text{ holds before iteration } i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-9ba5-e1b884c5fcfa" class="">and</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-b01b-deafdf694deb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(i) \Rightarrow P(i+1)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a15c-f9172d4353c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-aeac-f9c88b458c5a" class="">7) Sorting invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-97ea-ec2aba869375" class="">For sorted array :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b155-d262f66319fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall i&lt;j:\ A[i] \le A[j]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-afac-ef38b9dfd9f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-912e-fa4f3bc7e01d" class="">8) Binary search invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b75a-df18cd7db8f8" class="">At iteration :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9c3d-d452c61d1bfb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
target \in A[low_k, high_k]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-8149-d7c5b14bd107"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-9dbb-f87b0e0c3cdc" class="">9) Probability normalization</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9c28-ce7f5784ba96" class="">For probability vector :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-aa8e-cbaf1b23feb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_i p_i = 1 \quad \wedge \quad p_i \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-8b16-d9e54742997c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b1e5-ca1ccbeaea37" class="">10) Gradient descent stability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a3e9-f214303d7619" class="">For learning rate :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-bf5f-fd078ea1fb0c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\eta &lt; \frac{2}{L}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-a45e-da57437ac4b8" class="">Where  is Lipschitz constant (ensures convergence for convex case).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-b80c-cd6af11dd034"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8c73-ebb00593ee70" class="">C) ML / Data Pipeline Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-82e9-e442c3a79e20" class="">11) Train/test separation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-a42b-f13f7a02da5e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
train \cap test = \varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8da4-d68d76827b71"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-bd4d-f763712c277f" class="">12) Feature consistency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b6d9-f14dac84851c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
schema_{train} = schema_{inference}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-90bb-d3748bcaabc6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a3e1-c509ccaefcdf" class="">13) Label leakage invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b556-f031be69215c" class="">No feature  may satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9f31-dd9ed0b59003" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
f \Rightarrow y \text{ deterministically post-target timestamp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-ab5e-db8cac9f218c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8874-f6a1086ec8b6" class="">14) Model determinism under seed</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-bc7f-ff1de66014b5" class="">Given seed :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-a822-f376445ab7cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
model(s, data) = model(s, data)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-82c0-e8785e53afc5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9c21-d34b294860a7" class="">15) Drift detection threshold</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-ab7d-c4691dff7f5d" class="">If distribution  and baseline :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-91e9-d70ff9128a6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
KL(D_t \parallel D_0) &gt; \tau \Rightarrow trigger\_alert</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-aef2-ec5c96174a79"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-88cf-cfb63935ce74" class="">D) Cryptographic Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9e84-c277c9fbe770" class="">16) Nonce uniqueness (per key)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-9684-c13864026afd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall n_i \ne n_j \text{ under key } k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a708-ee8f51cd87ad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-8cdb-f5ccde6dba53" class="">17) Constant-time comparison</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-92f2-e12e90a1fffe" class="">Execution time independent of input equality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-bbf0-fe481c0a7c51" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(x,y) = T(x&#x27;,y&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-8d70-e3d07b78b377"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-a592-ca47844ff603" class="">18) Forward secrecy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-adad-f9a109a1eea3" class="">Compromise of long-term key  does not imply recovery of past session keys .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b31a-fa73d4955402" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-afc6-c05fcadc4843" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K_L \not\Rightarrow K_{S_i}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9a5b-f5bbffc8604a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-81f4-ed52c3655a3e" class="">E) API / Microservice Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-8d4c-c2bd84a5fe37" class="">19) Backward compatibility invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a08b-c95f81e7b5d3" class="">If version  deployed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-aa7c-e76383ab19a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall client_v:\ response_{v+1} \text{ parsable by } client_v</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-808c-cf9b86cbc654"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-bd29-f3f4bae91c51" class="">20) Idempotent PUT semantics</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8db6-f9e9b8f4e3fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PUT(x) \Rightarrow state = x</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-91bc-f88bcc758cf6" class="">Repeated calls:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-beb4-c3e21b50908a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PUT(x) = PUT(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a71c-d2324b0a9743"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-8127-eb397636f930" class="">21) Rate limiting envelope</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-acc0-e825efe03e0f" class="">For window :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8ad7-ca0c3779989d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
requests(u,W) \le limit(u,W)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-806f-f5c113b3f044"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b28a-ef47307a872f" class="">F) Build / DevOps Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a247-d896c559dcda" class="">22) Reproducible build</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-90a1-d31b499cc28a" class="">Given source  and environment :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-b2f0-e3d0a22f46b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
build(S,E) = artifact</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-a5b7-d3a20486639f" class="">Deterministic across runs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-be8a-d115b16d9776" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
build(S,E) = build(S,E)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a968-f75af28ecc84"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-96b1-ecb0e27b7f9d" class="">23) Dependency immutability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-83ed-e69b4f77e02b" class="">If lockfile :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-bafb-e4ed4641cd70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
deps(L) = deps(L)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9c50-dfd34f6a0260" class="">No floating versions allowed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-9900-d781ff5fff1e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8bd9-f666035c840c" class="">24) Test completeness invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b8da-e192baf50c58" class="">For critical module :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-a127-fa25b9cca1ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
coverage(M) \ge \tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-a465-d97ae808f68c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-93b4-e4e7a777df8b" class="">G) UX / Product Invariants (often ignored)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-825a-de912f7b468d" class="">25) Undo reversibility</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-95a7-d88e66783e85" class="">If action :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-9e81-ec2235d31648" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
undo(a(a(s))) = a(s)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-adf8-cde3d9c63608"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-bbcc-d63e262265d1" class="">26) Idempotent form submission</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-aa2d-cf6a90666eb0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
submit(x) = submit(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9904-e4ac412929ce" class="">No duplicate effects.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-ac7c-cb0ad4b21729"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9ec9-ca01e8e36440" class="">H) Memory / Systems Programming</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8074-eaa0af84d69c" class="">27) Ownership invariant (Rust-style)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a178-cefbaf56f206" class="">For resource :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b5b9-c857a62dd9a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\text{owners}(r)| \le 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b2b5-c163d02bd34c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a431-eb0fc9f9d763" class="">28) Reference count invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-8ea5-fc91675b1c2e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
refcount(r) = |\text{live references to } r|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-a399-c0b092f3409e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-83d4-d5510d1039ba" class="">29) No use-after-free</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-b7be-cd10712cbc80" class="">If  at time :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-87a9-fa4c1a215df7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t&#x27; &gt; t:\ access(r) = \bot</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-90a7-dbd1cb2c6970"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8f1c-ea85f83c87d2" class="">I) Financial / Ledger Systems</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8dcd-c6baadf76b49" class="">30) Double-entry balance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a908-f3231642a304" class="">For transaction :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-9115-fad44016c1fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum debits(T) = \sum credits(T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-b2dc-d6389b51422e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-b717-e5450f5028c0" class="">31) Conservation of total funds</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-ba84-f10836dbe181" class="">For ledger :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b73d-f94bf9aaec30" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum balances(t+1) = \sum balances(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-a7b2-e0967afba195" class="">(excluding mint/burn events explicitly modeled)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-a40b-ee659663f6c8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-aeff-d543dbc308b2" class="">J) Formal Specification Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-94c2-c94920a19ce7" class="">32) Safety property</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a54b-eb0438365db9" class="">Something bad never happens:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a53b-ed166b7c4b00" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Box \neg bad</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-934b-c049a940e857"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-89c8-c8f0f7d9f9d2" class="">33) Liveness property</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-ac6e-d7b66c5fcbb5" class="">Something good eventually happens:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a72a-d3a81ba8aebb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Box \Diamond good</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-8b90-e4d2cfde91a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-bf87-efd89f15b20f" class="">K) Meta-Invariant (Often the Most Overlooked)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a7f8-f5cd995e8538" class="">34) Invariant about invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-95ad-c466c7f97eed" class="">Every invariant must have:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-bcac-fe75aa05d8f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-b90e-d2576dc2c923" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforcement mechanism</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-b6b7-ff299a337194" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Failure mode</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-8991-c4d04bfc0f10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Monitoring signal</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8897-d42a9f38421e" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-ae8c-c3eee12dfc3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Invariant \Rightarrow (S \land E \land F \land M)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-8d11-cac36c0bb307" class="">Without this, invariants decay silently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-ac1c-ecd1ca361a0e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-999a-c6b6cc159d83" class="">If you want, I can now:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-9988-d3ce0355817a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Map these into a MECE invariant framework for a specific system type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-ab14-e27413fabd7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or formalize them into a single invariant lattice model</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a7ac-c2e1c2a55785" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or derive the minimal invariant basis for a high-reliability system</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-a733-f66ec9526ab4" class="">Specify domain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a405-e25fffe831b3" class="">State invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-b0ca-e99d800f24a1" class="">Balance non-negative</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b224-fe18b88280c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall a \in \text{Accounts}:\ \text{balance}(a) \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b182-f87ded98535f" class="">Closed object consistency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-b28c-e850299cba87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ \text{is\_closed}(x)=\text{True} \Rightarrow \text{closed\_at}(x)\neq \varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9a86-c081004c1dd2" class="">Finite-state machine legality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-ba73-ef6a4e1f49a2" class="">Let  be states,  allowed transitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8328-e0c301879630" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall (s_i \to s_{i+1})\ \text{in a run}:\ (s_i, s_{i+1}) \in T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-b899-e4186b6c2b02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-ac8f-e3fa5f431221" class="">Data structure invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-bd4b-c7d1a853ba18" class="">Array bounds safety</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8748-e356470a1467" class="">For array  of length :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-bb76-c777b609e43b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall i:\ 0 \le i &lt; n \Rightarrow A[i]\ \text{is defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a7fb-d4a004fe5b8f" class="">Stack size matches content</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-bb38-f461ed0c268b" class="">Let  be the list in the stack.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bbf5-f5252bdd289a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{size}(stk)=|\text{items}(stk)|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-86d1-d6e211fd9de0" class="">Queue conservation under enqueue/dequeue</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-b258-cbd279d6aba8" class="">If :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-acb7-fb482f9fa269" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|q&#x27;| = |q| + 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-aa0f-f6fc3a83f5c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|q&#x27;| = |q| - 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-a069-f187d2009174"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-a707-d51d5a54c3b0" class="">Type/shape invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8f1f-db099fa79d73" class="">Homogeneous list</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9c53-cbf1cf34a23a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall i:\ \text{type}(L_i)=\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-bf00-d9ce81671961" class="">JSON required keys</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-841e-f079b90381ce" class="">If required key set is  and payload keys are :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-992b-e472856e7afe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R \subseteq K(p)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-ab24-d0f233d11b64"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-a731-c2559d93d93b" class="">Database/data integrity invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-a1be-cccb8117bf70" class="">Primary key uniqueness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-9620-fda7fc4d4e50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall r_1\neq r_2 \in \text{Table}:\ \text{pk}(r_1)\neq \text{pk}(r_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-ac74-c8aca49f309b" class="">Foreign key validity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-83c3-ced0e0493ab1" class="">If  references parent keys :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-b2f7-d5f3fa28890b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c \in \text{Child}:\ fk(c) \in PK(\text{Parent})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9e5c-c9003bf8b438" class="">Check constraint example</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-86ac-fd06e6d9ed82" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall o \in \text{Orders}:\ o.\text{total} = \sum_{i\in o.\text{items}} i.\text{price}\cdot i.\text{qty}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-ae20-cedeabb0b0da"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9bd9-e540ea7de5ce" class="">Temporal invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-aa61-c40376553f61" class="">Created time precedes updated time</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-9f37-fb7d9732f877" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ \text{created\_at}(x) \le \text{updated\_at}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9f3a-d5dbb9436521" class="">Per-key event ordering</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9253-c4ee93ecd317" class="">For events  with key  and sequence :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-9261-c54a9c382f93" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
k(e_1)=k(e_2)\ \wedge\ seq(e_1) &lt; seq(e_2)\ \Rightarrow\ \text{process}(e_1)\prec \text{process}(e_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-aff7-dbc88b2c5e41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-afe5-c3207feac983" class="">Concurrency invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-aad6-dfaf8aa6138e" class="">Mutual exclusion (single writer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-82a7-d927b7c0821f" class="">Let  be writers holding the lock at time :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b4f2-ddf157710850" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t:\ |W(t)| \le 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-8b98-d3b174e9da1f" class="">Deadlock-avoidance via lock ordering</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-99c4-e743600bb7fb" class="">Let  be a total order on locks; a thread acquires locks in sequence :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8bfc-fdfbba581f4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall j&lt; m:\ order(L_j) &lt; order(L_{j+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a991-c50f18405fa2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-b025-fcbf6297797b" class="">Resource invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-bc1e-e33c8fe80dd4" class="">“No leaked handles”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-995c-c0e729ac9111" class="">Let  be open handles at time . For a request starting at  and ending :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-989b-f0baf1cdeab3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Open(t_1)=Open(t_0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8a2e-e11e98420ea5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8c69-c087b6517b14" class="">Security invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8070-d27a8dd6a238" class="">Authorization gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-89cb-f868ae7fc08a" class="">For any privileged operation  requiring permission :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-83a0-f665a917e520" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall u,op:\ \text{execute}(u,op) \Rightarrow perm(op)\in \text{Perms}(u)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-9b4e-e0d592fee5bb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-bc19-d4cc3eccfa54" class="">Correctness invariants for functions</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9ec4-e0dcf6328178" class="">Precondition/postcondition contract</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9f3a-ca5093c137eb" class="">For function  with precondition  and postcondition :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-acba-db76e67d746d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
