P(x)\Rightarrow Q(x, f(x))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-af8f-db49eba04f79" class="">Idempotency (same input → same effect)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a2ea-cfc333815623" class="">Let  be the state change function induced by operation :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-8abe-fabab5848d18" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta(s, op(x)) = \Delta(\Delta(s, op(x)), op(x))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-ac15-f188c454ba65"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a817-c807cfcc4d29" class="">If you tell me the context (e.g., “payment ledger”, “user auth service”, “compiler”, “distributed queue”) I can output a minimal invariant set as equations plus the exact enforcement mechanism for each (types/tests/DB constraints/runtime guards/CI gates).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-94a5-fc11683f9fe4" class="">Yes. Programming has invariants at multiple layers. An invariant is a property that must remain true for the program to be correct within a defined scope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-ab04-dbaffaa32e28" class="">Core invariant categories in coding</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a025-f3768db26842" class="">1) Type and shape invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8aa0-e2d707d41206" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A value’s type is consistent with its declared/expected type.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-96bf-c6163f9dfab0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data structures maintain required shape.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b05e-eeca37c02ce2" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-b904-e33f3bed1818" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>user_id</code> is always an integer (or always a UUID string).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a86d-dc4932249f68" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A list contains only <code>Order</code> objects.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a415-e4ffc0b6f1d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A JSON payload always includes required keys.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a64e-c4e73b273ede" class="">2) State invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-aa45-ee947dd475c0" class="">Properties that must hold for an object/module across all valid states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-a794-fcfce9ee3cbe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If <code>is_closed == True</code>, then <code>closed_at != None</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-817c-ebfdfe4167c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Account balance never negative (unless overdraft is allowed).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-b859-e4358d071f4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A queue’s <code>size</code> equals the number of elements stored.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a668-eed8c5566305" class="">3) Control-flow invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a34b-f0317512ae53" class="">Rules about when code paths may execute.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-af3a-ee23f3f75820" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“This function must never be called before initialization.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-8896-e20d21df031b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“This branch must only run after authentication.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-adaa-c37870a873b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Retries stop after N attempts.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a2de-d5e4b3a902b5" class="">4) Resource invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-bc97-fb525a0f13c9" class="">Safety rules about resources.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a276-c87ca30abdc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every opened file/socket is eventually closed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a70d-caad3d1d7706" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No memory is referenced after free (manual memory languages).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b0f3-cc1f49501dd1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Database connections are returned to pool.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-9589-f3680ea05509" class="">5) Concurrency invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-acf9-f7e9657ee305" class="">Rules that prevent races and inconsistent shared state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-b879-daee856c5ba4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only one writer at a time.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-ae41-c86a638ecde9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Locks are acquired in a consistent order (avoids deadlocks).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b206-d8221951288f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Operations are atomic where required.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-bece-ee2f15591ca1" class="">6) Data integrity invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8bfe-fca290939d04" class="">Rules that preserve correctness of stored data.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-bc30-cebbd8c90d72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Database primary key uniqueness.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8d79-d42edfa8e94f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Foreign keys always reference existing rows.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-b707-ec19463634d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Idempotency keys prevent duplicate writes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-a07f-fcd5018312b6" class="">7) Security invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-90cb-cf80b1e40de1" class="">Rules that must never be violated.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-afe8-da5864527b74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Secrets never logged.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8a4f-d2551e86a083" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Authorization is checked on every privileged operation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8aab-fa9a4d6dfe9d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Input validation is enforced at boundaries.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-93a1-d0d2d7189d8b" class="">8) Interface and contract invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-986e-d4ecb302330c" class="">Rules about APIs between modules/services.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-aa53-c20187d1d65f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Function preconditions/postconditions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-956d-e200f5ebff6d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Backward compatibility rules.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a186-effe7384b313" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Error format invariants (stable error codes/messages).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8604-f5b5cbad708d" class="">9) Temporal invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-ac10-e897d33be24d" class="">Rules about time ordering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a050-dd60180eec9a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Events are processed in order per key.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8732-c2bb1ef2d322" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Expirations and TTLs are enforced consistently.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b0f6-c1a6e7ddd5bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Created time ≤ updated time” always holds.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9730-e64e1a660a41" class="">10) Build and deployment invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8f49-e40abf83d24d" class="">Rules that keep the system reproducible and safe to ship.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-8ca4-ffadb06705e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tests must pass before merge.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9e31-c79047c65006" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic builds (same input → same artifact).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-93eb-fd88d4dddc90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dependency versions pinned.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-bb50-ea764cb50d70" class="">How invariants are enforced in practice</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-a72a-ca63ee18af53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Types</strong>: static typing (TypeScript, Rust), runtime checks (Python pydantic)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-ae90-d7290ee10397" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Assertions</strong>: <code>assert</code>, defensive checks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-9cff-f7002e355b89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tests</strong>: unit/property/integration tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8e9b-f8fa5736a7f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Static analysis</strong>: linters, security scanners</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-855d-e59506f66ab2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Formal methods</strong> (where needed): model checking, contracts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-befd-dd7e128730e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Runtime guards</strong>: feature flags, circuit breakers, rate limits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-b306-eebdff415897" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Database constraints</strong>: unique, foreign key, check constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-804f-d2708ecb3119" class="">Practical definition you can use</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9505-fcb7fbd2873c" class="">An invariant is “a rule that, if broken, means the program is wrong—not just slow or ugly.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a7bc-edb4e857dfe6" class="">If you tell me what you are building (language + domain), I can list the minimal invariant set (MECE) and map each invariant to an enforcement mechanism (type, test, runtime guard, DB constraint, CI gate).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8088-b1d1-e013ad324e6b" class="">If you say “more” again without selecting, the next step should be (2), because it is the only part not yet explicitly formalized as a uniform lemma schema.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8006-ba6c-d92fda3c0738" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Non-interference proofs fully formalized</strong>: show each rewrite cannot increase any earlier component (mechanical if rewrites are record updates and witnesses depend only on untouched fields).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b883-fd4ad155640f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Proof-assistant theorem DAG rendered as Lean/Coq stubs</strong> for the full index above (every lemma named, dependencies explicit).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9fa6-e156f537fd68" class="">8) What “more” can still mean (only two directions remain)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-ada1-dfd8f120b9ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-bd8a-cf228d482e24" class="">This bound is computable directly from .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-840b-c09799eeb8e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T \le C_0 + Y_0 + M_0 + O_0 + B_0 + R_0 + Q_0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8043-ac02-defd830a54c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_0=n_{\mathrm{con}},\ Y_0=n_{\mathrm{cyc}},\ M_0=n_{\mathrm{mb}},\ O_0=n_{\mathrm{own}},\ B_0=n_{\mathrm{bud}},\ R_0=n_{\mathrm{met}},\ Q_0=n_{\mathrm{mece}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802c-957e-ddfe073a8c3b" class="">Let initial witness counts be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a1cb-d4427004bf1a" class="">7) Explicit step bound (computable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fa-99f1-ff5d694c904f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802e-8e6f-f415bc664b6d" class="">Reason: no infinite descending chain exists in .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8035-b0e6-c4fcd18129dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma_N)=(0,0,0,0,0,0,0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-a52d-f6daabe22cbc" class="">From any initial , repeated application of <code>step</code> terminates in finite steps at  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-a63b-fb736a7c13cb" class="">Theorem (Termination)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-830b-f712ccfbb89c" class="">6) Termination theorem (full system)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-816f-dd3d7ed9c044"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a8-962a-d7591823e58e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Therefore lex decreases.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b3e1-cd6e037e8d5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Earlier components are unchanged (by write-set non-interference + side-condition arguments).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802b-8e92-c8216040eb75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">For that rewrite, the corresponding strict decrease lemma holds.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b45c-f79bbf47aa98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">By failcode priority, the chosen rewrite targets the earliest non-zero component of .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-ab8c-f1d4509aebf8" class="">Proof outline:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a2b9-fd482520fd10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma&#x27;) \prec_{lex} \Phi(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805d-a5ea-fa631c67adde" class="">If  is not sealed and , then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-b705-d2c6da7e4778" class="">Theorem (Progress)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8020-a159-cfcec89fcb9b" class="">5) Strict progress theorem (global lex decrease)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bbc8-fbe04e249d45"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8045-8773-f04aac5755a9" class="">Reason: failcode selection is total over the evaluated invariant list, and mapping  is total over failure codes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8048-b09c-d2a1aa8ae252" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg Sealed(\Sigma)\Rightarrow \exists!\ \mathcal{R}_j\in\mathcal{R}:\ \mathcal{R}_j=\mathcal{R}^*(failcode(\Sigma))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-9b2e-fc91ba9e8693" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-a198-fbf476589bf7" class="">If  is not sealed, then <code>failcode(Σ)</code> maps to exactly one rewrite in the library.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803f-9115-e13bb45b18b4" class="">Theorem (Coverage)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-805e-a30f-d673b680b5f5" class="">4) Completeness theorem (every failure is actionable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806f-8179-f46bc670c471"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-9211-ea556911db1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Canonical partition is MECE by construction, so it does not create a new MECE defect for that object.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a665-cc8bd80b4f24" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a30c-e35226f62ad8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^*\notin W_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma&#x27;) \Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{mece}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-88c6-d2be2fe15dd8" class="">Assuming  (required by construction):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8064-8fe3-dfee296834ed" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e7-aaaa-e08c2fb5fd76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only that MECE object’s bin list .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8a10-f2c7d6c41a0d" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-a785-fb4cf1353db4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R008: choose and overwrite its bins with canonical partition for .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8092-ae4a-fc59b7660316" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-bac4-d7e2a70de8be" class="">n_{\mathrm{mece}}=|W_{\mathrm{mece}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-89a5-f8dc6cdd49ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{mece}}=\{y\in\mathcal{Y}:\neg MECE(U_y,\mathcal{D}_y)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8061-832f-c4016356f2e4" class="">For each MECE object  with finite universe  and bins :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8084-9024-e7f054ef5031" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-894f-fd734ae28b69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0301–F0303 (collapsed to one MECE defect class for termination)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a3b1-e5bf2ce7e4b3" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-868e-edc572bec04e" class="">3.7 MECE (Rule-of-2/4)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d7-83be-c0fb018549f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802a-a53a-e88d6424c7b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Blocking a claim removes transform usage; it cannot create new illegal transform witnesses.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8093-aac8-f49d4dd0074a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Completing a metric cannot create “incomplete metric” witnesses if completion is total.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-81ae-da9f2e8896ac" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-a9a3-e022c29e390c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|W_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma&#x27;)| &lt; |W_{\mathrm{tr}}(\Sigma)|
\Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-948d-c0165ede66a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|W_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma&#x27;)| &lt; |W_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)|
\Rightarrow n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma&#x27;)&lt;n_{\mathrm{met}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-aad4-df560f115fb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007a:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9ae2-dc3493beb80e" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-a171-ea3f708c7c52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007b: only one claim’s status (or transform list).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8bc6-e8311a19a213" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007a: only one metric record’s fields.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8009-ae06-c0c1e6fcab54" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804b-88a2-e7bc0fc13406" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007b else if : block the first offending claim (or remove transform reference).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-ada7-fbd50bb93a82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R007a if : complete the first incomplete metric using policy defaults .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8079-a0df-fb21f87ce98d" class="">Rewrite (deterministic split)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bee7-ec79f0f93504" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{met}} = |W_{\mathrm{met}}| + |W_{\mathrm{tr}}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8004-94bb-d5234f9d2bd0" class="">Total metric defect count:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-90c2-f50d0496e5ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{tr}}=\{(c,m,T): c\in V, m\in c.metrics, T\in UsedTransforms(c,m), T\notin Allowed(m)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808f-a33c-e21b742b8065" class="">Illegal transform uses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8008-9a6b-e4fe82e0b4b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{met}}=\{m\in M:\neg MetricComplete(m)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a784-c00f12b22cfe" class="">Incomplete metrics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bd01-d8894d27c175" class="">Witness set (two parts)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-aeb8-f46d3097be35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0201–F0204 (collapsed into one “metric defect class” for termination)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ee-892a-c5ab315210db" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8090-85dc-fce9b23a6b08" class="">3.6 Metric completeness + illegal transforms</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-ab59-f9131e01caac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-8e1e-f2c8bd8d9b59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No other fields change; earlier components unchanged.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-82c2-c66e17f81565" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b288-f4edfd4afba0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma&#x27;) = 0 &lt; 1 = n_{\mathrm{bud}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-891d-daa7a40031b7" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-8306-f343d663045d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only <code>etype</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8015-938f-ee9e5c43666e" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-bb3d-dcb7497ebe0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R010: set <code>etype := MB</code> (tag; no evidence fabricated).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-aae0-ddab219b352b" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8590-d672c5e82108" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{bud}}=
\begin{cases}
1 &amp; K(\hat{E})&gt;b \wedge etype\neq MB\\
0 &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804d-89b1-c58a677c3043" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8031-9aa8-c31042d468c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0501</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ad-a6e6-cf51c48e1758" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-894a-e11ea2d0c170" class="">3.5 Epistemic budget (no overreach without MB tagging)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b2db-c154d8a64c26"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8017-aa35-f2b15e9e0215" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Restricted cannot be treated as exportable; this does not create contradiction/cycle/MB/budget/metric/mece witnesses.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808b-99ed-e75acd951517" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b4fe-e1bc822bcf2d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{own}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a7cd-eacfe6aaac33" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-81ea-f28eb12acbe9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only <code>Claim.own</code> for one claim.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a6d0-d825a48cd385" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-8fd1-c471d89d386b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R009: choose ; set .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8036-9654-e95fbd6bbe8e" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8054-bb58-e3e5370f3c42" class="">n_{\mathrm{own}}=|W_{\mathrm{own}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8070-9525-e5342242e62b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{own}}=\{c\in V:\ own(c)=Unknown\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a156-c4b20f8f5967" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8042-88b6-c2aaf553960f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0401</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801a-ad23-e07382aa320c" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-9eb9-c3a08afdde58" class="">3.4 Ownership (no Unknown)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-8a15-c030b4796788"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-b60a-d2a89c0e7cc2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nothing else changes; no earlier defect component changes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-a844-f8cb1f27bfe4" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8016-8cd9-eb73cf5b4060" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{mb}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-bd6d-c550b8ef5e55" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8549-db22fc17cf5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-a147-df35dbe67d22" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-8877-c01d405dca4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R005: choose ; set .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801c-84a2-ff8ef157b4f7" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8091-bbe2-d2720463a94c" class="">n_{\mathrm{mb}}=|W_{\mathrm{mb}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808e-99d0-c63df4419d8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{mb}}=\{c\in V:\ stype(c)=MB \wedge c.id\notin \Lambda\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b0a5-fd6fe00bc4d9" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8067-80b1-ebbe9e13d157" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0105</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b90b-f30878e55385" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9b9b-d3ebeeec2573" class="">3.3 Allowance (MB claims must be allowed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b849-c0ffbb3c8d27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9f12-fa6d4ff1c8dc" class="">This guarantees acyclicity without SCC.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800b-b3ed-c6602ae657a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-b22a-fe89b825a42d" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-ae98-e18247c76f29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R002: pick violating edge, remove it, replace with Primitive boundary claim edge as above.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8083-9d76-ecfe42ba313a" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f5-ad4e-c5951425321b" class="">n_{\mathrm{cyc}}=|W_{\mathrm{cyc}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809f-841f-c2b9cbaec4ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{cyc}}=\{(a\to b)\in A : level(a)\ge level(b)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-ba14-c061a4cfba47" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-875e-f3d2cf6097fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0002 (same slot)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80eb-916f-d997db3c0af3" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807b-b83d-c861dbad085b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\mathrm{DAG}}:\ \forall (a\to b)\in A,\ level(a)&lt;level(b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a92b-f9d2c0b42984" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every claim has <code>level : Nat</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8089-a667-f668632c1581" class="">Replace SCC invariant with level invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804f-870e-fe4b9a96e50e" class="">Path B: DAG-by-construction (no SCC)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807d-9297-d64c6ad91f87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-85cd-f20a80f0941e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Contradictions not increased: new claim is Primitive with restrictive context; it does not create contradiction witnesses.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-89a2-fb906eb58287" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-82ce-c47a573a5934" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{cyc}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8025-8c9b-cbbed00dfa35" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8026-80ad-d6fc8eab7180" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only <code>Graph.A</code> plus addition of one new claim in <code>V</code> and one new edge.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8018-bfdc-f52402ce8ee9" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-907f-f4f8567f6623" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R002: select , pick deterministic internal edge , remove it, and insert a Primitive boundary claim so the dependent node keeps a support source without cyclic dependency.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d2-82fe-e86e5730846d" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80db-8217-cb4f8ec0fcd2" class="">n_{\mathrm{cyc}}=|W_{\mathrm{cyc}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8224-d29059653179" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{cyc}}=\{S\in SCC(g): \neg AllowedSCC(S)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803e-81a4-d4fb8ddb8825" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b342-d2d20d475db5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0002</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806e-b97a-f8ef49e3e661" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-bf60-fb4a79fa2d91" class="">Path A: SCC oracle (axiomatized)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b64c-d2a72d7dd2f3" class="">Two valid options.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8021-b1b5-f0cf3d761673" class="">3.2 Cycles / SCC</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bd82-cf8778dcfa89"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804c-98ef-c524f846b710" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No other claim changes; compatibility only becomes stricter for that claim, so no new contradictions are created.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806d-bdba-d590da1671eb" class="">Non-increase side condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-806b-8a08-fa16636e2e14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma&#x27;) &lt; n_{\mathrm{con}}(\Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8056-b629-e7eb57344017" class="">Strict decrease</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-8c36-f38126a5a76a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only <code>Claim.nf.ctx</code> for one selected claim.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8027-becc-f240e214c29e" class="">Write-set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809c-87f8-d13aeeb8d6a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R001: choose and add a context literal (e.g., <code>Unmeasured</code>) to to break compatibility.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f6-aa3c-ce2c383d6343" class="">Rewrite</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9cd5-d694af574a27" class="">n_{\mathrm{con}}=|W_{\mathrm{con}}|<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-803d-85be-fedff01e8a2d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{\mathrm{con}}=\{(c_i,c_j): i&lt;j \wedge Con(c_i,c_j)=1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8081-bd4c-c3a767da1bec" class="">Witness set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8002-81c5-c3a572190a6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F0001</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8095-be70-ef01cc16c99a" class="">Failure code</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a24c-c6d878bc5909" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No contradictions among overlapping subject/time/context under grammar.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ff-af46-d28985baed70" class="">Invariant class</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8065-84e4-d62c2e8d9de6" class="">3.1 Contradictions</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8023-bae4-f3d63af63ec4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-ad01-e5949a33ebee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-increase side condition: earlier components do not increase (needed for lex descent)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8038-869a-fb09d37acc80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strict decrease statement: which component of decreases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-8e3e-faec16b399fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Write-set (fields that may change)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9ee6-ec381f865121" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic rewrite (unique choice under fixed ordering)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8f0a-df3765f0f782" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Witness set definition (finite, decidable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8d68-db1feee504fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Failure code class (what fails)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bda0-c927ecdb1526" class="">Each item below is a <strong>proof obligation bundle</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-a0dc-ca8d1ef14c37" class="">3) Invariant coverage mapping (complete)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8010-8f61-e042012dd6b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-809d-b52a-d213e99484eb" class="">Order: lexicographic. Well-founded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a853-d7fe05a027ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(\Sigma)=\big(
n_{\mathrm{con}},
n_{\mathrm{cyc}},
n_{\mathrm{mb}},
n_{\mathrm{own}},
n_{\mathrm{bud}},
n_{\mathrm{met}},
n_{\mathrm{mece}}
\big)\in\mathbb{N}^7</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b5a4-ce2d3cb03499" class="">Global measure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a6-88a6-f1c2f10e9078" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: MECE defect count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807e-aab0-dfd5a05d0022" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: metric defect count (incomplete metrics + illegal transforms)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9f07-e923674b3882" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: budget violation indicator (0/1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-800f-b8de-d664300876b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: Unknown ownership claim count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8087-a5ff-ce70649edf05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: MB-not-allowed claim count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b0c9-dbaa02387b35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: illegal SCC count (Path A) OR edge-level violation count (Path B)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8096-bb40-cd22c07fd2e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: contradiction pair count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80fd-96c8-f9e151b60309" class="">Define witness-count components:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-801d-8df9-fa15626604e8" class="">2) Global defect measure (single well-founded ranking)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-802d-911d-c5d69ac9225e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8014-b224-f4fef4b2e243" class="">Everything is finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8024-9d1c-ee2717598632" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: log (append-only)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8032-9877-c14f91afd731" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: MECE objects list (each has universe and bins )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8046-be89-e1ddd237c21e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>etype</code>: interpretation support type (MB or not)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8057-926f-e33bfac20355" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: interpretation representation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-808a-9097-c261d99d1232" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: epistemic budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804a-a73b-da653ce4dfb1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: metric registry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8051-ae32-f81047855937" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: allowance set (ClaimId)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80a7-aa0b-d48d79b84197" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: claim graph</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30dc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b4c7-ef94a8bbaf6c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma=(g,V,A,\Lambda,M,b,\hat{E},etype,\mathcal{Y},L)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-807f-9918-dbb9240cac11" class="">1.1 State (full proof scope)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8000-ba7f-c5268bb17b71" class="">1) Global objects</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8098-9c97-c0eee80cb352"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30dc5e6f-95bd-8033-a5b9-cf28a1d7d981" class="">This is the complete “coverage proof” layer: it shows (a) no invariant failure is unmapped, (b) each mapped rewrite is deterministic, (c) each rewrite is write-set sealed, and (d) each rewrite strictly decreases a well-founded global measure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30dc5e6f-95bd-804e-a88e-d138123e5ebd" class="">XXXIX. More: consolidated proof index — every invariant class ↔ witness set ↔ rewrite ↔ write-set ↔ strict decrease ↔ termination component</h1></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
